## 1790016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後悔するぞ。<line-break>俺はもう腹を決めているのだ。<end>"
EN: "You will regret this. I have already made my decision.<end>"
KO: "후회할 거다. 나는 이미 각오를 굳혔다.<end>"

## 1790017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう用はないようだな。<line-break>疲れた。下がらせてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "It seems I am no longer needed here. I am tired. I shall retire.<end>"
KO: "이제 볼일은 없는 모양이군. 피곤하다. 물러가겠다.<end>"

## 1790018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どういうつもりだ、ゼネテス。<line-break>手負いの獣を野に放つのか？<line-break>危険すぎるぞ。<end>"
EN: "What are you playing at, Xenetes? Would you turn a wounded beast loose in the wild? It is far too dangerous.<end>"
KO: "…무슨 생각이냐, 제네테스. 상처 입은 짐승을 들판에 풀어놓겠다고? 너무 위험해.<end>"

## 1790019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴、ディンガル帝国の手が<line-break>そこまで伸びてる。だから、反対派の<line-break>巨頭を消そうと目論んだんだろうが…<line-break>少なくとも<line-break>種族間の不理解をネタに使うのは、<line-break>俺は気に入らないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Aunt, the Dyneskal Empire's reach extends even this far. I suppose that's why you plotted to eliminate a leader of the opposition... but I, for one, don't like you exploiting the lack of understanding between races to do it.<end>"
KO: "아주머니, 딘갈 제국의 손이 거기까지 뻗어 있어. 그래서 반대파의 거물을 없애려 한 거겠지만…. 적어도 종족 간의 몰이해를 이용하는 건 나는 마음에 안 들어.<end>"

## 1790020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以後は、総司令の不興を買わぬよう<line-break>肝に銘じよう。<line-break>…だが、甘いな。<line-break>これで、私とそなたは<line-break>一歩、死に近づいたぞ。<end>"
EN: "I shall remember not to incur the Commander-in-Chief's displeasure again. But you are naive. This has brought both of us one step closer to death.<end>"
KO: "앞으로는 총사령관의 심기를 거스르지 않도록 명심하지. …하지만 무르군. 이걸로 나와 자네는 죽음에 한 걸음 가까워졌어.<end>"

## 1790021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰だって、死ぬのは決まってるさ。<line-break>俺は叔母貴と心中できるなら<line-break>本望だぜ。<end>"
EN: "Everyone dies sooner or later. If I get to die with you, Aunt, I couldn't ask for more.<end>"
KO: "누구든 죽는 건 정해져 있어. 나는 숙모와 함께 죽을 수 있다면 소원이 없지.<end>"

## 1790022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec179-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、私はごめんこうむる。<line-break>できるだけの手を打つだけだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. No, thank you. I shall simply do all I can.<end>"
KO: "후후, 나는 사양하지. 할 수 있는 수를 다할 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1800001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1800002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、大丈夫ですよ。<line-break>ディンガル軍が攻めてきても。<line-break>　<line-break>もうすぐ、すべて<line-break>終わってしまうのですから。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Don't worry. Even if the Dyneskal Army attacks, it will all be over soon.<end>"
KO: "후후, 괜찮습니다. 딘갈군이 쳐들어와도요. 곧 모든 것이 끝나 버릴 테니까요.<end>"

## 1800003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の心配はいりません。<line-break>それより、ご自分のことを<line-break>心配なさった方がいいですよ。<line-break>ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "You needn't worry about me. You'd be better off worrying about yourself. Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "제 걱정은 하지 마세요. 그보다 본인 걱정을 하시는 편이 좋겠네요. 후후후.<end>"

## 1800004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1800005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にできることは<line-break>竜王様や愛の神ライラネート様に<line-break>祈ること、ただそれだけです。<end>"
EN: "All I can do is pray to the Dragon King and Lailanate, the deity of love. Nothing more.<end>"
KO: "제가 할 수 있는 건 용왕님과 사랑의 신 라이라네트님께 기도하는 것, 그뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1800006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、こっけいですね。　　　　　　<line-break>ディンガルのカルラ進軍の報に<line-break>王宮はおおわらわです。<line-break>この前のディンガルとの戦では<line-break>勝利したとはいえ、兵は<line-break>壊滅的な打撃を受けていますから…。<end>"
EN: "Heh. How absurd. News that Dyneskal has launched Karula's Advance has thrown the royal palace into an uproar. We may have won our last battle with Dyneskal, but our forces suffered devastating losses...<end>"
KO: "후후, 우습군요. 딘갈의 카를라가 진군한다는 소식에 왕궁은 대혼란입니다. 지난 딘갈과의 전쟁에서 승리하긴 했지만 병력은 궤멸적인 타격을 받았으니까요….<end>"

## 1800007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もともと、<line-break>ディンガルの大軍の攻撃には<line-break>耐えられなかったでしょうね…。<end>"
EN: "We could never have withstood an attack by Dyneskal's vast army in the first place...<end>"
KO: "원래부터 딩갈의 대군 공격을 버티긴 어려웠겠지요….<end>"

## 1800008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…レムオン様が貴族をまとめ　　　　<line-break>今回の戦には出兵しないと<line-break>言ってきたのです。<line-break>お母様に対しての稚拙な脅しです。<line-break>国内で争っている場合ではないのに<line-break>レムオン様には見えていないのです。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon has rallied the nobles and sent word that no troops will be committed to this battle. It is a childish attempt to threaten Mother. This is no time for strife within the kingdom, but Lemghon cannot see that.<end>"
KO: "…레무온 님이 귀족들을 규합해 이번 전쟁에는 출병하지 않겠다고 통보해 왔습니다. 어머님을 향한 치졸한 협박입니다. 국내에서 싸울 때가 아닌데도 레무온 님은 그걸 보지 못하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1800009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、ゼネテス様に対する<line-break>対抗意識だけ…。<end>"
EN: "All he sees is his rivalry with Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "다만 제네테스 님에 대한 경쟁심뿐….<end>"

## 1800010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にできることは、<line-break>ただ祈ること、<line-break>それだけです。<end>"
EN: "All I can do is pray. That is all.<end>"
KO: "제가 할 수 있는 건 그저 기도하는 것, 그것뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1800011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にできることは、<line-break>ただ祈ること、<line-break>それだけです。<end>"
EN: "All I can do is pray. That is all.<end>"
KO: "제가 할 수 있는 건 그저 기도하는 것, 그것뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1800012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様は…愚かなあの方は…、<line-break>ファーロス家の兵だけで<line-break>カルラと戦うおつもりです…。<line-break>ディンガルの青い死神、カルラの<line-break>兵力の１０分の１にも足りません。<line-break>死ににいくようなものです！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... that fool means to fight Karula with only the soldiers of House Pharos. He has less than a tenth of the forces commanded by Karula, the Blue Reaper of Dyneskal. He is marching to his death!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님은… 그 어리석은 분은… 파로스 가문의 병사만으로 카를라와 싸우실 생각입니다…. 딘갈의 푸른 사신 카를라 병력의 10분의 1도 안 됩니다. 죽으러 가는 거나 다름없어요!<end>"

## 1800013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス様は、あなたがここに来たら<line-break>この戦いに一緒に出てほしいので<line-break>謁見の間に来るようにと…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes said that if you came, he wanted you to join him in this battle. He asked you to come to the audience chamber...<end>"
KO: "…제네테스 님께서 당신이 여기 오면 이번 싸움에 함께 나서 줬으면 하니 알현실로 오라고 하셨습니다….<end>"

## 1800014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かないで…。<line-break>あの人だけでなく…あなたまで…。<line-break>行ってしまわないでください…。<end>"
EN: "Don't go... Please, not both of you... Don't you leave too...<end>"
KO: "가지 말아요…. 그 사람뿐 아니라… 당신까지…. 떠나지 말아 주세요….<end>"

## 1800015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い…です…。<line-break>あなただけでも、ティアナのそばに<line-break>とどまってください…。<end>"
EN: "Please... At least you... stay here with Tiana...<end>"
KO: "부탁…드립니다…. 당신만이라도 티아나 곁에 남아 주세요….<end>"

## 1800016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く<end>"
EN: "Go<end>"
KO: "간다<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Don't go<end>"
KO: "안 간다<end>"

## 1800020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来てくれたのか、<value:$28>。<line-break>状況は知ってると思うが、<line-break>…最悪だ。<line-break>つっても、まだ事態は悪くなりえるから<line-break>最悪じゃねえか。<line-break>とすると、少しは気がラクだな。<end>"
EN: "You came, <value:$28>. I reckon you know the situation. It's... the worst. Then again, it can still get worse, so I guess it isn't the worst yet. That's a bit of a relief.<end>"
KO: "와 줬구나, <value:$28>. 상황은 알고 있겠지만… 최악이야. 그래도 아직 더 나빠질 수 있으니 지금이 최악은 아닌 건가. 그렇게 생각하면 조금 마음이 편하네.<end>"

## 1800021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…別に死にに行く気はないさ。　　　　<line-break>俺なりに少ないながら勝算はある。<line-break>　<line-break>ツェラシェルを使って、<line-break>ネメアがらみの有力な情報もつかんだ。<line-break>そして勝つためにお前を呼んだんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not planning to go off and die. The odds may be slim, but I do have a chance. I used Zeraschel to get hold of some valuable information about Nemea, and I called you here because I mean to win.<end>"
KO: "…딱히 죽으러 갈 생각은 없어. 나름 적지만 승산은 있다. 체라셸을 써서 네메아와 관련된 유력한 정보도 잡았지. 그리고 이기기 위해 널 부른 거야.<end>"

## 1800022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "単刀直入に言う。<line-break>俺と一緒に奴と戦ってほしい。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…有利な側から不利な負け側に来いと<line-break>言ってるんだ、無理強いはしない。<line-break>どうだ？　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll come straight to the point. I want you to fight him with me. I'm asking you to leave the winning side for the losing one, so I won't force you. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "단도직입적으로 말하지. 나와 함께 그 녀석과 싸워 줘. 유리한 쪽에서 불리한 쪽으로 오라는 말이니 강요는 안 해. 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1800023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスと一緒に戦う<end>"
EN: "Fight alongside Xenetes<end>"
KO: "제네테스와 함께 싸운다<end>"

## 1800024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが、一緒に戦えない<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but I can't fight alongside you<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 함께 싸울 수 없어.<end>"

## 1800025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか、分の悪いバクチだしな。<line-break>無理にとは言えない。<line-break>…じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "…All right. It's a bad bet, anyway. I can't force you. …See you.<end>"
KO: "…그래, 승산 낮은 도박이니까. 억지로 부탁할 순 없지. …그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 1800026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、戦場で会ったら、<line-break>適当に手加減してくれや。<end>"
EN: "Well, if we meet on the battlefield, go easy on me, will you?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 전장에서 만나면 적당히 봐줘.<end>"

## 1800027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんのアニキのレムオンは<line-break>今回の出兵に関して、貴族をまとめて<line-break>ボイコットをやってる。<line-break>お前さんも立場的には一緒に<line-break>来づらいだろうが、頼む<line-break>俺と来てくれ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Your brother Lemghon has rallied the nobles to boycott this campaign. I know your position makes it hard for you to come with me, but I'm asking you anyway. Come with me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네 형 레무온은 이번 출병을 두고 귀족들을 모아 보이콧 중이야. 너도 입장상 같이 오기 어렵겠지만 부탁한다. 나와 함께 가 줘, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1800028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…あばよ、<value:$28>。<end>…あばよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>いい女に、なれよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…So long, <value:$28>.<end>…So long, <value:$28>. Grow into a fine woman.<end>"

## 1800029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここに来てくれたってことは<line-break>ついてきてくれるんだな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "You came here, so I take it you're coming with me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "여기까지 와 줬다는 건 따라와 주겠다는 거지, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1800030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>んじゃ、行くか。<end>"
EN: "Thanks, <value:$28>. All right, let's go.<end>"
KO: "고맙다, <value:$28>. 그럼 갈까.<end>"

## 1800031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴…、一応、聞いておく。<line-break>あんたひとりなら生き延びる手は<line-break>あるんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Auntie... just let me ask you this. If it were only you, you'd have some way to survive this, wouldn't you?<end>"
KO: "숙모… 일단 물어볼게. 숙모 혼자라면 살아남을 방법이 있는 거지?<end>"

## 1800032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私には守るべき夫と娘がいる。<line-break>そなたこそ、そなたひとりなら<line-break>生き延びる手はあろう？<end>"
EN: "I have a husband and daughter to protect. And you? If you had only yourself to consider, surely you too could find a way to survive?<end>"
KO: "…내게는 지켜야 할 남편과 딸이 있다. 오히려 너야말로 혼자라면 살아남을 방법이 있지 않겠느냐?<end>"

## 1800033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺にも、守るべきいろんなものが<line-break>あるんでな。<line-break>じゃ、叔母貴、天国で会おうや。<end>"
EN: "I've got all sorts of things to protect too. So, Auntie, I'll see you in heaven.<end>"
KO: "나도 지켜야 할 게 여러 가지 있어서 말이야. 그럼 이모, 천국에서 만나자.<end>"

## 1800034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ずうずうしいものだな。<line-break>地獄こそが、私にもそなたにも<line-break>ふさわしかろう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. How presumptuous. Hell would be more fitting for us both.<end>"
KO: "후후, 뻔뻔하군. 지옥이야말로 나와 너에게 어울리겠지.<end>"

## 1800035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愛する誰かのために<line-break>一生懸命な人間は<line-break>天国に行くんだよ、叔母貴。<end>"
EN: "People who give their all for someone they love go to heaven, Auntie.<end>"
KO: "사랑하는 누군가를 위해 열심히 사는 사람은 천국에 가는 거야, 이모.<end>"

## 1800036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本来、一国の王女の地位にある者が<line-break>こんなことを言ってはならないのです。<line-break>…でも、ありがとうございます。<line-break>…こんな時だからこそ、<line-break>大切な誰かを失いたくないのです。<line-break>大切な誰かにここにいてほしいのです。<end>"
EN: "As a princess, I should never say such things. But... thank you. At a time like this, I don't want to lose someone dear to me. I want that person to stay here.<end>"
KO: "본래 한 나라의 왕녀인 사람이 이런 말을 해선 안 되는 거겠지요. …그래도 고맙습니다. …이런 때이기에 더욱 소중한 누군가를 잃고 싶지 않습니다. 소중한 누군가가 여기 있어 주었으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1800037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは<value:$28>様、<line-break>お帰りなさいませ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome back.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 돌아오셨군요.<end>"

## 1800039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうか、<value:$28>様、<line-break>王宮へ行き、ファーロス総司令を　　　<line-break>お助けになってください。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>, go to the royal palace and help Commander-in-Chief Pharos.<end>"
KO: "부디 <value:$28>님, 왕궁으로 가서 파로스 총사령관을 도와주세요.<end>"

## 1800040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王様のご加護が<value:$28>様を<line-break>お守りになりますように。　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "May the Dragon King's blessing watch over you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "용왕님의 가호가 <value:$28>님을 지켜 주시기를.<end>"

## 1800041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。　　<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<line-break>しかし、伯爵様、よろしいのですか？<line-break>お兄君のエリエナイ公は…<line-break>い、いえ、出過ぎだ発言でした。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see the Queen? Very well. Please proceed. But, my lord, are you sure? Your elder brother, Duke Elienai... N-no, that was out of line.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 들어가십시오. 하지만 백작님, 괜찮으십니까? 형님이신 에리에나이 공은…. 아, 아닙니다. 제가 주제넘은 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 1800042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いになるのですね。<line-break>かしこまりました。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see the Queen? Very well. Please proceed.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가시는군요. 알겠습니다. 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1800043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様に会うのか。<line-break>よかろう、話は承っている。<line-break>通るとよい。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see the Queen? Very well. I have been informed. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나러 가는가. 좋다, 이야기는 들었다. 지나가라.<end>"

## 1800044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た、フリントの息子<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>今日は何用か？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>, son of Flint. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, 플린트의 아들 <value:$28>이여. 오늘은 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 1800045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダム解放軍への援助要請か。<line-break>あいにくと事態はさほど悠長ではない。<line-break>あまり興味はないな。<end>"
EN: "You seek aid for the Liberdam Liberation Army? Unfortunately, the situation does not permit such leisure. It does not interest me.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 해방군에 대한 지원 요청인가. 유감이지만 상황은 그렇게 한가하지 않아. 별로 관심 없군.<end>"

## 1800046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それよりも、ものは相談じゃが、<line-break>そなた、次の戦に参戦してはくれぬか？<line-break>カルラ進軍のこと、聞いていよう？<line-break>そなたが、こちらの言うことを<line-break>聞いてくれるなら、こちらも<line-break>援助の件、引き受けよう。いかがか？<end>"
EN: "More importantly, I have a proposal. Would you take part in the next battle? You have heard of Karula's advance, I trust? If you do as I ask, I will agree to the matter of aid as well. What say you?<end>"
KO: "그보다 의논할 게 있는데, 자네 다음 전투에 참전해 주지 않겠나? 카를라가 진군한다는 건 들었겠지? 자네가 우리 부탁을 들어준다면 우리도 지원 건을 맡아 주지. 어떤가?<end>"

## 1800047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1800048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1800049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、引き受けてくれるか。　<line-break>ありがたい。<line-break>今回の戦、かなり分が悪くてな。<line-break>その上、レムオン坊やが、<line-break>貴族をまとめて、今回の戦には<line-break>参加せぬと申しておる。<end>"
EN: "You will accept? I am grateful. The odds are quite poor in this battle. On top of that, young Lemghon has rallied the nobles and declared they will not take part.<end>"
KO: "그래, 맡아 주겠나. 고맙군. 이번 전투는 꽤 불리해서 말이야. 게다가 레무온 도련님이 귀족들을 모아 이번 전쟁엔 참가하지 않겠다고 하고 있어.<end>"

## 1800050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆえに、ファーロス家の軍だけで<line-break>立ち向かう。しかし、将星も乏しい。<line-break>そなたのような人材を欲していた。<line-break>頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>我が甥、ゼネテスの力になってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thus House Pharos's forces must stand alone. Yet we lack capable commanders. I had hoped for someone of your talent. I am counting on you, <value:$28>. Lend your strength to my nephew, Xenetes.<end>"
KO: "그러므로 파로스 가문의 군대만으로 맞선다. 하지만 장수도 부족하다. 너 같은 인재가 필요했다. 부탁한다, <value:$28>. 내 조카 제네테스에게 힘이 되어 다오.<end>"

## 1800051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、残念だな。<line-break>それならば、こちらも援助できぬ。<line-break>下がるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. What a shame. Then I cannot offer aid either. You may leave.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 유감이군. 그렇다면 이쪽도 지원할 수 없다. 물러가라.<end>"

## 1800052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様には<line-break>お忙しく、どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<line-break>は？　ファーロス総司令閣下の書状…。<line-break>あ！　そういえば…。失礼しました！<line-break>それではご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, but the Queen is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave... What? A letter from Commander-in-Chief Pharos...? Ah! Now that you mention it... My apologies. I will show you in.<end>"
KO: "죄송하지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구도 만나실 수 없습니다. 돌아가 주십시오…. 네? 파로스 총사령관 각하의 서신…. 아! 그러고 보니…. 실례했습니다! 안내해 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1800053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>らちもない。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<line-break>なに、総司令閣下の書状が…。<line-break>あ、それなら、承っております。<line-break>どうぞ、ご案内いたします。<end>"
EN: "You wish to see the Queen? Nonsense. Her Majesty is busy. Off with you! What? A letter from the Commander-in-Chief...? Ah, in that case, I have been informed. Please, let me show you in.<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 만나고 싶다고? 말도 안 된다. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가! 뭐, 총사령관 각하의 서신이…. 아, 그거라면 전달받았습니다. 이쪽으로 안내하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1800054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がエリスだ。<line-break>なんの用か？<end>"
EN: "I am Eris. What do you want?<end>"
KO: "내가 에리스다. 무슨 일이지?<end>"

## 1800055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様には<line-break>お忙しく、どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, but the Queen is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave...<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 1800056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様にお会いしたいだと？<line-break>らちもない。王妃様はお忙しいのだ。<line-break>さあ、帰った帰った！<end>"
EN: "You wish to see the Queen? Nonsense. Her Majesty is busy. Off with you!<end>"
KO: "왕비님을 뵙고 싶다고? 가당찮군. 왕비님은 바쁘시다. 자, 돌아가 돌아가!<end>"

## 1800057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ファーロス総司令が貴族を召集しています。<line-break>どうぞ、謁見の間へ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Commander-in-Chief Pharos is summoning the nobles. Please go to the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 파로스 총사령관께서 귀족들을 소집하고 계십니다. 알현실로 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1800058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "謁見の間へ行く<end>"
EN: "Go to the audience chamber<end>"
KO: "알현실로 간다<end>"

## 1800059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Do not go to the audience chamber<end>"

## 1800060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、どうぞ<line-break>お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Please proceed.<end>"
KO: "그럼 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1800061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、あの神速の戦術家カルラが<line-break>勇猛なディンガル兵をわんさか連れて<line-break>こっちに向かってるそうだ。<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんとの戦いで<line-break>こっちは兵力をあらかた失ってる上に<line-break>レムオンのダンナがな…<end>"
EN: "I hear Karula, that lightning-fast tactician, is heading this way with a great many brave Dyneskal soldiers. After our battle with old man Angeerdan, we have hardly any troops left, and then Lord Lemghon...<end>"
KO: "이봐, 그 신속의 전략가 카를라가 용맹한 딘갈 병사들을 잔뜩 끌고 이쪽으로 온다는군. 앙길단 영감과의 싸움으로 우린 병력을 거의 다 잃은 데다 레무온 나리까지….<end>"

## 1800062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族を集めて、兵を出さねえってさ。<line-break>んで、俺はファーロス家の兵だけで<line-break>カルラを迎え撃つことにした。<line-break>状況は最悪だ…つっても、<line-break>まだ事態は悪くなりえるから<line-break>最悪じゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "Rallied the nobles and says they will not send troops. So I have decided to meet Karula with House Pharos's soldiers alone. The situation is the worst... Though it can still get worse, so perhaps it isn't the worst.<end>"
KO: "귀족들을 모아 놨더니 병력을 안 내겠대. 그래서 난 파로스 가문의 병력만으로 카를라를 맞아 싸우기로 했어. 상황은 최악…이라고 하고 싶지만 더 나빠질 수도 있으니 아직 최악은 아니군.<end>"

## 1800063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1800064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにごとだ。<line-break>今は、王宮は大変なのだ。<line-break>用なら、あとにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? The palace is in turmoil now. If you have business, come back later.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이냐. 지금 왕궁은 매우 바쁘다. 볼일이라면 나중에 와라.<end>"

## 1800071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様の所に<line-break>いらっしゃるのですね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"
EN: "You are going to see Lady Tiana. Please proceed.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님께 가시는군요. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 1800072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1800073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼ですが、王妃様には<line-break>お忙しく、どなたともお会いになれません。<line-break>どうかお引き取りを…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me, but the Queen is busy and cannot receive anyone. Please leave...<end>"
KO: "실례지만 왕비님께서는 바쁘셔서 누구도 만나실 수 없습니다. 부디 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 1800074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの魔の手が迫ってきて…<line-break>いったい、これから<line-break>どうなるのでしょう、伯爵様。<end>"
EN: "Dyneskal's evil hand is closing in... What will become of us, my lord?<end>"
KO: "딩갈의 마수가 다가오고 있습니다…. 대체 앞으로 어떻게 되는 걸까요, 백작님.<end>"

## 1800075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、王宮は大騒ぎなのだ。<line-break>さあ、行った行った！<end>"
EN: "The palace is in an uproar right now. Move along!<end>"
KO: "지금 왕궁은 큰 소동이다. 자, 어서 가!<end>"

## 1800076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、<value:$28>、<line-break>困りごとかい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Need a hand with something?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 무슨 고민이라도 있어?<end>"

## 1800077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラがロストール進攻を発表してから<line-break>城の中は煮えくりかえってるからな。<line-break>ま、相手にしてもらえないだろうな。<line-break>俺が一筆書いておいてやるよ。<line-break>これで、叔母貴にも会えるだろ。<line-break>一応な。<end>"
EN: "Ever since Karula announced the campaign against Rostorl, the whole castle's been in an uproar. I doubt anyone will give you the time of day. I'll write you a note. That ought to get you in to see my aunt, at least. Should, anyway.<end>"
KO: "카를라가 로스톨 침공을 발표한 뒤로 성 안은 완전히 뒤집혔어. 뭐, 그냥 가서는 상대도 안 해 주겠지. 내가 한 줄 써 줄게. 이걸로 숙모도 만나게 해 줄 거야. 일단은.<end>"

## 1800078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いいってことよ。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Don't mention it. See you around.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 1800079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ここに来れば会えると思ってたぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I figured I'd find you here.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 여기 오면 만날 수 있을 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 1800080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルのカルラが攻めてくる。<line-break>青い死神、神速の戦術家がな。<line-break>俺と一緒に彼女を迎え撃ってほしい。<end>"
EN: "Karula of Dyneskal is coming to attack―the Blue Reaper, a tactician swift as lightning. I want you to help me meet her in battle.<end>"
KO: "딘갈의 카를라가 쳐들어온다. 푸른 사신, 신속의 전술가가 말이지. 나와 함께 그녀를 맞아 싸워 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1800081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ざっと状況を説明すると　　　　　　　<line-break>前にアンギルダンのとっつぁんに<line-break>さんざんやられて兵がない上に、<line-break>レムオンが貴族をまとめて出兵を<line-break>集団ボイコットしてきた。だから、<line-break>ファーロス家の軍だけで出撃する。<end>"
EN: "Here's the situation. Old Angeerdan mauled us so badly last time that we've scarcely any troops left, and Lemghon has rallied the nobles into a collective boycott of the campaign. So House Pharos will be marching alone.<end>"
KO: "상황을 간단히 말하면, 전에 앙길단 아저씨한테 호되게 당해서 병력도 없는데 레무온이 귀족들을 규합해 출병을 집단 보이콧했어. 그래서 파로스 가문의 군대만으로 출격한다.<end>"

## 1800082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵の兵力は１０倍。状況は最悪だな。<line-break>ま、まだ事態は悪くなりえるから、<line-break>最悪ってわけでもないか。<end>"
EN: "They outnumber us ten to one. Things couldn't be worse. Well, they could still get worse, so I suppose that's not quite true.<end>"
KO: "적 병력은 열 배. 상황은 최악이군. 뭐, 아직 더 나빠질 수도 있으니 진짜 최악은 아닌가.<end>"

## 1800083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…別に死にに行く気はないさ。　　　　<line-break>俺なりに少ないながら勝算はある。<line-break>　<line-break>ツェラシェルを使って、<line-break>ネメアがらみの有力な情報もつかんだ。<end>"
EN: "It's not as if I'm going there to die. I do have a chance of winning, however slim. I also used Zeraschel to obtain valuable information concerning Nemea.<end>"
KO: "…딱히 죽으러 갈 생각은 없어. 나름대로 적지만 승산은 있다. 체라셸을 이용해서 네메아와 관련된 유력한 정보도 잡았고.<end>"

## 1800084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前がカルラの配下なのは知ってる。<line-break>だが、この際、奴の手下をやめて<line-break>俺と一緒に奴と戦ってほしいんだ。<line-break>…有利な側から不利な負け側に来いと<line-break>言ってるんだ、無理強いはしない。<line-break>どうだ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "I know you serve under Karula. But I'm asking you to quit being her lackey and fight her with me. I know that means leaving the side with the advantage for the losing side, so I won't pressure you. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "네가 카를라의 부하라는 건 알고 있어. 하지만 이번 기회에 그 녀석 밑에서 나와 나와 함께 싸워 줬으면 해. …유리한 쪽에서 불리한 패배자 쪽으로 오라는 말이니 강요하진 않겠다. 어때, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1800085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>俺と一緒に出撃してくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, come fight beside me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나와 함께 출격해 줘.<end>"

## 1800086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、行こうぜ。<line-break>青い死神のお相手をしに！<end>"
EN: "Then let's go. Time to take on the Blue Reaper!<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자. 푸른 사신을 상대하러!<end>"

## 1800087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　青竜将軍カルラ率いるディンガル軍と<line-break>　　　ゼネテス率いるロストール軍は<line-break>　　　竜骨の砂漠南の街道で対峙した。<line-break>　<line-break>　　レムオンら有力貴族の出兵拒否により<line-break>　　　　ロストール軍は、青竜軍の<line-break>　　　５分の１ほどの戦力だったが…、<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Army, led by Azure Dragon General Karula, faced the Rostorl Army under Xenetes on the road south of the Dragonbone Desert. Because Lemghon and other powerful nobles had refused to send troops, the Rostorl Army had only about one-fifth the strength of the Azure Dragon Army. But...<end>"
KO: "청룡 장군 카를라가 이끄는 딘갈군과 제네테스가 이끄는 로스톨군은 용골의 사막 남쪽 가도에서 대치했다. 레무온 등 유력 귀족들이 출병을 거부해 로스톨군의 전력은 청룡군의 5분의 1 정도였지만….<end>"

## 1800088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　足止めのために空の本陣を敷き、併せて<line-break>　ネメア死すという偽情報を流すことで青竜軍の<line-break>　動揺を誘い、そこに奇襲をかけるという機略で<line-break>　　　　辛くも緒戦の勝利をものにした。<end>"
EN: "An empty command camp was established to hold the enemy back, and a false report of Nemea's death was spread to unsettle the Azure Dragon Army. A surprise attack amid the resulting confusion narrowly secured victory in the opening battle.<end>"
KO: "발을 묶기 위해 빈 본진을 설치하고, 네메아가 죽었다는 거짓 정보를 흘려 청룡군의 동요를 유도한 뒤 기습을 가하는 계략으로 가까스로 첫 전투의 승리를 거머쥐었다.<end>"

## 1800089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　だが、青竜軍の兵力は、依然として<line-break>　ロストール軍を大きく上回っており、緒戦の<line-break>勝敗に関係なく、勝機はいまだカルラにあった。<line-break>　　　しかし、青竜軍は進軍をとめた。　　　<end>"
EN: "Even so, the Azure Dragon Army still vastly outnumbered the Rostorl Army. Regardless of the opening battle's outcome, the advantage still lay with Karula. Yet the Azure Dragon Army halted its advance.<end>"
KO: "하지만 청룡군의 병력은 여전히 로스톨군을 크게 웃돌았고, 첫 전투의 승패와 관계없이 승기는 아직 카를라에게 있었다. 그러나 청룡군은 진군을 멈췄다.<end>"

## 1800090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア死す。<line-break>ゼネテスの放った<line-break>偽情報を裏付けるかのごとく、<line-break>　<line-break>ゼグナ鉱山で<line-break>闇の神器を探索したあと、<line-break>青竜軍に合流するはずだった<line-break>ネメアが、ついに戻らなかったのである。<end>"
EN: "Nemea is dead. As if confirming the false report spread by Xenetes, Nemea, who was supposed to join the Azure Dragon Army after searching Zegna Mine for the Artifact of Darkness, never returned.<end>"
KO: "네메아 사망. 제네테스가 흘린 거짓 정보를 뒷받침하듯, 제그나 광산에서 어둠의 신기를 탐색한 뒤 청룡군에 합류할 예정이던 네메아는 끝내 돌아오지 않았다.<end>"

## 1800091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　一方、ロストールでは、<line-break>　レムオンが突然姿を消したことをきっかけに<line-break>　　　　国内の戦力が、再び統合され、<line-break>　青竜軍と戦えるだけの態勢が取り戻された。<line-break>　　こうして、ネメアを失ったディンガルと<line-break>　　　国内の統合に成功したロストールは、<line-break>　　　　再びにらみ合いの状態に入った。<end>"
EN: "Meanwhile, Lemghon's sudden disappearance in Rostorl led the nation's forces to unite once more, restoring its ability to fight the Azure Dragon Army. Thus Dyneskal, having lost Nemea, and Rostorl, having restored its internal unity, faced each other in a standoff once again.<end>"
KO: "한편 로스톨에서는 레무온이 갑자기 모습을 감춘 것을 계기로 국내 전력이 다시 통합되어 청룡군과 맞설 수 있는 태세를 되찾았다. 이렇게 네메아를 잃은 딩갈과 국내 통합에 성공한 로스톨은 다시 서로 대치하는 상태에 들어갔다.<end>"

## 1800092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァイ、ちょうどいいとこに来たわ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>いよいよ、ロストールを攻略するわよ。<end>"
EN: "Hi there. You came at just the right time, <value:$28>. We're finally going to take Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "하이, 마침 잘 왔어, <value:$28>. 이제 로스톨을 공략할 거야.<end>"

## 1800093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様が敵国深くのゼグナにある<line-break>闇の神器を取りに行くってっからさ。<line-break>放っとくわけにもいかないじゃん？<end>"
EN: "Lord Nemea says he's going deep into enemy territory to Zegna to retrieve the Dark Artifact. We can't very well leave him to it, can we?<end>"
KO: "네메아 님이 적국 깊숙한 제그나에 있는 어둠의 신기를 가지러 간다잖아. 내버려 둘 순 없지?<end>"

## 1800094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、このタイミングになったわけ。<line-break>しっかし、うちの皇帝陛下も<line-break>何考えてんだか、わかんないよね。<line-break>ま、今回の戦、<line-break>たぶん勝てるから<line-break>いいんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "That's why we're making our move now. Still, who knows what our Emperor is thinking? Not that it matters. We'll probably win this battle.<end>"
KO: "그래서 이 타이밍이 된 거야. 그나저나 우리 황제 폐하도 무슨 생각인지 모르겠다니까. 뭐, 이번 전쟁은 아마 이길 테니 상관없지만.<end>"

## 1800095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、アンギルダン将軍に勝った<line-break>ゼネテスが相手ですよ。<end>"
EN: "But we're facing Xenetes, the man who defeated General Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단 장군을 이긴 제네테스가 상대예요.<end>"

## 1800096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらゼネテスでも、<line-break>兵隊がいなくちゃ勝てないでしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしがつかんだ情報だと、<line-break>向こうさんは仲違いしてて、<line-break>ゼネテスは私兵しか使えないみたいよ。<end>"
EN: "Even Xenetes can't win without soldiers. According to my sources, they've had a falling-out, so it looks like he can only use his private troops.<end>"
KO: "아무리 제네테스라도 병사가 없으면 못 이기겠지. 내가 잡은 정보로는 저쪽이 사이가 틀어져서 제네테스는 사병밖에 못 쓴대.<end>"

## 1800097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほ、本当ですか！？　　　　<line-break>…なんて愚かな。<line-break>　<line-break>貴族というのは、<line-break>どうして自分のことしか…！<end>"
EN: "R-really?! How foolish... Why do nobles think only of themselves?!<end>"
KO: "저, 정말입니까!? …어리석군요. 귀족이라는 자들은 어째서 자기 생각밖에…!<end>"

## 1800098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とは言っても、<line-break>この情報を鵜呑みにすると<line-break>足下すくわれるかもね。<line-break>ゼネテスってのは<line-break>油断ならないから。<end>"
EN: "Still, if we swallow that information whole, we could get caught off guard. Xenetes is not someone to underestimate.<end>"
KO: "그렇다고 해도 이 정보를 그대로 믿으면 발밑을 잡힐지도 몰라. 제네테스라는 자는 방심할 수 없으니까.<end>"

## 1800099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、カルラ様。<end>"
EN: "Um... Lady Karula.<end>"
KO: "저기…, 카를라 님.<end>"

## 1800100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　なに、アイリーン？<end>"
EN: "Hm? What is it, Irene?<end>"
KO: "응? 왜, 아이린?<end>"

## 1800101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の戦い、私に指揮を<line-break>とらせてください。<end>"
EN: "Please let me command this battle.<end>"
KO: "이번 싸움은 제가 지휘하게 해 주세요.<end>"

## 1800102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あたしには、自分の祖国を<line-break>踏みにじらせたくないのかな？<end>"
EN: "You don't want me trampling your homeland, is that it?<end>"
KO: "…난 내 조국이 짓밟히는 걸 원하지 않는 걸까?<end>"

## 1800103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうことではありません。<line-break>貴族とは、<line-break>決着をつけておきたいのです。<end>"
EN: "That is not it. I want to settle things with the nobles.<end>"
KO: "그런 뜻은 아닙니다. 귀족들과는 결판을 내 두고 싶습니다.<end>"

## 1800105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと考えていました。<line-break>…なぜ騎士になりたかったのか。<end>"
EN: "I've been thinking about it for a long time... Why I wanted to become a knight.<end>"
KO: "계속 생각하고 있었습니다. …왜 기사가 되고 싶었던 건지.<end>"

## 1800106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は騎士になって戦いたかったんです、<line-break>私たち平民を苦しめる社会の仕組みと。<line-break>そして、平和な日常を守りたかった…。<line-break>本来なら、私たちの暮らしを<line-break>守ってくれるはずの貴族たちが、<line-break>私たちを苦しめるのが許せなかった。<end>"
EN: "I wanted to become a knight and fight the social system that oppresses us commoners. And I wanted to protect peaceful daily life... I could not forgive the nobles, who should have protected our lives, for making us suffer.<end>"
KO: "저는 기사가 되어 싸우고 싶었습니다. 우리 평민을 괴롭히는 사회 구조와요. 그리고 평온한 일상을 지키고 싶었죠…. 원래 우리 삶을 지켜야 할 귀족들이 오히려 우리를 괴롭히는 걸 용서할 수 없었습니다.<end>"

## 1800107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから私は騎士になって、<line-break>平民を苦しめる貴族たちと<line-break>戦いたかったんです。<line-break>自分のことだけを考えて、平民の<line-break>憂いを見ようとしない貴族たちに<line-break>思い知らせてやりたいんです！<end>"
EN: "That is why I wanted to become a knight and fight the nobles who oppress commoners. I want to make those nobles, who think only of themselves and refuse to see the worries of commoners, understand!<end>"
KO: "그래서 저는 기사가 되어 평민을 괴롭히는 귀족들과 싸우고 싶었어요. 자기 일만 생각하고 평민의 근심은 보려 하지 않는 귀족들에게 똑똑히 알려 주고 싶어요!<end>"

## 1800108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。いいわよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. You may.<end>"
KO: "알았어. 좋아.<end>"

## 1800109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Thank you!<end>"
KO: "감사합니다!<end>"

## 1800110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そういうことだから、<line-break><value:$28>、助けてあげてね。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's how it is, so <value:$28>, give her a hand, all right?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 거니까 <value:$28>, 도와줘.<end>"

## 1800111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。<line-break>んじゃ、出発よ。<end>"
EN: "Good. Then let's get moving.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 출발하자.<end>"

## 1800116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本国からの連絡ではないのね？<line-break>じゃあ、情報の出所はどこなの？<end>"
EN: "This did not come from the homeland? Then where did the information come from?<end>"
KO: "본국에서 온 연락은 아니지? 그럼 정보 출처는 어디야?<end>"

## 1800117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それが…、はっきりしないのです。<line-break>突然広まったものですから。<end>"
EN: "W-we aren't certain. It just started spreading all of a sudden.<end>"
KO: "그, 그것이… 확실하지 않습니다. 갑자기 퍼진 소문이라서요.<end>"

## 1800118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし思うんだけどさ…。　　<line-break>それってたぶん、偽情報よ。<line-break>目の前の敵はおとり。<line-break>こうやってあたしたちが<line-break>混乱してるところに<line-break>奇襲がくるってな感じかしら？<end>"
EN: "You know what I think? It's probably false information. The enemy in front of us is a decoy. While we're in disarray like this, they'll hit us with a surprise attack. That's my guess.<end>"
KO: "내 생각인데 말이야…. 그건 아마 가짜 정보야. 눈앞의 적은 미끼. 이렇게 우리가 혼란스러운 틈에 기습이 오는 식 아닐까?<end>"

## 1800119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "か、カ、カルラ様！<line-break>大変です！<end>"
EN: "L-L-Lady Karula! We have an emergency!<end>"
KO: "카, 카, 카를라 님! 큰일입니다!<end>"

## 1800120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そら来た。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>はいはい、落ち着いて。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストール軍が攻めてきたって<line-break>言うんでしょ。<line-break>それも、後ろから。<end>"
EN: "There it is. All right, all right, calm down. You're going to tell me the Rostorl Army has attacked, aren't you? From behind.<end>"
KO: "올 게 왔네. 자자, 진정해. 로스톨군이 공격해 왔다고 말하려는 거지. 그것도 뒤에서.<end>"

## 1800121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな！<line-break>私がもたついたばかりに…！<end>"
EN: "N-no! All because I was too slow...!<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가! 제가 꾸물거리는 바람에…!<end>"

## 1800122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんまり気にしないの。　　　　　　　<line-break>落ち着きなさいって。<line-break>　<line-break>これだけの人間の命を預かってんだし、<line-break>状況の把握につとめようとした<line-break>指揮に間違いはなかったよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry about it. Calm down. You have all these lives in your hands. There was nothing wrong with your decision to get a clear picture of the situation.<end>"
KO: "너무 신경 쓰지 마. 진정하라고. 이 많은 사람의 목숨을 맡고 있으니 상황 파악에 힘쓰려 했던 지휘가 잘못된 건 아니야.<end>"

## 1800123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし！<end>"
EN: "B-but!<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만!<end>"

## 1800124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスの方が一枚上手だったのよ。<line-break>あたしだって、こんなに早くくるとは、<line-break>思ってなかったわ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes was one step ahead of us. Even I didn't expect him to get here this quickly.<end>"
KO: "제네테스가 한 수 위였어. 나도 이렇게 빨리 올 줄은 몰랐거든.<end>"

## 1800125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、気持ちを切り替えなさい！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>兵の命を救いたかったら、<line-break>早めに引き上げるしかないわ。<line-break>しんがりにはあたしが出るわ。<end>"
EN: "Now, put it behind you! If you want to save the soldiers' lives, we have to withdraw while we still can. I'll take the rear guard.<end>"
KO: "자, 마음을 다잡아! 병사들의 목숨을 구하고 싶으면 일찍 철수할 수밖에 없어. 후미는 내가 맡을게.<end>"

## 1800127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は、兵の命が大事よ。<end>"
EN: "Right now, the soldiers' lives come first.<end>"
KO: "지금은 병사들의 목숨이 중요해.<end>"

## 1800128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ！　そうではなく…！<line-break>私の敗北で、カルラ様を危険な目に<line-break>あわせるわけには…！<end>"
EN: "No, that isn't what I mean! I can't let my defeat put you in danger, Lady Karula...<end>"
KO: "아니요! 그런 게 아니라…! 제 패배 때문에 카를라 님을 위험하게 만들 수는…!<end>"

## 1800129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンの敗北はあたしの敗北よ。<line-break>それに、次は勝つわ。<end>"
EN: "Your defeat is my defeat, Irene. Besides, we'll win next time.<end>"
KO: "아이린의 패배는 내 패배야. 그리고 다음엔 이길 거야.<end>"

## 1800130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、<line-break>あなたが退却の指揮を執って。<line-break>兵の命、頼んだわ。<end>"
EN: "Irene, take command of the retreat. I'm entrusting the soldiers' lives to you.<end>"
KO: "아이린, 당신이 퇴각을 지휘해. 병사들의 목숨을 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1800131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここで、迎え撃つわよ。<line-break>負けたら死ぬんだからね。<line-break>覚悟してかかってよ！<end>"
EN: "We'll make our stand here. If we lose, we die, so be ready to give it everything you've got!<end>"
KO: "여기서 맞아 싸울 거야. 지면 죽는 거니까 각오 단단히 해!<end>"

## 1800132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いたぞ！<line-break>カルラだ！！<end>"
EN: "There she is! It's Karula!!<end>"
KO: "찾았다! 카를라다!!<end>"

## 1800133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はん、来たわね！<line-break>さあ、相手してあげるよ！！<end>"
EN: "Hah, there you are! Come on, I'll take you on!!<end>"
KO: "흥, 왔구나! 자, 상대해 주지!!<end>"

## 1800134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "제네테스!<end>"

## 1800135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったね…。<line-break>カルラだけじゃないわけか。<end>"
EN: "Well, this is a problem... So it isn't just Karula.<end>"
KO: "곤란하군…. 카를라만이 아니었나.<end>"

## 1800136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんとこういう形で　　<line-break>戦うことになるとはな。<line-break>　<line-break>人生ってのは、おもしれぇ。<line-break>なあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>手加減はしない。行くぜ！<end>"
EN: "Never thought I'd end up fighting you like this. Life's a funny thing. Listen, <value:$28>. I won't hold back. Here I come!<end>"
KO: "너와 이런 식으로 싸우게 될 줄이야. 인생이란 참 재미있군. <value:$28>, 봐주지 않겠다. 간다!<end>"

## 1800137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここでお前を討ちとれば、<line-break>戦力差がどうあれ、<line-break>こっちの勝ちだ。<line-break>その首、もらうぜ！<end>"
EN: "If I bring you down here, we win, no matter how badly we're outnumbered. Your head is mine!<end>"
KO: "여기서 너를 쓰러뜨리면 전력 차이가 어떻든 이쪽 승리다. 그 목, 받아 가겠다!<end>"

## 1800138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>強くなりやがって…。<line-break>　<line-break>へへ…、複雑な気分だな。<line-break>悪い気はしねぇよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You've gotten strong... Heh. I don't quite know how to feel about that, but it isn't a bad feeling, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "큭…. 강해졌구나…. 헤헤… 복잡한 기분이네. 나쁜 기분은 아니야, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1800139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<line-break>カルラひとりならやれたものを！<end>"
EN: "Damn... If it had only been Karula, I could have won!<end>"
KO: "큭…. 카를라 혼자였다면 해치웠을 텐데!<end>"

## 1800141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまでね。<line-break>あたしたちも退くわよ。<end>"
EN: "This is as far as we go. We are withdrawing too.<end>"
KO: "여기까지야. 우리도 물러나자.<end>"

## 1800145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、ネメア様が　　　<line-break>戻ってこないなんてね。<line-break>　<line-break>死んだとは思えないけど、<line-break>何かあったのは確かね。<end>"
EN: "I never imagined Lord Nemea would not return. I do not believe he is dead, but something has certainly happened.<end>"
KO: "설마 네메아 님이 돌아오지 않을 줄이야. 죽었다고는 생각하지 않지만 뭔가 생긴 건 확실해.<end>"

## 1800146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し訳ありません。<line-break>私がもたついたばかりに…！<end>"
EN: "I am sorry. It was because I was too slow...!<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 제가 꾸물거리는 바람에…!<end>"

## 1800147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのせいじゃないわ。　　<line-break>気にしちゃダメ。<line-break>　<line-break>ネメア様の運命を<line-break>左右できるほどの力なんて、<line-break>あたしたちにはないんだから。<end>"
EN: "It is not your fault. Do not dwell on it. We do not have the power to decide Lord Nemea's fate.<end>"
KO: "당신 탓이 아니야. 신경 쓰지 마. 네메아 님의 운명을 좌우할 만큼의 힘은 우리에게 없으니까.<end>"

## 1800148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともかく、<value:$28>。<line-break>あんたのおかげで、<line-break>助かったわ。ありがと。　　<end>"
EN: "In any case, <value:$28>, you saved us. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "아무튼, <value:$28>. 네 덕분에 살았어. 고마워.<end>"

## 1800149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ロストール攻略は中止ね。　　<line-break>ネメア様の捜索が先決だわ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>も、<line-break>なんかわかったら教えてくれない？<line-break>あたしは、ここにいると思うから。<end>"
EN: "Well, the campaign to take Rostorl is cancelled. Finding Lord Nemea comes first. If you learn anything, <value:$28>, will you tell me? I expect I will be here.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 로스톨 공략은 중지야. 네메아 님 수색이 우선이야. <value:$28>도 뭔가 알게 되면 알려 주지 않을래? 난 여기 있을 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1800150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다려, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1800151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私ね…、退却しながら　　　　<line-break>ずっと思ってた。<line-break>　<line-break>生きて帰らせなきゃ。<line-break>みんな死なせちゃダメだって。<end>"
EN: "While we were retreating, I kept thinking... I have to get them home alive. I cannot let them die.<end>"
KO: "나 말이야… 후퇴하면서 계속 생각했어. 살아서 돌아가게 해야 해. 모두 죽게 두면 안 된다고.<end>"

## 1800152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、少しわかったの。<line-break>私は、やっぱり何かを守るために<line-break>戦っているんだってこと。<end>"
EN: "I understand a little now. I really am fighting to protect something.<end>"
KO: "나, 조금 알게 됐어. 역시 나는 무언가를 지키기 위해 싸우고 있다는 걸.<end>"

## 1800153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様のように、<line-break>勝つために戦っているんじゃ<line-break>ないんだってこと。その違い…。<end>"
EN: "I am not fighting to win, as Lady Karula does. That is the difference...<end>"
KO: "카를라 님처럼 이기기 위해 싸우는 게 아니라는 것. 그 차이…<end>"

## 1800154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけ…、<line-break>あなたに聞いておいてほしかったの。<end>"
EN: "That is all... I just wanted you to hear that.<end>"
KO: "그것만은… 당신이 들어 줬으면 했어.<end>"

## 1800155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あんたも来て。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you're coming with me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너도 와.<end>"

## 1800157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3もしや、今の人影は<line-break>ネメア様ではなかったか？<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4今の人影、ネメア様に似てたかも。<line-break>でも、関わるとろくなことに<line-break>ならない気がするし…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5おい、<value:$28>。<line-break>今の人影、ネメアだったよな。<line-break>追いかけるか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6今の人…<line-break>あれ…ネメアじゃないかな？<line-break>どうしよう、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13今のは、ネメア。<line-break>…追うのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、今の見たか？<line-break>あれ、絶対ネメアだぜ。<line-break>追うの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14今のはネメアではなかったか？<line-break>こんな所で供も連れずにいったい…？<line-break>どうするんじゃ、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>今の人、ネメアさんじゃないかな？<line-break>どうしようか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17なに、あれ、ネメアぁ？　<line-break>まさか、まさか、まさか、<line-break>あなた、ネメアを追うつもりなの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねぇねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>今の人、ネメアさんみたいだったよ。<line-break>どうするのぉ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…追うのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>11今のは…ネメア様…<line-break><value:$28>、追いかけるの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>21今のはネメアか？<line-break>こんな所で供も連れずにいったい…？<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23今のは…ネメア様…<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27今のは、ネメア…。<line-break>追うのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26もしや、今の人影は<line-break>ネメアではなかったか？<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>24今のは…ネメア様？<line-break><value:$28><line-break>追いかけますか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>25今の人影…<line-break>ネメア…だよね？<line-break>追いかけるの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>192あれはネメアですね。<line-break>どうしますか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193今のはネメアさん？<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>ど、どうしましょう？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194嫌な奴が視界に入ったな…。<line-break><value:$28>、どうする？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191今の、ネメアじゃないかしら？<line-break>追いかける？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190あれはネメアじゃないか？<line-break>追うか、<value:$28>？<end>見た見た？　今のネメアだよ。<line-break>でも、怒りんぼのオイフェとか<line-break>ぶあいそうなゼリグとか、いないなぁ。<line-break>ヒゲオヤジのドルドラムもいないよ。<line-break>何してるのか、気になるよね。<line-break>追いかけるの？ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Could that have been Lord Nemea? What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4That figure looked a little like Lord Nemea. But getting involved feels like asking for trouble...<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hey, <value:$28>. That was Nemea, wasn't it? Go after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6That person... Could that have been Nemea? What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13That was Nemea. Go after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, did you see that? That was definitely Nemea. Going after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Was that not Nemea? What could he be doing in a place like this without a single attendant...? What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>. Don't you think that was Nemea? What now?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wait, was that Nemea? No, no, no. You're not planning to go after him, are you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey, hey, <value:$28>. That person looked like Nemea. What now?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Go after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11That was... Lord Nemea... <value:$28>, are you going after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Was that Nemea? What could he be doing in a place like this without a single attendant...? What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23That was... Lord Nemea... What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27That was Nemea... Go after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Could that have been Nemea? What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Was that... Lord Nemea? <value:$28>, will you go after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25That figure... That was Nemea, wasn't it? Going after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192That was Nemea. What now?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Was that Nemea? W-what should we do, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Someone unpleasant just came into view... What now, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Wasn't that Nemea? Going after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Wasn't that Nemea? Pursue him, <value:$28>?<end>Did you see? That was Nemea. But hot-tempered Aoife and surly Zelig aren't with him. And that bearded old man Doldrum isn't here either. Aren't you curious what Nemea is doing? Going after him?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3혹시 방금 그림자는 네메아 님 아니었나? 어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4방금 그림자, 네메아 님과 닮았을지도. 그래도 관여하면 좋을 게 없을 것 같고….<end><if><value:$01><equal>5야, <value:$28>. 방금 그림자 네메아였지. 쫓을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6방금 사람… 네메아 아닐까? 어떡하지, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13방금 건 네메아다. …쫓을 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 방금 봤어? 저거 분명 네메아야. 쫓을 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14방금 건 네메아가 아니었나? 이런 곳에서 수행원도 없이 대체…? 어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>. 방금 사람 네메아 씨 아닐까? 어떻게 할까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야, 저거 네메아야? 설마 네메아를 쫓을 생각이야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19있잖아, <value:$28>. 방금 사람 네메아 씨 같았어. 어떡할 거야아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…쫓을 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11방금 건… 네메아 님… <value:$28>, 쫓아갈 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21방금 건 네메아인가? 이런 곳에서 수행원도 없이 대체…? 어쩔 거지, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23방금 건… 네메아 님… 어떻게 할 거지, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27방금 건 네메아…. 쫓을 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26혹시 방금 그림자는 네메아 아니었나? 어쩔 거냐, <value:$28>?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24방금 건… 네메아 님? <value:$28> 쫓아가시겠습니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25방금 그림자… 네메아… 맞지? 쫓아갈 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192저건 네메아군요. 어떻게 하시겠습니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193방금 건 네메아 씨? <value:$28> 씨, 어, 어떡하죠?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194싫은 녀석이 시야에 들어왔군…. <value:$28>, 어떻게 할래?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191방금 네메아 아니야? 쫓아갈래?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190저건 네메아 아닌가? 쫓을까, <value:$28>?<end>봤어? 방금 네메아야. 그런데 화 잘 내는 오이페도 무뚝뚝한 젤리그도 없네. 수염 아저씨 돌드람도 없어. 뭘 하는지 신경 쓰이네. 쫓아갈래?<end>"

## 1800158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアを追う<end>"
EN: "Follow Nemea<end>"
KO: "네메아를 쫓는다<end>"

## 1800159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアを追わない<end>"
EN: "Don't follow Nemea<end>"
KO: "네메아를 쫓지 않는다<end>"

## 1800160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しかし、ついてない。　　　　　　　<line-break>せっかく闇の神器、貪欲の盾の<line-break>ありかを突き止めたのに。<line-break>よりによって<line-break>獅子帝ネメアと鉢合わせとはな。<line-break>俺より強い奴なんて何人もいないのに。<end>"
EN: "What rotten luck. I finally tracked down the Dark Artifact known as the Shield of Greed, only to run straight into Nemea, the Lion Emperor. There aren't many people stronger than me.<end>"
KO: "…그래도 운이 없군. 모처럼 어둠의 신기, 탐욕의 방패가 있는 곳을 알아냈는데 하필 사자제 네메아와 마주치다니. 나보다 강한 놈은 몇 명 되지도 않는데.<end>"

## 1800161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "システィーナの伝道師のひとり、<line-break>黒の祈りのジュサプブロス<line-break>といったか？<line-break>少しでも時間を稼いで<line-break>生き残ろうと計算している。<line-break>いい根性をしているな。<end>"
EN: "Jusabros of the Black Prayer―one of Sistina's missionaries, was it? You're trying to buy whatever time you can and stay alive. You've got nerve.<end>"
KO: "시스티나의 전도사 중 하나, 검은 기도의 주사프브로스라고 했던가? 조금이라도 시간을 벌어 살아남으려 계산하고 있군. 배짱 한번 좋군.<end>"

## 1800162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お褒めにあずかり、光栄だね。<line-break>だが、獅子はネズミを殺すにも<line-break>全力を尽くす…わけだよな。<end>"
EN: "I'm honored by the compliment. But a lion uses all his strength even to kill a mouse... doesn't he?<end>"
KO: "칭찬해 주니 영광이군. 하지만 사자는 쥐를 잡을 때도 전력을 다한다… 그런 거지.<end>"

## 1800163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様らか？　破壊神を<line-break>復活させようとしている者は？<end>"
EN: "Are you the ones seeking to resurrect the God of Destruction?<end>"
KO: "네놈들인가? 파괴신을 부활시키려는 자들이?<end>"

## 1800164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちじゃないさ。<line-break>運命だよ。<line-break>わかってるんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Not us. Fate. You know that, don't you?<end>"
KO: "우리가 아니야. 운명이지. 알고 있잖아?<end>"

## 1800165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神は復活させぬ。<line-break>運命が復活させようとするなら<line-break>運命を叩き伏せるまでだ。<end>"
EN: "I will not allow the God of Destruction to be resurrected. If fate seeks to revive it, then I shall strike fate down.<end>"
KO: "파괴신은 부활시키지 않는다. 운명이 부활시키려 한다면 운명을 때려눕힐 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1800166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと言うから、<line-break>運命が敵に回るんだよ。<line-break>運命は俺を助けようってさ。<end>"
EN: "That's the sort of thing that makes fate turn against you. Fate says it wants to help me.<end>"
KO: "그런 말을 하니까 운명이 적으로 돌아서는 거야. 운명은 나를 도우려는 모양인데.<end>"

## 1800168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこから離れろ！<line-break><value:$28>！！<end>"
EN: "Get away from there, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "거기서 떨어져! <value:$28>!!<end>"

## 1800169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやいや、さすがだよネメア。<line-break>やはり助けたか…、くくく。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Impressive as ever, Nemea. So you did come to the rescue... Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "아니, 역시 네메아답군. 역시 구해냈나… 크크크.<end>"

## 1800170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアかい？<line-break>君の代わりに<line-break>次元のはざまに落ちたのさ。　　　　　<line-break>出口のない闇の世界にね。<line-break>次元を操れるってのは、すばらしいね。<line-break>あのネメアを倒せるなんてな。<end>"
EN: "Nemea? He fell into the Interdimensional Rift in your place, into a world of darkness with no way out. The ability to control dimensions really is marvelous. Imagine being able to defeat even Nemea.<end>"
KO: "네메아 말이야? 네 대신 차원의 틈새로 떨어졌어. 출구 없는 어둠의 세계로 말이지. 차원을 다룰 수 있다는 건 멋지네. 그 네메아를 쓰러뜨릴 수 있다니.<end>"

## 1800171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…、はっはっは！　　　　<line-break>実に愉快だよ。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>君のおかげだよ。<line-break>君を助けてネメアは死んだ。<line-break>俺の命の恩人だよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Ha ha ha! This is delightful. Thank you, <value:$28>. It's all thanks to you. Nemea died saving your life. That makes you my savior.<end>"
KO: "후후… 하하하! 정말 유쾌하군. 고맙다, <value:$28>. 네 덕분이야. 네가 도와줘서 네메아는 죽었어. 넌 내 생명의 은인이야.<end>"

## 1800172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけじゃない。<line-break>これでネメアは、より闇に近づいた。<line-break>復活がたやすくなったよ。<line-break>まったく、いいことづくめだね。<end>"
EN: "And that's not all. Nemea is closer to darkness now, which will make his resurrection that much easier. Everything has worked out beautifully.<end>"
KO: "그뿐이 아니야. 이걸로 네메아는 더욱 어둠에 가까워졌어. 부활도 쉬워졌지. 정말 좋은 일뿐이네.<end>"

## 1800173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、悪いが逃げさせてもらうよ。<line-break>君と戦える余力はない。<line-break>ネメアは本当に強くてね。<line-break>そのかわり、貪欲の盾を置いてゆこう。<line-break>君を命がけで守ったネメアの形見だよ。<line-break>大事にね。フフフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Oops. Sorry, but I'll be making my escape. I haven't enough strength left to fight you. Nemea really is strong. Instead, I'll leave you the Shield of Greed―a memento of Nemea, who gave his life to protect you. Take good care of it. Heh heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "이런, 미안하지만 도망가게 해 줘. 너와 싸울 여력은 없어. 네메아는 정말 강했거든. 대신 탐욕의 방패는 두고 가지. 목숨 걸고 널 지킨 네메아의 유품이야. 소중히 해. 후후후후….<end>"

## 1800174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3なんということじゃ…。<line-break>あのネメア様が…。<line-break>　<line-break>…あとで一度は猫屋敷に行って<line-break>報告した方がよさそうじゃな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4なんか大変なことしちゃったかも。<line-break>でも、あれは<line-break>あのヒゲメガネのせいよね。<line-break>私たちは、悪くないかも。<line-break>でも、オルファウスさんには<line-break>言っておいた方がいいかな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5うそだろ…、おい。<line-break>ネメアって、死んじまったのかよ！？<line-break>　<line-break>なあ、オルファウスに知らせた方が<line-break>いいんじゃないか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6信じられない。　　　　　　　<line-break>ネメアが…、死んだ…？<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、オルファウスさんに<line-break>知らせた方がいいんじゃない？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13あの男、<line-break>妙なことを言っていたな。<line-break>ネメアが闇に近づいたとか…。<line-break>一度、オルファウスに<line-break>話してみた方が、<line-break>よさそうだな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、これって、<line-break>なんかすげえやばいんじゃないの？　<line-break>　<line-break>あとで、オルファウスに<line-break>知らせた方がいいよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14なんということじゃ…。<line-break>あのネメアが…。<line-break>　<line-break>一度、オルファウスに<line-break>話してみた方が、<line-break>よさそうじゃな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15こんなことって…。<line-break>あのネメアさんが…。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>オルファウスさんに<line-break>知らせた方がよくないかな？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17まったく、王様殺して、<line-break>世間騒がしといて、<line-break>こんな所で死ぬなんて。　　　　　　<line-break>非常識にもほどがあってよ。<line-break>これは親の責任ね。<line-break>オルファウスに文句言いに行くわよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ウソぉ…。<line-break>ネメアさん、死んじゃったのぉ？<line-break>　<line-break>大変だよ、オルファウスさんに<line-break>知らせないと！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20死んだとは、思えんが…。<line-break>オルファウスには<line-break>知らせた方がよかろう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11ネメア様が…<line-break>こんなことって…<line-break> <line-break>…このことをオルファウスさんに<line-break>知らせましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21信じられない。　　　　　　　<line-break>ネメアが…、死んだ…？<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、オルファウスに<line-break>知らせよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23そんな…まさか…<line-break>ネメア様が…<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>、オルファウスに<line-break>知らせるぞ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27こんなことって…。<line-break>あのネメアが…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>オルファウスに<line-break>知らせよう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんということじゃ…。<line-break>あのネメアが…。<line-break>　<line-break>…あとで一度は猫屋敷に行って<line-break>報告した方がよさそうじゃな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24あのネメア様が…。<line-break>…あとで一度は猫屋敷に行って<line-break>オルファウス様に報告をしましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25うそ…死んじゃった…。<line-break>大変よ、オルファウスさんに<line-break>知らせないと！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>192…ネメアの消滅は、<line-break>猫屋敷に知らせねばなりませんね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193そ、そんな！？<line-break>わ、私たちのせいで<line-break>ネメアさんを死なせちゃった…！<line-break>わ、私のせいで…ご、ごめんなさい…！<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>オルファウスさんに謝らないと…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…次元の狭間への扉は<line-break>ジュサプブロスにしか開けられない。<line-break>奴は完全にシャリたちの手に落ちた。<line-break>一度、猫屋敷に戻ろう。<line-break>善後策を練らないと…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191そんな。<line-break>あのネメアが死ぬなんて。<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく<line-break>猫屋敷に報告に行ったほうが<line-break>いいかもしれない…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…バカな！<line-break>奴が死ぬなんて！<line-break>俺には考えられん！<line-break>…だが、なんにせよ<line-break>オルファウスたちには知らせないとな。<line-break>…奴らは、ネメアの家族なんだから。<end>あのネメアがやられちゃうなんて…。<line-break>こまったなぁ。歌の題材が<line-break>ひとりいなくなっちゃったよ。　　　<line-break>うーん、でも、<line-break>ほんとに死んじゃったのかな？<line-break>オルファウスにでも聞いてみる？ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3How could this be...? Lord Nemea... We should visit Cat Manor later and report what happened.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I think we might've done something really bad. But it was all that bearded four-eyes' fault, right? Maybe we're not to blame. Still, I suppose we should tell Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5You've got to be kidding me... Nemea's dead?! Hey, don't you think we ought to tell Orphaus?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6I can't believe it. Nemea... is dead? Don't you think we should tell Orphaus?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13That man said something strange―that Nemea had drawn closer to darkness. We should probably speak with Orphaus about it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, isn't this seriously bad? We should tell Orphaus later.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14How could this be...? Nemea... We should probably speak with Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15How could this happen...? Nemea... <value:$28>, don't you think we should tell Orphaus?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Honestly! First he kills a king and throws everyone into an uproar, then he goes and dies in a place like this. The sheer impropriety! Clearly, this is his parent's fault. We're going to complain to Orphaus!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19No way... Nemea's dead? This is terrible! We have to tell Orphaus!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20I don't believe he's dead... Even so, we should inform Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Lord Nemea... How could this happen...? We should tell Orphaus what happened.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21I can't believe it. Nemea... is dead? <value:$28>, let's tell Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23No... It can't be... Lord Nemea... <value:$28>, we're going to tell Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27How could this happen...? Nemea... <value:$28>, let's tell Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26How could this be...? Nemea... We should visit Cat Manor later and report what happened.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Lord Nemea... We should visit Cat Manor later and report this to Lord Orphaus.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25No... He died... This is terrible! We have to tell Orphaus!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192We must inform Cat Manor of Nemea's vanishing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193N-no! We got Nemea killed...! I-it was my fault... I'm s-so sorry...! <value:$28>! We have to apologize to Orphaus...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Only Jusabros can open the door to the Interdimensional Rift. Shalli and the others have him completely in their clutches. Let's return to Cat Manor and decide what to do next...<end><if><value:$01><equal>191No. Nemea can't be dead. In any case, perhaps we should go report this at Cat Manor...<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Impossible! He cannot be dead! I can't even conceive of it! But whatever happened, we must tell Orphaus and the others. They are Nemea's family.<end>I can't believe even Nemea was defeated... That's a problem. Now I've got one less subject for my songs. Hmm... but is he really dead? Shall we ask Orphaus?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3이럴 수가…. 그 네메아 님이…. …나중에 한 번 고양이 저택에 가서 보고하는 게 좋겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4왠지 큰일을 저질러 버린 것 같아. 그래도 저건 그 수염 안경 탓이지? 우린 잘못 없을지도. 그래도 오르파우스 씨에게는 말하는 게 좋을까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5거짓말이지… 야. 네메아가 죽은 거야!? 있잖아, 오르파우스에게 알리는 게 좋지 않을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6믿을 수 없어. 네메아가… 죽었다고…? 있잖아, 오르파우스 씨에게 알려야 하지 않을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13그 남자, 이상한 말을 했지. 네메아가 어둠에 가까워졌다나…. 한 번 오르파우스에게 이야기하는 게 좋겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 이거 엄청 위험한 거 아니야? 나중에 오르파우스에게 알리는 게 좋겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이럴 수가…. 그 네메아가…. 한 번 오르파우스에게 이야기하는 게 좋겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15이럴 수가…. 그 네메아 씨가…. 있잖아, <value:$28>. 오르파우스 씨에게 알리는 게 좋지 않을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17정말, 왕을 죽여 세상을 떠들썩하게 해 놓고 이런 데서 죽다니. 몰상식에도 정도가 있어. 이건 부모 책임이네. 오르파우스에게 따지러 가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19거짓말…. 네메아 씨 죽어 버린 거야아? 큰일이야, 오르파우스 씨에게 알려야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20죽었다고는 생각되지 않지만…. 오르파우스에게는 알리는 게 좋겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11네메아 님이… 이럴 수가…. …이 일을 오르파우스 씨에게 알리자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21믿을 수 없어. 네메아가… 죽었다고…? <value:$28>, 오르파우스에게 알리자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23그럴 수가… 설마… 네메아 님이…. <value:$28>, 오르파우스에게 알리자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이럴 수가…. 그 네메아가…. <value:$28>. 오르파우스에게 알리자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26이럴 수가…. 그 네메아가…. …나중에 고양이 저택에 가서 보고하는 게 좋겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24그 네메아 님이…. …나중에 고양이 저택에 가서 오르파우스 님에게 보고하죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25거짓말… 죽어 버렸어…. 큰일이야, 오르파우스 씨에게 알려야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…네메아의 소멸은 고양이 저택에 알려야겠군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그, 그럴 수가!? 우, 우리 때문에 네메아 씨를 죽게 했어…! 내, 내 탓이야… 미안해요…! <value:$28> 씨! 오르파우스 씨에게 사과해야 해요…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…차원의 틈으로 가는 문은 주사프브로스밖에 열 수 없어. 녀석은 완전히 샤리 일당 손에 넘어갔어. 일단 고양이 저택으로 돌아가자. 대책을 세워야 해….<end><if><value:$01><equal>191그럴 수가. 그 네메아가 죽다니. 아무튼 고양이 저택에 보고하러 가는 게 좋을지도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>190…말도 안 돼! 그 녀석이 죽다니! 난 믿을 수 없어! …하지만 어쨌든 오르파우스들에게는 알려야 해. …그들은 네메아의 가족이니까.<end>그 네메아가 당하다니…. 곤란하네. 노래 소재가 하나 줄었어. 음, 그래도 정말 죽은 걸까? 오르파우스에게라도 물어볼래?<end>"

## 1800175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3ネメア様を追わんでよいのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4うふふ、<value:$28>って<line-break>ホントに忘れんぼさん。<line-break>教えてあげよっかな。<line-break>ね、ここにはネメア様を<line-break>追ってきたんだけど、<line-break>追わなくてもいいの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5おい、<line-break>ネメアを追わなくても<line-break>いいのかよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6え？<line-break>ネメアを追わなくていいの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…ネメアを追うのでは<line-break>なかったのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、<line-break>ネメアを追わなくても<line-break>いいのかよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14なんじゃ。<line-break>ネメアを追うとるんじゃ<line-break>なかったのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアさんを追わなくていいの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17なによぉ！　なによぉ！　なによぉ！　<line-break>あのネメアって男のあとを追って<line-break>ここに来たんじゃなかったのぉ？<line-break>行き当たりばったりばっかり！<line-break>もうイヤ！　もうイヤ！　もうイヤ！<line-break>信じられない。ホント信じられないわ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアさんを追わなくてもいいのぉ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…ネメアを<line-break>追わぬのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>11ネメア様を追わないの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>21どうした、<value:$28>。<line-break>ネメアを追いかけないのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>23どうした、<value:$28>。<line-break>ネメア様を追わないのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…ネメアを追うのでは<line-break>なかったのか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんじゃ。<line-break>ネメアを追わんのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>24どうかされたのですか？<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>ネメア様を追わないのですか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>25どうしたの？<line-break>追わないの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>192ネメアの追跡は、<line-break>断念しますか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193そうですよね！<line-break>もう、ネメアさんを追っかけるのは<line-break>終わりにするんですよね？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194ゼグナ鉱山を出るのかい？<line-break>それがいい。<line-break>奴の心配なんてする必要はない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191ネメアを追わなくていいの？<line-break>鉱山の張り詰めた空気…。<line-break>なんだか嫌な予感がするんだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190ネメアを追ってる途中だってのに<line-break>ゼグナを出ちまうのか？<line-break>後悔しない選択をしろよ？<end>あれ？<line-break>ネメアのあとをつけてたんだよね？<line-break>ネメアはまだ、この中だよ。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Not going after Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Hee hee, <value:$28>, you really are forgetful. Shall I remind you? We came here after Lord Nemea, remember? Aren't you going to keep following him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hey, aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Huh? Aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Weren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What's this? Weren't you following Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>, aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What?! What?! What?! Didn't we come here following that man Nemea? Always making it up as you go! I've had it! I've had it! I've had it! I can't believe this. I really can't!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Not pursuing Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Aren't you going after Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21What's wrong, <value:$28>? Aren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23What's wrong, <value:$28>? Aren't you going after Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Weren't you going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What's this? Not going after Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Is something wrong, <value:$28>? Are you not going after Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25What's wrong? Not going after him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Will you abandon the pursuit of Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193That's right! You're done chasing after Nemea now, aren't you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Are you leaving Zegna Mine? Good. There's no need to worry about him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Aren't you going after Nemea? The air in this mine is so tense... I have a bad feeling about this...<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Are you leaving Zegna Mine when you're still pursuing Nemea? Choose a path you won't regret, all right?<end>Huh? I thought we were following Nemea. He's still in here.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3네메아 님을 쫓지 않아도 되는가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4후후, <value:$28>는 정말 잘 잊네. 알려 줄까? 여기엔 네메아 님을 쫓아온 건데, 안 쫓아도 돼?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5야, 네메아를 안 쫓아도 되는 거냐.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6어? 네메아를 안 쫓아도 돼?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…네메아를 쫓는 게 아니었나?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 네메아를 안 쫓아도 돼?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14뭐냐. 네메아를 쫓고 있던 게 아니냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, <value:$28>, 네메아 씨를 안 쫓아도 돼?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야! 뭐야! 뭐야! 네메아라는 남자를 쫓아 여기 온 거 아니었어? 즉흥적으로 움직이기만 하고! 정말 싫어! 못 믿겠어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, 네메아 씨를 안 쫓아도 돼에?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…네메아를 쫓지 않는가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11네메아 님을 안 쫓는 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21왜 그러지, <value:$28>. 네메아를 쫓지 않을 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23왜 그러지, <value:$28>. 네메아 님을 쫓지 않을 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…네메아를 쫓는 게 아니었나?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26뭐냐. 네메아를 안 쫓는가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24무슨 일이십니까, <value:$28> 님. 네메아 님을 쫓지 않으십니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25왜 그래? 안 쫓아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192네메아 추적은 단념합니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그렇죠! 이제 네메아 씨를 쫓는 건 그만둘 거죠?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194제그나 광산을 나갈 거야? 잘 생각했어. 녀석 걱정 따윈 할 필요 없어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191네메아를 안 쫓아도 돼? 광산의 팽팽한 공기…. 왠지 불길한 예감이 드는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>190네메아를 쫓는 중인데 제그나를 나갈 건가? 후회하지 않을 선택을 해.<end>어라? 네메아의 뒤를 쫓고 있었지? 네메아는 아직 이 안에 있어.<end>"

## 1800176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、ネメアを追う<end>"
EN: "Keep following Nemea<end>"
KO: "계속 네메아를 쫓는다<end>"

## 1800177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアはいい、ゼグナを出る<end>"
EN: "Forget Nemea and leave Zegna<end>"
KO: "네메아는 됐어, 제그나를 나간다<end>"

## 1800178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルが、　　　　　　　　<line-break>攻めてくるのですね。<line-break>王宮内は大騒ぎです。<line-break>レムオン様は、貴族をまとめて、<line-break>出兵を拒否なされたそうですし。<end>"
EN: "So Dyneskal is going to attack. The whole palace is in an uproar. I hear Lord Lemghon has rallied the nobles and refused to send troops.<end>"
KO: "딘갈이 공격해 오는군요. 왕궁 안은 큰 소동입니다. 레무온 님은 귀족들을 규합해 출병을 거부하셨다고 하고요.<end>"

## 1800179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "国内がこれだけ乱れていて、<line-break>ディンガル軍に<line-break>勝てるのでしょうか…。<line-break>でも…、<value:$28>様の<line-break>助けがあれば、勝てるかもしれないと、<line-break>ゼネテス様がおっしゃっていました。<end>"
EN: "With the country in such turmoil, can we really defeat the Dyneskal Army? Still, Xenetes said we might have a chance with your help, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "국내가 이렇게 혼란스러운데 딘갈군을 이길 수 있을까요…. 그래도… <value:$28> 님의 도움이 있다면 이길지도 모른다고 제네테스 님께서 말씀하셨어요.<end>"

## 1800180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…、ゼネテス様に<line-break>力を貸していただけませんか？<line-break>　<line-break>無茶苦茶なお願いだということは、<line-break>わかっています。でも…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, would you help Xenetes? I know it's an unreasonable thing to ask. But...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 제네테스 님께 힘을 빌려주실 수 없나요? 무리한 부탁이라는 건 알아요. 하지만….<end>"

## 1800181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を貸そう<end>"
EN: "I'll help.<end>"
KO: "힘을 빌려주지<end>"

## 1800182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他にやることがある<end>"
EN: "I have something else to do.<end>"
KO: "다른 할 일이 있다<end>"

## 1800183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…本当ですか？<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテス様は、謁見の間で<line-break>お待ちしているそうです。<end>"
EN: "Do you really mean it? Thank you. I believe Xenetes is waiting in the audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "…정말입니까? 감사합니다. 제네테스 님은 알현실에서 기다리고 계신다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1800184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それから…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and one more thing...<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고….<end>"

## 1800185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、感じるのです。今、<line-break>世界に大きな闇が迫っています。<line-break>とても大きな闇が。<line-break>私はずっと闇の中で<line-break>暮らしてきました。<line-break>だから、感じるのです。<end>"
EN: "I can feel it. A great darkness is closing in on the world. A vast darkness. I have lived in darkness all my life. That is why I can feel it.<end>"
KO: "…저는 느껴요. 지금 세계에 거대한 어둠이 다가오고 있습니다. 아주 거대한 어둠이. 저는 줄곧 어둠 속에서 살아왔기에 느낄 수 있어요.<end>"

## 1800186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目に見えなくとも、<line-break>それは確かに存在しています。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様は、<line-break>いつかその闇と戦うことに<line-break>なるのですね…。<end>"
EN: "It may be invisible, but it is undoubtedly there. Someday, <value:$28>, you will find yourself fighting that darkness...<end>"
KO: "눈에 보이지 않아도 그것은 분명 존재합니다. <value:$28>님은 언젠가 그 어둠과 싸우게 되겠군요….<end>"

## 1800187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうか、お気をつけて。<end>"
EN: "Please be careful, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 부디 조심하십시오.<end>"

## 1800188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来てくれたのか、<value:$28>。<line-break>状況は知ってると思うが、<line-break>…最悪だ。<line-break>つっても、まだ事態は悪くなりえるから<line-break>最悪じゃねえか。<line-break>とすると、少しは気がラクだな。<end>"
EN: "You came, <value:$28>. Guess you know the situation. It's... the worst. Though if things can still get worse, I guess it isn't the worst after all. Makes me feel a little better.<end>"
KO: "와 줬구나, <value:$28>. 상황은 알고 있겠지만… 최악이야. 그래도 아직 더 나빠질 수 있으니 지금이 최악은 아닌 건가. 그렇게 생각하면 조금 마음이 편하네.<end>"

## 1800189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>仕方ありませんね。<end>"
EN: "I see... It can't be helped, then.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까…. 어쩔 수 없군요.<end>"

## 1800190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの、私…。<end>"
EN: "Um... I...<end>"
KO: "…저, 저는….<end>"

## 1800191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、感じるのです。今、<line-break>世界に大きな闇が迫っています。<line-break>とても大きな闇が。<line-break>私はずっと闇の中で<line-break>暮らしてきました。<line-break>だから、感じられるのです。<end>"
EN: "I can feel it. A great darkness is closing in on the world. A vast darkness. I have lived in darkness all my life. That is why I am able to feel it.<end>"
KO: "…저는 느낄 수 있어요. 지금 세계에 거대한 어둠이 다가오고 있습니다. 아주 거대한 어둠이. 저는 줄곧 어둠 속에서 살아왔기에 느낄 수 있어요.<end>"

## 1800192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec180-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目に見えなくとも、<line-break>それは確かに存在しています。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様の<line-break>しなければならないこととは、<line-break>その闇と関係があるのですか？<end>"
EN: "It may be invisible, but it is undoubtedly there. Is what you must do connected to that darkness, <value:$28>?<end>"
