## 1840068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>アキュリュースの傭兵としてではなく、<line-break>冒険者のあなたにお願いします。　　　<line-break>イークレムン様を助けてください。<line-break>おそらく、イークレムン様も<line-break>それを待っていると思います。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I ask this of you not as a mercenary of Aqyurius, but as an adventurer. Please save Lady Iiklmn. I believe she is waiting for you to do just that.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 아큐류스 용병으로서가 아니라 모험가인 당신께 부탁드립니다. 이크레문 님을 도와주세요. 아마 이크레문 님도 그것을 기다리고 계실 겁니다.<end>"

## 1840069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのイヤリングは　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>あなたが持っていてください。<line-break>何かの役に立つかもしれません。<line-break>それでは、イークレムン様のこと、<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Please keep the earring. It may prove useful somehow. I leave Lady Iiklmn in your hands, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 귀걸이는 당신이 가지고 계세요. 무언가 도움이 될지도 모릅니다. 그럼 이크레문 님을 잘 부탁드립니다. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1840070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人魚のイヤリングを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained the Mermaid Earrings.<end>"
KO: "인어의 귀걸이를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1840071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン様のこと、<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Please look after Iiklmn.<end>"
KO: "이클렘 님을 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1840072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その耳飾りは、先代の水の巫女である　<line-break>ルフェイ様が、恋仲の男性から<line-break>贈られたものだと聞いています。<line-break>つまり、イークレムン様にとっては<line-break>ご両親の形見ということになるのです。<end>"
EN: "I was told Lufei, the previous Priestess of Water, received it as a gift from a man with whom she was romantically involved. That would make it a memento of Iiklmn's parents.<end>"
KO: "그 귀걸이는 선대 물의 무녀 루페이 님께서 사랑하던 남성에게 받은 것이라고 들었습니다. 즉 이크렘 님에게는 부모님의 유품인 셈입니다.<end>"

## 1840073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアにより西方攻略を命じられた<line-break>白虎将軍ジラークは、<line-break>難航不落といわれたアキュリュースを<line-break>ほぼ無傷のまま支配下に収めた。<end>"
EN: "Ordered by Nemea to conquer the west, White Tiger General Zsilak brought Aqyurius, said to be impregnable, under his control almost entirely intact.<end>"
KO: "네메아에게 서방 공략을 명령받은 백호장군 지라크는 난공불락이라 불리던 아큐류스를 거의 피해 없이 지배하에 두었다.<end>"

## 1840074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この作戦に貢献したシャリと名乗る<line-break>傭兵は、報酬として水の巫女の少女を<line-break>要求。彼女を連れていずこかへと<line-break>姿を消した。<end>"
EN: "A mercenary calling himself Shalli, who had contributed to the campaign, demanded the Priestess of Water, a young girl, as his reward. He then disappeared with her, bound for parts unknown.<end>"
KO: "이 작전에 공헌한 샤리라는 용병은 보수로 물의 무녀 소녀를 요구했다. 그는 그녀를 데리고 어디론가 사라졌다.<end>"

## 1840075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークは引き続き大陸統一構想に<line-break>従い大陸北西部への進軍を計画。<line-break>神聖都市アルノートゥン攻略に<line-break>向けて軍を進めようとしていた。<end>"
EN: "Still pursuing the plan to unite the continent, Zsilak planned an advance into the continent's northwest and prepared to march his forces on the sacred city of Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "지라크는 계속 대륙 통일 구상에 따라 대륙 북서부 진군을 계획했다. 신성도시 알노툰 공략을 향해 군을 움직이려 했다.<end>"

## 1840076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんということでしょう…。<line-break>次代の水の巫女となるべき<line-break>イークレムン様が…。<line-break>ミズチも撃ち滅ぼされ、<line-break>もはやこの町を支えるものは<line-break>何も…何もありません。<end>"
EN: "How can this be...? Iiklmn was to become the next Priestess of Water... The Mizuchi have been wiped out as well. Now there is nothing left to sustain this town. Nothing...<end>"
KO: "이럴 수가…. 다음 세대의 물의 무녀가 되셔야 할 이크레문 님이…. 미즈치들도 격멸당했고, 이제 이 마을을 떠받칠 것은 아무것도… 아무것도 없습니다.<end>"

## 1840077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この町は、守られたのですね。<line-break>でも、イークレムン様は…。<end>"
EN: "So the town has been saved. But Iiklmn...<end>"
KO: "이 마을은 지켜졌군요. 하지만 이크레문 님은….<end>"

## 1840078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>大丈夫か？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まったく、無茶をしおる。<line-break>相手はあのミズチらを一撃で<line-break>倒すほどの魔道士なのに。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, are you all right? Honestly, that was reckless. That mage defeated those Mizuchi with a single blow.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 괜찮나? 정말 무모하군. 상대는 그 미즈치들을 일격에 쓰러뜨릴 정도의 마도사인데.<end>"

## 1840079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、お主がやらなんだなら<line-break>きっとわしが同じように<line-break>飛び出しただろうがな。<line-break>あのイークレムンという娘…、<line-break>確信は持てんが、おそらくは<line-break>わしの…。<end>"
EN: "Still, if you hadn't rushed in, I surely would have done the same. That girl Iiklmn... I cannot be certain, but she may be my...<end>"
KO: "하지만 자네가 그러지 않았더라면 분명 나도 똑같이 뛰쳐나갔을 걸세. 그 이크렘이라는 소녀… 확신은 없지만 아마도 나의….<end>"

## 1840080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>あれは…。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Is that...?<end>"
KO: "응? 저건….<end>"

## 1840081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを見ろ。<end>"
EN: "Look at this.<end>"
KO: "이걸 봐.<end>"

## 1840082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、人魚のウロコで作った<line-break>耳飾りじゃ。<line-break>イークレムンが落としていった…。<line-break>やはり、そうなのか…。<end>"
EN: "It is an earring made from a mermaid's scale. Iiklmn dropped it... So I was right...<end>"
KO: "그건 인어의 비늘로 만든 귀걸이다. 이크레문이 떨어뜨리고 갔지…. 역시 그런 건가….<end>"

## 1840083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、すまんが<line-break>わしに力を貸してくれんか？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>イークレムンを、あの不気味な魔道士<line-break>シャリから取り戻したいのじゃ。<line-break>お主がいれば、頼りになる。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'm sorry to ask, but will you lend me your strength? I want to rescue Iiklmn from that sinister mage, Shalli. I could count on you at my side.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안하지만 내게 힘을 빌려 주지 않겠나? 이크레문을 그 기분 나쁜 마도사 샤리에게서 되찾고 싶다. 네가 있으면 든든하겠어.<end>"

## 1840084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、わしはジラークに<line-break>単独行動の許可を受けてくる。<line-break>またあとで合流じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall ask Zsilak for permission to act independently. We can meet again later.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나는 지라크에게 단독 행동 허가를 받고 오마. 나중에 다시 합류하자.<end>"

## 1840085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、まずは二手に分かれて動こう。<line-break>何かあったら、以前お主が話しとった<line-break>猫屋敷で呼び出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "For now, let us split up. If anything happens, call for me at the Cat Manor you mentioned before.<end>"
KO: "그럼 우선 둘로 나눠 움직이자. 무슨 일이 생기면 전에 자네가 말한 고양이 저택에서 불러 주게.<end>"

## 1840086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、お主に預けておこう。<line-break>何かの手がかりになるかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "I will leave this with you. It may provide some clue.<end>"
KO: "이건 자네에게 맡겨두지. 무언가 단서가 될지도 모르네.<end>"

## 1840087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、ひとまずさらばじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Until we meet again, then.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 일단 작별이다.<end>"

## 1840088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…さっきの大きな赤い鎧の方は<line-break>もしや、ルフェイ様の…。<line-break>ああ、なんと数奇な運命でしょう！？<end>"
EN: "Um... could that great red-armored figure from earlier have been Lufei's...? Oh, what a strange turn of fate!<end>"
KO: "저… 아까 그 커다란 붉은 갑옷의 분은 혹시 루페이 님의…. 아아, 이 무슨 기구한 운명이란 말입니까!?<end>"

## 1840089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この町は、守られたのですね。<line-break>でも、イークレムン様は…。<end>"
EN: "The town has been saved. But Iiklmn...<end>"
KO: "이 마을은 지켜졌군요. 하지만 이크레문 님은….<end>"

## 1840090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンは<line-break>軍を離れ単独行動を開始。<line-break>娘である水の巫女イークレムンを<line-break>取り戻すための旅の道を踏み出した。<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan left the army and struck out alone, embarking on a journey to bring back his daughter, Iiklmn, the Priestess of Water.<end>"
KO: "앙길단은 군을 떠나 단독 행동을 시작했다. 딸인 물의 무녀 이크레문을 되찾기 위한 여행에 나섰다.<end>"

## 1840091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン！！<end>"
EN: "Iiklmn!<end>"
KO: "이클레문!!<end>"

## 1840092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、やぼったいなあ。<line-break>おじさんの出る幕じゃないんじゃない？<end>"
EN: "Honestly, how tactless. This is no place for an old man to butt in, is it?<end>"
KO: "이런, 촌스럽군. 아저씨가 나설 차례는 아니지 않아?<end>"

## 1840093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "軽口に付き合う気はない！<line-break>その娘を渡すわけにはいかん！<end>"
EN: "I have no time for your mockery! I will not let you take that girl!<end>"
KO: "농담에 어울려 줄 생각은 없다! 그 아이를 넘길 수는 없어!<end>"

## 1840094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、面倒だなあ。<line-break>これで、黙っててよ。<end>"
EN: "You're such a nuisance. This should keep you quiet.<end>"
KO: "정말 귀찮네. 이걸로 조용히 해 줘.<end>"

## 1840095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、じゃ、行こうかな。<line-break>バイバ～イ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose I'll be going. Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 갈까. 바이바~이.<end>"

## 1840096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…年甲斐もなく<line-break>熱くなりすぎた…かの。<line-break>どうやらここが限界らしいわい。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>頼む、イークレムンを…いや、<line-break>わしの娘を助けてやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I let myself get too worked up for a man my age... It seems this is as far as I can go. <value:$28>, please, save Iiklmn... No, save my daughter.<end>"
KO: "…나잇값도 못 하고 너무 열을 올렸나 보군. 아무래도 여기까지인 것 같아. <value:$28>, 부탁한다. 이크레문을… 아니, 내 딸을 구해 줘.<end>"

## 1840097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人魚のウロコで作った耳飾りじゃ。<line-break>今、イークレムンが落としていった。<line-break>なんらかの手がかりになろう。<end>"
EN: "It is an earring made from a mermaid's scale. Iiklmn dropped it just now. It may provide some clue.<end>"
KO: "인어의 비늘로 만든 귀걸이다. 방금 이크레문이 떨어뜨리고 갔어. 무언가 단서가 되겠지.<end>"

## 1840098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "老いぼれの最期の頼みと思って<line-break>聞いてやってくれ。<line-break>あとは、任せたぞ…。<end>"
EN: "Grant an old man his final request. The rest is in your hands...<end>"
KO: "늙은이의 마지막 부탁이라 생각하고 들어다오. 나머지는 맡기마….<end>"

## 1840100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…あの赤い鎧のお方は<line-break>もしや、ルフェイ様の…？<line-break>ああ、なんと悲しい運命でしょう！<end>"
EN: "Um... that person in red armor―could that be Lufei's...? Oh, what a tragic fate!<end>"
KO: "저… 저 붉은 갑옷을 입은 분은 혹시 루페이 님의…? 아아, 이 얼마나 슬픈 운명인가요!<end>"

## 1840101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってあげてください。<line-break>たぶん、あの方は、倒れる姿を<line-break>あなたに見られたくないのです。<end>"
EN: "Please go. That person probably doesn't want to be seen falling―not by you.<end>"
KO: "가 주세요. 아마 그분은 쓰러지는 모습을 당신에게 보이고 싶지 않은 겁니다.<end>"

## 1840102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この時、朱雀将軍アンギルダンは、<line-break>自らの娘を巡って魔道士シャリと対立。<line-break>シャリの魔法の前に倒れ、<line-break>過去の名将となった。<end>"
EN: "At this time, Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan came into conflict with the mage Shalli over his daughter. Felled by Shalli's magic, the famed general passed into history.<end>"
KO: "이때 주작장군 앙길단은 자신의 딸을 두고 마도사 샤리와 대립했다. 샤리의 마법 앞에 쓰러져 과거의 명장으로 남았다.<end>"

## 1840103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　ディンガル帝国は、アンギルダンという<line-break>　　　　優秀な将軍を失いながらも、<line-break>　　　　その勢いを損なうことなく<line-break>　　大陸に覇をとなえようとしていた…。<end>"
EN: "Despite losing an outstanding general in Angeerdan, the Dyneskal Empire's momentum remained undiminished as it sought dominion over the continent...<end>"
KO: "딩갈 제국은 앙길단이라는 뛰어난 장군을 잃었음에도 기세를 잃지 않고 대륙에 패권을 떨치려 하고 있었다….<end>"

## 1840104

KO: "<value:$28> 씨.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "안녕하세요, <value:$28>님. 하나 묻고 싶은 게 있습니다. 괜찮을까요?<end>"

## 1840106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、水の精霊神に近づこうとする<line-break>存在を感じます。<line-break>…あなたではありませんか？<end>"
EN: "Lately, I've sensed a presence trying to draw near to the Water Spirit God. Could that be you?<end>"
KO: "최근 물의 정령신에게 가까이 다가가려는 존재가 느껴집니다. …당신 아닙니까?<end>"

## 1840107

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "바로 그 말대로입니다<end>"

## 1840108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは人違い<end>"
EN: "You've got the wrong person.<end>"
KO: "사람을 잘못 봤다<end>"

## 1840109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>…やはり…。<end>"
EN: "I see... I thought as much.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. …역시….<end>"

## 1840110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…水の神殿に仕える私が言うのも<line-break>なんですが、水の精霊神の言うことは<line-break>聞かない方がいいと思います。<line-break>精霊神はあなたに<line-break>隠していることがあります。<line-break>気をつけた方がいいです。<end>"
EN: "I realize this may sound strange coming from someone who serves at the Water Temple, but I don't think you should heed the Water Spirit God. The Spirit God is hiding something from you. Be careful.<end>"
KO: "…물의 신전을 섬기는 제가 이런 말을 하기도 그렇지만, 물의 정령신 말은 듣지 않는 편이 좋다고 생각합니다. 정령신은 당신에게 숨기는 게 있어요. 조심하는 게 좋습니다.<end>"

## 1840111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとの判断はあなたにお任せします。<line-break>…突然こんなことを言い出して<line-break>ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "What you do now is up to you. I'm sorry to spring this on you so suddenly.<end>"
KO: "나머지 판단은 당신께 맡기겠습니다. …갑자기 이런 말을 꺼내서 미안해요.<end>"

## 1840112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでしたか。<line-break>これは大変失礼いたしました。<line-break>　<line-break>私の言ったことは<line-break>忘れてくださって結構です。<line-break>それでは、失礼します。<end>"
EN: "I see. Please accept my sincerest apologies. You may forget everything I said. Now, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그랬군요. 대단히 실례했습니다. 제가 한 말은 잊어 주셔도 됩니다. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1840113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここより先は水の神殿。　　　　<line-break>水の精霊神の<line-break>聖なる座所です。<line-break>神殿の許しなき者は<line-break>入ることが許されておりません。<line-break>どうぞ、おひきとりください。<end>"
EN: "Beyond this point lies the Water Temple, sacred seat of the Water Spirit God. No one may enter without the temple's permission. Please turn back.<end>"
KO: "이 앞은 물의 신전, 물의 정령신의 성스러운 처소입니다. 신전의 허락을 받지 않은 자는 들어갈 수 없습니다. 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1840114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　私の声が聞こえますか…？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　私は、イークレムンの母<line-break>　　　　　　ルフェイと申します。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... <value:$28>... Can you hear my voice...? I am Lufei, Iiklmn's mother.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. <value:$28>…. 제 목소리가 들리나요…? 저는 이크렘의 어머니 루페이라고 합니다.<end>"

## 1840115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　今、ミズチたちの魂と　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　人魚のイヤリングの力を借りて　　　<line-break>　　　　　あなたに話しかけています。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　私の娘イークレムンは<line-break>　　　　　　今、竜骨の砂漠にある<line-break>　　　　ラドラスに捕らえられています。<end>"
EN: "I am speaking to you now with the aid of the souls of the Mizuchi and the power of the Mermaid Earrings. My daughter Iiklmn is being held captive in Ladras, in the Dragonbone Desert.<end>"
KO: "지금 미즈치들의 영혼과 인어의 귀걸이의 힘을 빌려 당신에게 말을 걸고 있습니다. 제 딸 이크렘은 지금 용골의 사막에 있는 라드라스에 붙잡혀 있습니다.<end>"

## 1840116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　お願いです。<line-break>　　　　あの子を助けてあげてください。　　　<line-break>　　　　世界が悲しみに包まれる前に…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　私はすでに身体なき身なれば、<line-break>　　　　何をもなすことができません。<line-break>　　　　あなただけが、頼りなのです。<end>"
EN: "Please, save her before the world is engulfed in sorrow... I am already without a body and can do nothing. You are my only hope.<end>"
KO: "부탁드립니다. 그 아이를 구해 주세요. 세계가 슬픔에 뒤덮이기 전에…. 저는 이미 육신이 없는 몸이라 아무것도 할 수 없습니다. 오직 당신만이 희망입니다.<end>"

## 1840117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　お願いします。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>…………。<end>"
EN: "Please... <value:$28>... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "부탁드립니다. <value:$28>…. <value:$28>………….<end>"

## 1840118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に、いいお天気ですね…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>私は、お仕事がなければ<line-break>大抵、ここにいます。<line-break>また、いらしてください。<end>"
EN: "It really is lovely weather... When I don't have work to do, I'm usually here. Please come again.<end>"
KO: "정말 좋은 날씨네요…. 저는 일이 없으면 대개 여기에 있습니다. 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1840119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>こんにちは。<line-break>今日もいいお天気ですね…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, hello, <value:$28>. Lovely weather again today, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님, 안녕하세요. 오늘도 날씨가 좋네요….<end>"

## 1840120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの方々と…<line-break>アキュリュースの町の人…、　　<line-break>結構、うまくやってます。<line-break>時々、物資の調達とかをされて<line-break>町が騒ぎ立つこともありますけど、<line-break>普段は、穏やかなものです。<end>"
EN: "The people from Dyneskal and the townsfolk of Aqyurius are getting along quite well. Sometimes there's a commotion in town over things like obtaining supplies, but most of the time it's peaceful.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 사람들과… 아큐류스 마을 사람들, 제법 잘 지내고 있어요. 가끔 물자를 조달한다고 마을이 시끄러워지긴 하지만 평소에는 평온해요.<end>"

## 1840121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰の支配下かなんて、本当は<line-break>どうでもいいことで、大切なのは<line-break>お互いどう接しあうかなんですよね…。<end>"
EN: "Being under one ruler or another doesn't really matter. What matters is how we treat one another...<end>"
KO: "누구의 지배 아래 있느냐는 사실은 사실 별로 중요하지 않고, 중요한 건 서로 어떻게 대하느냐겠죠….<end>"

## 1840122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "水の巫女になって、<line-break>私の中で色々ガラッと変わりました。<line-break>　<line-break>だから世の中も変わるかなあって、<line-break>…そんな風に考えてたんですけど<line-break>なんだか、そんなに違いません。<end>"
EN: "Becoming the Priestess of Water changed so many things inside me, all at once. I thought perhaps the world would change as well... but somehow, it doesn't seem all that different.<end>"
KO: "물의 무녀가 되고 나서 제 안에서 여러 가지가 확 바뀌었어요. 그래서 세상도 바뀔까 생각했는데… 왠지 그렇게 다르지는 않네요.<end>"

## 1840123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少しは、お仕事が増えたけど、<line-break>やっぱり私はここにいて<line-break>こうしてお日様をながめてる…。　　　<line-break>水の流れと、おんなじですね。<line-break>少しずつ、絶対に前の瞬間とは違うのに<line-break>やっぱり、そこを流れてる…。<end>"
EN: "I have a little more work now, but I'm still here, gazing up at the sun like this... It's just like the flow of water. Little by little, it's never quite the same as it was a moment before, and yet it still flows through the same place...<end>"
KO: "조금은 일이 늘었지만 역시 나는 여기에서 이렇게 햇볕을 바라보고 있어…. 물의 흐름과 똑같네요. 조금씩, 분명 전 순간과는 다른데 그래도 계속 그곳을 흘러가고 있어….<end>"

## 1840124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、先代の水の巫女の…、<line-break>お母様のことを、よく考えます。<line-break>　<line-break>お母様は、ア…<line-break>いえ、お父様のことを<line-break>深く愛しておられました。<end>"
EN: "Lately, I've often found myself thinking about my mother, the previous Priestess of Water... She loved A― no, my father very deeply.<end>"
KO: "요즘 선대 물의 무녀였던… 어머니 생각을 자주 합니다. 어머니는 아… 아니, 아버지를 깊이 사랑하셨습니다.<end>"

## 1840125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、自分のことみたいに<line-break>わかってしまうので、時々<line-break>戸惑ってしまうんですけど…。<line-break>私も、将来、大切な誰かを<line-break>のちの時代に誇れるくらいに<line-break>深く愛したいものです…。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes it unsettles me, because I understand that love as if it were my own... Someday, I hope I can love someone dear to me just as deeply―with a love worthy of pride even in ages to come.<end>"
KO: "그게 마치 제 일처럼 느껴져서 가끔 당황하기도 합니다만…. 저도 언젠가는 소중한 누군가를 후대에 자랑할 수 있을 만큼 깊이 사랑하고 싶습니다….<end>"

## 1840126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>すいません。<line-break>また、ボーッとしてしまって…。<line-break>でも、本当にいいお天気ですね…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I'm sorry. My mind wandered again... But it really is lovely weather, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "아… 죄송해요. 또 멍하니 있었네요… 하지만 정말 좋은 날씨네요…<end>"

## 1840127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　よく来ました、無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　<line-break>　　　私は水の精霊神アキュリュース。<line-break>　海王をしりぞけ、私を訪れてくれたこと、<line-break>　　　　　大変嬉しく思います。<line-break>　　その強さを見込んでお願いがあります。<line-break>　　　　私の力を封じる恐ろしき巨人を<line-break>　　　　あなたの力で倒してください。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I am Aqyurius, the water spirit god. I am very pleased that you drove back the Sea King and came to see me. I have a request to make of one as strong as you. Please use your strength to defeat the fearsome giant who seals away my power.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔습니다, 무한의 소울을 가진 자여. 저는 물의 정령신 아큐류스. 해왕을 물리치고 저를 찾아와 준 것을 매우 기쁘게 생각합니다. 그 강함을 믿고 부탁이 있습니다. 제 힘을 봉인하는 무시무시한 거인을 당신의 힘으로 쓰러뜨려 주세요.<end>"

## 1840128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たやすい話です<end>"
EN: "That will be easy.<end>"
KO: "쉬운 일입니다<end>"

## 1840129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておきます<end>"
EN: "I'd rather not.<end>"
KO: "그만두겠습니다<end>"

## 1840130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　ありがとうございます。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　では、あなたを<line-break>　　　封印の扉へとみちびきましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　優しき水の光に身をゆだね、　　　　　<line-break>　　封印の巨人ゲヘナに挑んでください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Then allow me to guide you to the sealed door. Entrust yourself to the gentle light of the water, and face Gehenna, the sealed giant.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 그럼 당신을 봉인의 문으로 안내하겠습니다. 부드러운 물의 빛에 몸을 맡기고 봉인의 거인 게헤나에게 도전해 주세요.<end>"

## 1840131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　そうですか、　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　それは残念です…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　またいつか、気が変わったら<line-break>　　　　　　ここに来てください。<line-break>　　　　私はいつまでも待っています…。<end>"
EN: "I see. What a pity... If you ever change your mind, please come back. I will wait for you forever...<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까, 안타깝군요…. 언젠가 마음이 바뀌면 다시 이곳으로 와 주세요. 저는 언제까지나 기다리겠습니다….<end>"

## 1840132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　…どうしました？　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　巨人は目の前ですよ？<end>"
EN: "…What's wrong? The giant is right in front of you, you know.<end>"
KO: "…왜 그러시죠? 거인은 눈앞에 있습니다.<end>"

## 1840133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、行きます<end>"
EN: "Yes, I'll go.<end>"
KO: "네, 가겠습니다<end>"

## 1840134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…恐いです<end>"
EN: "Um... I'm scared.<end>"
KO: "저기… 무서워요<end>"

## 1840135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　はい、頑張ってください。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　期待していますよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Do your best. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "네, 힘내세요. 기대하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1840136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　そうですか、仕方ありませんね。　　　<line-break>　では、あなたの世界までお送りしましょう。<line-break>　またいつか、私のもとを訪ねてください…。<end>"
EN: "I see. It cannot be helped, then. I shall send you to your world. Please come and visit me again someday...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요, 어쩔 수 없네요. 그럼 당신의 세계까지 보내 드리겠습니다. 언젠가 다시 저를 찾아와 주세요….<end>"

## 1840137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…人の子か。<line-break>アキュリュースめの仕業だな。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…多くは語るまい。<line-break>汝はただ、焼き尽くされるがいい。<end>"
EN: "So... a child of man. That wretch Aqyurius is behind this. I shall say no more. You need only be consumed by fire.<end>"
KO: "…인간의 아이인가. 아큐류스의 짓이군. …더 말하지 않겠다. 너는 그저 불타 없어져라.<end>"

## 1840138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、不覚。<line-break>我が力、及ばぬとはな。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>人の子よ、<line-break>しかし、汝はその行いの意味を<line-break>知っているのか…ガハッ。<end>"
EN: "Guh... I was careless. To think my power would fall short. But tell me, child of man: do you understand what your deed means? Gah!<end>"
KO: "크, 불찰이군. 내 힘이 미치지 못하다니. 인간의 아이여, 하지만 너는 그 행동의 의미를 알고 있느냐… 컥.<end>"

## 1840139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　よくやりました…　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<end>"
EN: "Well done... Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘했습니다… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 1840140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よくぞ、巨人ゲヘナを倒しました。　　　<line-break>　　　　　ありがとうございます。<line-break>　　　お礼に、私が持つ最高の水の力を<line-break>　　　　　あなたに授けましょう。<line-break>　　　　　あなたの魂が魔道を極め、<line-break>　　内なる水が果てなく、湧き出るならば<line-break>　　　　　　このにごりなき水は、<line-break>　　　　きっとあなたを助けるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "You have done well to defeat Gehenna the Giant. You have my thanks. In gratitude, I shall bestow upon you the greatest of my powers over water. If your soul masters the ways of magic, and the water within you wells forth without end, then this clear, unsullied water shall surely aid you.<end>"
KO: "거인 게헤나를 훌륭히 쓰러뜨렸군요. 감사합니다. 답례로 제가 지닌 최고의 물의 힘을 당신에게 드리겠습니다. 당신의 혼이 마도를 극한까지 닦고 내면의 물이 끝없이 솟는다면, 이 흐림 없는 물은 분명 당신을 도울 것입니다.<end>"

## 1840141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "水の最高位の魔法が解放された。<end>"
EN: "The highest-level water spell has been unlocked.<end>"
KO: "물의 최고위 마법이 해방되었다.<end>"

## 1840142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　さようなら　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　<value:$28>、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, <value:$28>, Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "안녕히…. <value:$28>, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 1840143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　<value:$28>、<line-break>　　　　　よく戻ってきてくれました。<line-break>　　　ゲヘナを倒しに行ってくれるのですね？<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad you've come back, <value:$28>. You're going to defeat Gehenna, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 잘 돌아와 주셨습니다. 게헤나를 쓰러뜨리러 가 주시는 거군요?<end>"

## 1840144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec184-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度はがんばります<end>"
EN: "I'll do my best this time.<end>"
KO: "다음엔 열심히 할게요<end>"

## 1840145

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1850001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちっと耳に入れときたいことがあってな。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. There's something I thought you ought to know.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 네 귀에 넣어 둘 이야기가 좀 있어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1850002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、仕事の話じゃないんだ。<line-break>リベルダムが落ちたって噂、聞いてるか？<end>"
EN: "No, it's not about a job. Have you heard the rumor that Liberdam has fallen?<end>"
KO: "아니, 일 얘기는 아니야. 리벨덤이 함락됐다는 소문, 들었어?<end>"

## 1850003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚くのも無理はねぇ。<line-break>俺も信じられないからな。<line-break>なんでも一夜でカルラが落としたそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Can't blame you for being surprised. I can hardly believe it myself. They say Karula took the place in a single night.<end>"
KO: "놀랄 만도 하지. 나도 믿기 힘드니까. 듣자 하니 카를라가 하룻밤 만에 함락시켰대.<end>"

## 1850004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、自分の目で確かめてみるのが、　　　<line-break>一番いいだろうな。<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムに知り合いがいるなら、<line-break>なおさらな。<end>"
EN: "Best see for yourself, I reckon. Especially if you know anyone in Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 직접 눈으로 확인해 보는 게 제일 좋겠지. 리벨덤에 아는 사람이 있다면 더더욱.<end>"

## 1850005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　大陸の商業と文化の中心地、リベルダム。　<line-break>　　　　自由都市とも呼ばれるこの都市は　　　<line-break>　　　これまで、大国の力に屈することなく　　<line-break>　　　常に自治と中立の姿勢を貫いてきた。　　<end>"
EN: "Liberdam, the continent's center of commerce and culture. Also known as the Free City, it had always upheld its autonomy and neutrality, never yielding to the power of great nations.<end>"
KO: "대륙 상업과 문화의 중심지 리벨덤. 자유도시라고도 불리는 이 도시는 지금까지 강대국의 힘에 굴복하지 않고 언제나 자치와 중립을 지켜 왔다.<end>"

## 1850006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、その誇り高き自由の歴史は、　　<line-break>ディンガル帝国と内通した商人　　　　<line-break>アンティノ・マモンの裏切りにより、　　<line-break>内部から終止符が打たれることとなった。　<end>"
EN: "But that proud history of freedom came to an end from within through the betrayal of Antino Mammon, a merchant who had conspired with the Dyneskal Empire.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 긍지 높은 자유의 역사는 딩갈 제국과 내통한 상인 안티노 마몬의 배신으로 인해 내부에서 종지부를 찍게 되었다.<end>"

## 1850007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンティノの手引きで<line-break>リベルダムを攻めた青竜将軍カルラは、<line-break>反乱分子の掃討を名目として、<line-break>一夜のうちに市内を徹底的に破壊した。<end>"
EN: "With Antino's aid, Azure Dragon General Karula attacked Liberdam and, under the pretext of purging the insurgents, laid waste to the city in a single night.<end>"
KO: "안티노의 안내로 리벨덤을 공격한 청룡장군 칼라는 반란분자 소탕을 명목으로 하룻밤 만에 시내를 철저히 파괴했다.<end>"

## 1850008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、反乱分子の<line-break>指導者と目される商家の娘、<line-break>クリュセイス・クロイスは見つからず、<line-break>内通者アンティノの消息も不明であった。<end>"
EN: "Yet Khryuseis Krois, who was the daughter of a merchant family and was believed to be the insurgents' leader, was nowhere to be found, and the whereabouts of Antino, the collaborator, were also unknown.<end>"
KO: "그러나 반란분자의 지도자로 지목된 상가의 딸 크류세이스 크로이스는 발견되지 않았고, 내통자 안티노의 행방도 알 수 없었다.<end>"

## 1850009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　結果、リベルダムの繁栄は失われ、　　　<line-break>　　　　　そこに残されたものは　　　　　　　<line-break>　かつて美しい町並みであったはずのガレキと　<line-break>　　　力なき人々の泣き声だけであった…。　　<end>"
EN: "Thus Liberdam's prosperity was lost. All that remained was the rubble of what had once been a beautiful cityscape and the cries of helpless people...<end>"
KO: "그 결과 리벨덤의 번영은 사라지고, 남겨진 것은 한때 아름다운 거리였을 잔해와 힘없는 사람들의 울음소리뿐이었다….<end>"

## 1850010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶対、あそこにいるわね。<line-break>クリュセイスの奴、<line-break>間違いなくフゴーを頼ってるわ。<end>"
EN: "She's definitely there. Khryuseis must have turned to Hugo for help.<end>"
KO: "분명 거기 있을 거야. 크류세이스 녀석, 틀림없이 휴고에게 의지하고 있어.<end>"

## 1850011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、フゴーに手を出すと、<line-break>ディンガルの国家財政にまで<line-break>影響が及びます。<line-break>下手なマネはできません。<line-break>これ以上は刺激しない方が…。<end>"
EN: "But if we move against Hugo, it could affect the Dyneskal treasury itself. We cannot afford to act rashly. It may be best not to provoke him any further.<end>"
KO: "하지만 휴고에게 손대면 딘갈의 국가 재정에까지 영향이 갑니다. 서투른 짓은 할 수 없습니다. 더 자극하지 않는 편이….<end>"

## 1850012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴーって、<line-break>そんなにすごいんですか？<end>"
EN: "Is Hugo really that powerful?<end>"
KO: "휴고라는 게 그렇게 대단한가요?<end>"

## 1850013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー、まぁね。<line-break>この大陸の経済を握ってるの、<line-break>フゴーだからね。<line-break>あいつを敵に回しちゃうと、<line-break>税収が激減するのよ。<end>"
EN: "Mm, you could say that. Hugo controls the economy of this entire continent. If we make an enemy of him, our tax revenues will plummet.<end>"
KO: "음, 뭐 그렇지. 이 대륙 경제를 쥐고 있는 건 휴고니까. 그자를 적으로 돌리면 세수가 크게 줄어들어.<end>"

## 1850014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美術館壊しちゃって、<line-break>ちょっとやばかったもんね。<line-break>追い打ちかけちゃまずいわね。<end>"
EN: "Wrecking the museum already put us in a tight spot. We'd better not make things any worse.<end>"
KO: "미술관을 부숴 버려서 좀 위험했잖아. 여기서 더 몰아붙이면 안 되겠네.<end>"

## 1850015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美術品の運び出しを手伝って、<line-break>やっと機嫌を取り戻せたことですし。<end>"
EN: "It took helping move the artwork out just to get back into his good graces.<end>"
KO: "미술품 운반을 도와서 겨우 기분을 풀어 드렸으니까요.<end>"

## 1850016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かといって、クリュセイスの<line-break>居場所を確認しないで<line-break>放っておくわけにもいかないし。<end>"
EN: "Still, we can't just let this go without confirming where Khryuseis is.<end>"
KO: "그렇다고 크류세이스가 어디 있는지도 확인하지 않고 내버려 둘 순 없고.<end>"

## 1850017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンティノも逃がすし、<line-break>なんか手際悪いわね。<end>"
EN: "We let Antino get away too. We're not handling this very well.<end>"
KO: "안티노도 놓치고, 왠지 일처리가 서툴네.<end>"

## 1850018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<line-break>私の指揮がいたらないばかりに…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. This is all because I failed to lead properly...<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다. 제 지휘가 미숙했던 탓에….<end>"

## 1850019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "落ち込んでる暇があったら、<line-break>抜かりがないか、<line-break>もう一度確認してきなさいな。<end>"
EN: "If you have time to mope, go back and make sure nothing's been overlooked.<end>"
KO: "우울해할 시간 있으면 빠뜨린 건 없는지 다시 한번 확인하고 와.<end>"

## 1850020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はいっ！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes!<end>"
KO: "네, 네!<end>"

## 1850021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>ちょーどいいところに来た。<line-break>あんたフゴーと付き合いある？<end>"
EN: "Oh, hey, <value:$28>. Perfect timing. You know Hugo, right?<end>"
KO: "어, <value:$28>잖아. 딱 잘 왔다. 너 휴고랑 아는 사이야?<end>"

## 1850022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラッキー！<line-break>さすがは<value:$28>。<line-break>頼りになるじゃん。<end>"
EN: "Lucky me! I knew I could count on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "럭키! 역시 <value:$28>. 믿음직하잖아.<end>"

## 1850023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でさ、ちょっと、<line-break>フゴーのところまで<line-break>行ってきてくんない？<line-break>クリュセイスのこと<line-break>聞いてきてほしいんだけど。<end>"
EN: "So, could you pay Hugo a little visit? I want you to ask him about Khryuseis.<end>"
KO: "그래서 말인데, 휴고에게 좀 다녀와 줄래? 크류세이스에 대해 물어봐 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1850024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "情報がほしいだけ。<line-break>フゴーがらみなら、<line-break>もう捜索は打ち切らなきゃ。<line-break>手が出せないからね。<line-break>でも、そうじゃないなら…、<line-break>ま、そんときはそんとき。<end>"
EN: "All I want is information. If Hugo's involved, we'll have to call off the search. We can't touch him. But if he isn't... well, we'll deal with that when the time comes.<end>"
KO: "정보가 필요할 뿐이야. 휴고와 관련된 거라면, 수색은 이제 중단해야 해. 손댈 수 없으니까. 하지만 그렇지 않다면…뭐, 그땐 그때지.<end>"

## 1850025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら？　あんた冒険者？<line-break>ちょうどよかった、<line-break>ちょっと頼みたいことがあるのよ。<line-break>ここに来られたってことは、<line-break>フゴーと付き合いがある<line-break>ってことよね？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Are you an adventurer? Perfect. I've got a little favor to ask. If you managed to get in here, that means you know Hugo, right?<end>"
KO: "어머? 너 모험자야? 마침 잘됐다. 부탁할 게 있어. 여기까지 왔다는 건 휴고와 아는 사이라는 거지?<end>"

## 1850026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしは、カルラ。　　　　　　　<line-break>ディンガル帝国の将軍。<line-break>　<line-break>で、リベルダムを落として、<line-break>今その後片付けの最中なんだけど、<line-break>片づかないことがひとつあってね。<end>"
EN: "I'm Karula, a general of the Dyneskal Empire. We took Liberdam, and now we're cleaning up afterward. There's just one loose end we haven't been able to tie up.<end>"
KO: "난 카를라. 딘갈 제국의 장군이야. 그래서 리벨덤을 함락시키고 지금 뒤처리 중인데, 정리되지 않은 일이 하나 있거든.<end>"

## 1850027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスっていう娘が<line-break>見あたらないのよ。<line-break>解放軍の首謀者なんだけどさ、<line-break>そいつがどうもこの上にある<line-break>フゴー邸に<line-break>かくまわれてるっぽいのよね。<end>"
EN: "A girl named Khryuseis is missing. She's the ringleader of the Liberation Army, and it looks like Hugo may be sheltering her at his estate up above.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스라는 아이가 안 보여. 해방군의 주모자인데, 아무래도 이 위에 있는 휴고 저택에 숨겨져 있는 것 같아.<end>"

## 1850028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、フゴーのところに行ってさ、<line-break>クリュセイスのことについて、<line-break>探りを入れてきてくれない？<line-break>あたしらは、ちょっと<line-break>行けない事情があるのよ。<line-break>頼まれてくれないかなぁ？<end>"
EN: "So, could you go see Hugo and quietly find out what he knows about Khryuseis? There are reasons we can't go ourselves. How about doing us this favor?<end>"
KO: "그러니까 휴고한테 가서 크류세이스에 대해 좀 캐봐 주지 않을래? 우리는 조금 갈 수 없는 사정이 있거든. 부탁 좀 들어줄래?<end>"

## 1850029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう、引き受けてくれる？<line-break>カルラ軍とは、関係ないふりして、<line-break>聞き出してきて。<line-break>成功すれば、<line-break>あたしが自腹で報酬出すわよ。<end>"
EN: "Well? Will you take the job? Pretend you have nothing to do with Karula's Army and get the truth out of him. If you succeed, I'll pay you out of my own pocket.<end>"
KO: "어때, 맡아 줄래? 칼라군과 관계없는 척하고 알아내 와. 성공하면 내가 사비로 보수를 줄게.<end>"

## 1850030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくっちゃね。<line-break>じゃ、よろしくー。<end>"
EN: "That's what I wanted to hear. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그래야지. 그럼 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1850031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、まだ行ってないの？<line-break>あんましぐずぐずしてるのは、<line-break>みっともないんじゃない？<end>"
EN: "Oh, you still haven't gone? Dragging your feet like this is a little pathetic, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "어머, 아직 안 갔어? 너무 꾸물거리는 건 보기 안 좋지 않아?<end>"

## 1850033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。<line-break>やるわね、クリュセイス。　<line-break>したたかじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Nicely done, Khryuseis. You're a shrewd one, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "흐음. 제법이네, 크류세이스. 만만치 않잖아.<end>"

## 1850034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、フゴーがらみじゃ、　　<line-break>しゃーないわね。<line-break>よし、捜索は打ち切り！<line-break>最後に、時計塔を壊して、<line-break>リベルダム占領作戦は終了！<end>"
EN: "Well, if Hugo's involved, I suppose it can't be helped. All right, call off the search! We'll finish by destroying the clock tower, and then the operation to occupy Liberdam is over!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 휴고가 얽혔다면 어쩔 수 없지. 좋아, 수색은 중단! 마지막으로 시계탑을 부수고 리벨덤 점령 작전은 종료!<end>"

## 1850035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほい、これ報酬ね。<end>"
EN: "Here you go. Your reward.<end>"
KO: "자, 이게 보상이야.<end>"

## 1850036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伝令部隊は、各地に<line-break>リベルダム占領の報を<line-break>飛ばしなさい！！<end>"
EN: "Messenger units, spread word of Liberdam's occupation throughout the land!<end>"
KO: "전령 부대는 각지에 리벨덤 점령 소식을 전해라!!<end>"

## 1850037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、あんた、あたしの部下にならない？<line-break>すごく見込みありそうだからさ。<line-break>　<line-break>普段は勝手なことしてていいよ。<line-break>ただ、あたしがでかいことする時には、<line-break>力貸してくれないかな？<end>"
EN: "Hey, how about working for me? You seem to have a lot of potential. Most of the time, you can do whatever you like. But when I do something big, would you lend me a hand?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 너 내 부하 안 될래? 엄청 가능성이 있어 보여서 말이야. 평소엔 마음대로 해도 돼. 다만 내가 큰일을 벌일 때 힘을 빌려주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1850038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を貸す<end>"
EN: "Agree to help<end>"
KO: "힘을 빌려준다<end>"

## 1850039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておく<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "그만둔다<end>"

## 1850040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、交渉成立！<line-break>そんじゃ、次の作戦の時まで<line-break>時間の無駄かな…。<line-break>暇だったら、会いに来てよ。<line-break>ロセンの王宮、本拠地にしてるからさ。<end>"
EN: "Good, we have a deal! Well then, I guess you'd only be wasting your time hanging around until the next operation... Come see me if you're free. I'm based at Rocen's royal palace.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 협상 성립! 그럼 다음 작전까지 시간 낭비인가… 한가하면 만나러 와. 로센 왕궁을 본거지로 삼고 있으니까.<end>"

## 1850041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだったのー！？<line-break>あーあ、がっくり。<end>"
EN: "It didn't work out!? Aw, what a letdown.<end>"
KO: "안 된 거야!? 아아, 실망이네.<end>"

## 1850042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、しゃーないか。<line-break>これ以上捜すのは時間の無駄かな…。<line-break>　<line-break>よし、捜索は打ち切り！<line-break>最後に、時計塔を壊して、<line-break>リベルダム占領作戦は終了！<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it can't be helped. Searching any longer would just be a waste of time. All right, call off the search! We'll finish by destroying the clock tower, and then the operation to occupy Liberdam is over!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 어쩔 수 없지. 더 찾는 건 시간 낭비겠네…. 좋아, 수색 중지! 마지막으로 시계탑을 부수고 리벨덤 점령 작전은 종료!<end>"

## 1850043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとちょっと、<line-break>報告してくれなきゃ。<line-break>冒険者の基本でしょ？<end>"
EN: "Come on, you have to report back. That's basic adventuring, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 잠깐. 보고는 해 줘야지. 모험자의 기본 아니야?<end>"

## 1850044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はもうお引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid you'll have to leave for today.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이제 돌아가 주세요.<end>"

## 1850045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、<value:$28>様。<line-break>お入りください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 들어오세요.<end>"

## 1850046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>様。<line-break>このような時に来られるとは。<line-break>どのようなご用ですかな？<end>"
EN: "Why, <value:$28>. I did not expect you at a time like this. What brings you here?<end>"
KO: "이런, <value:$28>님. 이런 때 오시다니. 무슨 용건이신지요?<end>"

## 1850047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かな、何かな。<end>"
EN: "What is it, then? What is it?<end>"
KO: "뭘까, 뭘까.<end>"

## 1850048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ…。<line-break>クリュセイス・クロイスの<line-break>ことについてですか。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Is this about Khryuseis Krois?<end>"
KO: "흠…. 크류세이스 크로이스에 관한 이야기입니까.<end>"

## 1850049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんじゃ…。<line-break>お宝を持ってきたんじゃ<line-break>ないんじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "What...? So you haven't brought us any treasure.<end>"
KO: "뭐냐… 보물을 가져온 건 아니구먼.<end>"

## 1850050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまらんのぉ。<end>"
EN: "How dull.<end>"
KO: "재미없구먼.<end>"

## 1850051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーほっほっほ。<line-break>そーですわねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호. 그렇군요오.<end>"

## 1850052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたには、貴重な品を<line-break>お持ちいただいたご恩もあります。<line-break>　<line-break>事の次第によっては、<line-break>情報をお教えしましょう。<end>"
EN: "We are indebted to you for the valuable things you have brought us. Depending on the circumstances, we may be willing to tell you what we know.<end>"
KO: "당신에게는 귀중한 물건을 가져와 주신 은혜도 있습니다. 사정에 따라서는 정보를 알려 드리죠.<end>"

## 1850053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信用できる人物なら<line-break>話すんじゃがのぉ。<end>"
EN: "If you can be trusted, we might tell you.<end>"
KO: "믿을 만한 사람이라면 말해 줄 텐데 말이지.<end>"

## 1850054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の質問にお答えください。<line-break>どなたに頼まれたのですか？<line-break>正直にお答えください。<end>"
EN: "Please answer my question. Who sent you? Answer honestly.<end>"
KO: "제 질문에 답해 주세요. 누구의 부탁을 받았습니까? 솔직하게 답해 주세요.<end>"

## 1850055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラに頼まれた<end>"
EN: "Karula asked me to do it.<end>"
KO: "카를라에게 부탁받았다<end>"

## 1850056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼人の情報は話せない<end>"
EN: "I can't reveal anything about my client.<end>"
KO: "의뢰인 정보는 말할 수 없어.<end>"

## 1850057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむー。<line-break>話せないのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Then we can't tell you, eh?<end>"
KO: "흐음. 말할 수가 없구나.<end>"

## 1850058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼人がカルラだからでは　　<line-break>ありません。<line-break>　<line-break>我々は、信用できる人物にしか<line-break>情報を流さないのです。<end>"
EN: "This is not because Karula is your client. We share information only with those we can trust.<end>"
KO: "의뢰인이 카를라이기 때문이 아닙니다. 우리는 믿을 수 있는 인물에게만 정보를 흘립니다.<end>"

## 1850059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "依頼人の名を<line-break>簡単に明かすような方には、<line-break>お話いたしかねます。<end>"
EN: "We cannot share what we know with someone who would reveal a client's name so readily.<end>"
KO: "의뢰인의 이름을 쉽게 밝히는 분께는 말씀드리기 어렵습니다.<end>"

## 1850060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "Please leave.<end>"
KO: "돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1850061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむー。<line-break>見込みがあるのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. You may have promise after all.<end>"
KO: "흠~. 가능성이 있구나.<end>"

## 1850062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしいでしょう。<line-break>依頼人の名を簡単に明かさない。<line-break>そうでなくてはいけません。<end>"
EN: "Very well. A client's name must not be revealed lightly. That is as it should be.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 의뢰인의 이름을 쉽게 밝히지 않는다. 그래야지요.<end>"

## 1850063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとは、<line-break>情報を守るだけの力が<line-break>あるかどうかじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Now we must see whether you're strong enough to protect that information.<end>"
KO: "이제 남은 건 정보를 지킬 힘이 있는지 여부구먼.<end>"

## 1850064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、そのことも、<line-break>証明していただけますかな？<line-break>我々を相手に。<end>"
EN: "Now then, can you prove that as well? By taking us on?<end>"
KO: "자, 그것도 증명해 주시겠습니까? 우리를 상대로.<end>"

## 1850065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むにゅー…。<end>"
EN: "Mnyuh...<end>"
KO: "우뉴우….<end>"

## 1850066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>むにゅー、ですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Mnyuh indeed.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 말랑말랑하군요~.<end>"

## 1850068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムには、<line-break>すでにいらっしゃいません。<end>"
EN: "She is no longer in Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "리벨덤에는 이미 계시지 않습니다.<end>"

## 1850069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勝った。勝った。<end>"
EN: "We won! We won!<end>"
KO: "이겼어. 이겼어.<end>"

## 1850070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まことに申しわけありませんが、<line-break>情報を流すには、力不足のようです。<line-break>お引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "I am truly sorry, but you do not appear strong enough for us to entrust you with this information. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "정말 죄송하지만 정보를 흘리기에는 힘이 부족한 듯합니다. 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1850071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者か？　名を名乗れ。<end>"
EN: "An adventurer? State your name.<end>"
KO: "모험자인가? 이름을 대라.<end>"

## 1850072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えーと、<value:$28>…。<line-break>フゴー邸に出入りのある者です。<end>"
EN: "Let's see... <value:$28>. You have access to Hugo's residence.<end>"
KO: "음, <value:$28>…. 휴고 저택에 출입하는 사람입니다.<end>"

## 1850073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、通れ。本来、今は中に入れないが、<line-break>カルラ様がフゴー邸に出入りできる人物を<line-break>捜しておいでだ。<line-break>居住区に向かえ。<line-break>急げよ。<end>"
EN: "All right, pass. Ordinarily no one is allowed inside right now, but Lady Karula is looking for someone with access to Hugo's residence. Head to the residential district. Hurry.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 지나가라. 원래 지금은 들어갈 수 없지만 칼라 님께서 휴고 저택에 출입할 수 있는 사람을 찾고 계신다. 주거 구역으로 가라. 서둘러.<end>"

## 1850074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現在、リベルダムは、封鎖中だ。<line-break>立ち去るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Liberdam is under blockade. Leave at once.<end>"
KO: "현재 리벨덤은 봉쇄 중이다. 물러가라.<end>"

## 1850075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様は、<line-break>今はいらっしゃらない。<line-break>日をあらためて、また来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Khryuseis isn't here at present. Come back another day.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님은 지금 계시지 않는다. 날을 바꿔 다시 와라.<end>"

## 1850076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴーと面識があるとは、<line-break>大した冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "You know Hugo personally? You must be quite the adventurer.<end>"
KO: "휴고와 아는 사이라니, 대단한 모험자군.<end>"

## 1850077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様もさすがに<line-break>フゴーには手を出せんよ。<line-break>金の力はすごいな。<end>"
EN: "Even Lady Karula wouldn't dare touch Hugo. Money is a powerful thing.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님도 아무리 그래도 휴고에게는 손대지 못해. 돈의 힘은 대단하네.<end>"

## 1850078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話はつけてきたのか？<line-break>早く頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "Did you get it sorted out? Hurry up, will you?<end>"
KO: "이야기는 끝냈나? 빨리 부탁해.<end>"

## 1850079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec185-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってる？　クリュセイスって娘。<line-break>ここん家の一人娘なんだけどさ、<line-break>解放軍の首謀者やってんだわ。これが。<line-break>で、そのクリュセイスちゃんが<line-break>フゴーの所に逃げこんでるらしいの。<line-break>そこんとこ、知りたいんだなあ。<end>"
EN: "You know a girl named Khryuseis? She's the only daughter of the family here, and wouldn't you know it, she's the ringleader of the Liberation Army. Word is little Khryuseis has run off to Hugo for protection. That's what I want you to find out.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스라는 여자 알아? 이 집 외동딸인데 해방군의 주모자야. 그런데 그 크류세이스가 휴고에게 도망쳐 들어갔다더라고. 그 부분을 알고 싶어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1850084

## 1860001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントさん。<line-break>今日、ノーブルをご出発ですか。　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Mr. Flint, are you leaving Noble today?<end>"
KO: "플린트 씨. 오늘 노블을 떠나시는 겁니까.<end>"

## 1860002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>ここでの商売も終わりましたので、　<line-break>ロストールへ向かうつもりです。<end>"
EN: "Yes. My business here is finished, so I intend to head for Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "네. 여기서 장사도 끝났으니 로스톨로 갈 생각입니다.<end>"

## 1860003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントさん、あなた、<line-break>ノーブルに来る途中でも何者かに<line-break>襲われたって言ってましたよね。<line-break>最近はこのあたりも<line-break>物騒になってきましたから、<line-break>十分に注意してくださいよ。<end>"
EN: "Mr. Flint, you said someone attacked you on your way to Noble, didn't you? Things have been getting dangerous around here lately, so please be very careful.<end>"
KO: "플린트 씨, 노블에 오는 길에도 누군가에게 습격당했다고 하셨죠. 요즘은 이 근처도 흉흉해졌으니 충분히 조심하세요.<end>"

## 1860004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。気をつけておきます。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're right. I'll be careful.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 조심해 두겠습니다.<end>"

## 1860005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれぇー？　フリントさん、<line-break><value:$28>はぁ？<end>"
EN: "Huh? Mr. Flint, where's <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어라? 플린트 씨, <value:$28>은?<end>"

## 1860006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ルルアンタ。<line-break>あの子なら剣の稽古だと言って　　　<line-break>町の外にある林に行ってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, Lulluantha. Out in the woods beyond town, apparently. Said something about practicing with a sword.<end>"
KO: "아, 루루안타. 그 아이라면 검술 연습을 한다며 마을 밖 숲으로 갔어.<end>"

## 1860007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またぁ？<line-break>しょうがないんだからぁ！<end>"
EN: "Again? Honestly!<end>"
KO: "또야? 정말 어쩔 수 없다니까!<end>"

## 1860008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前みたいに、<line-break>変なおじさんたちに襲われたら<line-break>どうするつもりなのかしら。<end>"
EN: "What if those strange men attack again, like last time?<end>"
KO: "지난번처럼 이상한 아저씨들에게 습격당하면 어쩌려고 그러는 걸까.<end>"

## 1860009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあまあ、あのときも<line-break>幸い無事ですんだことだし…。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Now, now. No one was hurt that time, fortunately...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그때도 다행히 무사했으니까….<end>"

## 1860010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、<value:$28>には<line-break>商売を継がせるんでしょお？<line-break>だったら剣の稽古なんて…。<end>"
EN: "But you're going to have <value:$28> take over the business, aren't you? So what's the point of practicing with a sword...?<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>한테 장사를 물려줄 거잖아? 그럼 검술 수련은….<end>"

## 1860011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ。私は、<line-break>あの子はあの子で好きな道を<line-break>進んでくれればいいと思うよ。　　　<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha, I want my child to be free to follow whatever path feels right.<end>"
KO: "루루안타. 난 그 아이는 그 아이대로 자신이 원하는 길을 갔으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1860012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな風に甘い父親だから、　　　　<line-break><value:$28>は<line-break>いつまでもあんな風なの！<line-break>あのときはフリントさんが<line-break>駆けつけたからよかったけど、<line-break>いつも間に合うとは限らないよぉ？<end>"
EN: "You're too soft as a father! That's why <value:$28> never changes! It was lucky you got there in time before, Mr. Flint, but you might not always make it in time, you know?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 무른 아버지니까 <value:$28>는 언제까지고 저런 식인 거야! 그때는 플린트 씨가 달려와서 다행이었지만 언제나 제때 올 수 있는 건 아니잖아?<end>"

## 1860013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けど、あのときのフリントさん、　　<line-break>すごかったね。<line-break>ほんとうは強いんだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, you were amazing that time, Mr. Flint. I never knew you were so strong.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그때 플린트 씨, 대단했지. 사실은 강한 사람이었어.<end>"

## 1860014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのときは、<value:$28>を<line-break>守るので必死だったからね。<line-break>親というものはそんなものだよ。　　<end>"
EN: "I was desperate to protect <value:$28>. That's what being a parent is.<end>"
KO: "그때는 <value:$28>을 지키느라 필사적이었으니까. 부모란 그런 거야.<end>"

## 1860015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうなんだ。<line-break>ルルアンタ、お父さんもお母さんも<line-break>いないから、よくわかんないや。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Lulluantha doesn't have a mother or a father, so I don't really understand.<end>"
KO: "…그렇구나. 루루안타는 아빠도 엄마도 없어서 잘 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1860016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そういうものなんだよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だから、ルルアンタが大変なときは<line-break>私は、命がけでルルアンタを<line-break>助けにいくよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's what it's like. So if you're ever in trouble, Lulluantha, I'll risk my life to come and save you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그런 거야. 그래서 루루안타가 위험할 때는 목숨 걸고 루루안타를 구하러 갈 거야.<end>"

## 1860017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、今は、<line-break>私がルルアンタのお父さん、　　　　<line-break>なんだからね。<end>"
EN: "Because I'm your father now, Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "왜냐하면 지금은 내가 루루안타의 아버지니까.<end>"

## 1860018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うれしいな、フリントさん。<end>"
EN: "That... makes me happy, Mr. Flint.<end>"
KO: "…기쁘네, 플린트 씨.<end>"

## 1860019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。えっと、えっと！<line-break>じゃ、じゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break><value:$28>を探してくるね！<end>"
EN: "Oh... um, um! Th-then Lulluantha will go find <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아…. 저기, 저기! 그럼 루루안타가 <value:$28>을 찾아올게!<end>"

## 1860020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタが、私たちと<line-break>一緒に行動するようになって１０年か。<line-break>…あっという間だったな。<line-break>あの子のほほえみは、<line-break>母親を亡くした幼い<value:$28>を<line-break>ずいぶんと元気づけてくれた。<end>"
EN: "It's been ten years since Lulluantha joined us. It went by so quickly. Her smile did so much to cheer up little <value:$28>, whose mother had died.<end>"
KO: "루루안타가 우리와 함께 다니게 된 지도 10년인가. …순식간이었군. 그 아이의 미소는 어머니를 잃은 어린 <value:$28>에게 큰 힘이 되어 줬어.<end>"

## 1860021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして、私もな。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "And me as well.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 나도 말이지.<end>"

## 1860022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>。<line-break>戻ったか。<line-break>ルルアンタは一緒じゃないのか？　　<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You're back. Isn't Lulluantha with you?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>. 돌아왔구나. 루루안타는 같이 안 왔어?<end>"

## 1860023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break>いや、今お前を捜しに行ったんだ。　<line-break>すれ違ってしまったみたいだね。<line-break>そろそろ、ロストールへ出発だ。<line-break>悪いが、<value:$28>、<line-break>ルルアンタを捜してきてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "I see. She just went out looking for you, so you must have passed each other. It's nearly time to leave for Rostorl. Sorry to ask, <value:$28>, but could you go and find Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "그래. 아니, 방금 널 찾으러 갔었어. 엇갈린 모양이네. 이제 로스톨로 출발할 때야. 미안하지만 <value:$28>, 루루안타를 찾아와 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1860024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを持っていきなさい。　　　　　<line-break>十分、注意するんだよ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Take this with you, and be very careful.<end>"
KO: "이걸 가져가렴. 충분히 조심하고.<end>"

## 1860025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前がノーブルにいる間中行っていた<line-break>町の外の林に行ったはずだ。<line-break>頼んだよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "She must have gone to the woods outside town where you spent so much time while you were in Noble. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네가 노블에 있는 동안 계속 가던 마을 밖 숲으로 갔을 거야. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1860026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは、<line-break>町の外の林に向かったはずだ。<line-break>頼んだよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha should be in the woods outside town. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 마을 밖 숲으로 향했을 거야. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1860027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "오오, 플린트 씨네 <value:$28>구나. 오늘 출발하나?<end>"

## 1860028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつも一緒のおチビちゃんが<line-break>お前を捜しとった。<line-break>町の外の林の方に行ったぞ。<end>"
EN: "That little one who's always with you was looking for you. Headed toward the woods outside town.<end>"
KO: "늘 같이 다니는 꼬마가 널 찾고 있었어. 마을 밖 숲 쪽으로 갔다.<end>"

## 1860029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、おチビちゃんを捜しに<line-break>行っとったんじゃないのかの？<line-break>早く行ってやりなさい。<end>"
EN: "Well now, weren't you off looking for the little one? Hurry along, then.<end>"
KO: "어라, 꼬마를 찾으러 간 거 아니었나? 어서 가 봐.<end>"

## 1860030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ、<value:$28>！<line-break>もう、どこ行ってたのよぉ！<line-break>捜したんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>! Where have you been? I've been looking everywhere for you!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>! 대체 어디 갔던 거야! 찾았잖아.<end>"

## 1860031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！？　すれちがっちゃったの？<line-break>それで、<value:$28>は<line-break>わざわざ、捜しにきてくれたんだ…。<end>"
EN: "What!? We missed each other? So you came all this way looking for me, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "뭐!? 엇갈린 거야? 그래서 <value:$28>이 일부러 찾으러 와 준 거구나….<end>"

## 1860032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゴメンね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…미안해. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1860033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがと。　　　　　　　　<line-break>やさしいね。<line-break><value:$28>って。<line-break>じゃ、行こ！<line-break>早く戻らないとフリントさん、<line-break>心配してるよぉ！<end>"

## 1860034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml

## 1860037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けっ。威勢のいいガキだぜ。<line-break>まぁいい。お前もフリントへの　　　　<line-break>人質に使えそうだからなあ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Feisty little brat, aren't you? No matter. You could be useful as another hostage against Flint.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 기세 좋은 꼬맹이군. 뭐 됐어. 너도 플린트에게 쓸 인질이 될 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1860038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、人質ぃ！？<end>"

## 1860039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前らには、フリントから<line-break>密書を奪うための人質になってもらう！<line-break>おとなしく捕まっちまいな！<end>"
EN: "I'll use the two of you as hostages to get the Secret Letter from Flint! Now come quietly!<end>"
KO: "너희는 플린트에게서 밀서를 빼앗기 위한 인질이 되어 줘야겠다! 얌전히 잡혀!<end>"

## 1860040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、人が気持ちよく<line-break>昼寝してるってのに、<line-break>ぎゃあぎゃあ騒がないでくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on. A man can't even enjoy a nice nap without you lot making a racket.<end>"
KO: "어이, 사람이 기분 좋게 낮잠 자고 있는데 시끄럽게 떠들지 좀 마.<end>"

## 1860041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大の大人が子供を相手に刃物かい？<line-break>見られたもんじゃねえなぁ。<end>"
EN: "A grown man pulling a blade on children? That's a sorry sight.<end>"
KO: "다 큰 어른이 아이를 상대로 칼을 드나? 보기 좋진 않군.<end>"

## 1860042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Who the hell are you!? Stay out of this, or you'll get hurt!<end>"
KO: "넌 또 뭐야!? 방해하면 아픈 꼴을 볼 줄 알아!<end>"

## 1860044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう一度かかってくるかい？<line-break>今度はもうちょっと加減してやるぜ？<end>"
EN: "Want another go? I'll hold back a little more next time.<end>"
KO: "한 번 더 덤빌래? 이번엔 조금 더 봐주지.<end>"

## 1860046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ。こんな奴がいるなんて<line-break>聞いてないぜ。ちくしょお！<end>"
EN: "Tch. Nobody told me there'd be someone like this around. Damn it!<end>"
KO: "쳇. 이런 녀석이 있다는 얘긴 못 들었는데. 젠장!<end>"

## 1860047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてと。<line-break>今の奴とお友達とは思えないが？<end>"
EN: "Well, then. I take it you weren't friends with that fellow?<end>"
KO: "자. 방금 녀석과 친구로는 안 보이는데?<end>"

## 1860048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も知らないよぉー、<line-break>あんなおじさん。<line-break>　<line-break>お前がフリントの子かって<line-break><value:$28>に<line-break>近づいてきただけだもん。<end>"
EN: "I don't know that guy either! He just walked up to <value:$28> and asked, “Are you Flint's child?”<end>"
KO: "나도 몰라~ 그런 아저씨. 네가 플린트의 아이냐고 하면서 <value:$28>에게 다가왔을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1860049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フリント？<end>"
EN: "Flint...?<end>"
KO: "…플린트?<end>"

## 1860050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>のお父さんだよ。<line-break>ね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Flint is <value:$28>'s father. Right, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1860051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどね。　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Ah, that explains it.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나.<end>"

## 1860052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気になるな。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、今の奴は当分戻ってこないだろうが<line-break>気をつけて帰るんだぜ。じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Something about this bothers me. Well, that thug won't be coming back anytime soon, but watch yourselves on the way home. See you around.<end>"
KO: "…신경 쓰이네. 뭐, 지금 녀석은 당분간 돌아오지 않겠지만 조심해서 돌아가. 그럼.<end>"

## 1860053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、ありがとぉ！<end>"
EN: "Okay! Thanks!<end>"
KO: "응, 고마워어!<end>"

## 1860054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>はやく戻ろうよ。<line-break>フリントさんが心配するよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>. Let's hurry back. Flint will be worried.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>. 빨리 돌아가자. 플린트 씨가 걱정할 거야.<end>"

## 1860055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、早く宿屋に戻らないと。<line-break>ルルアンタ、先に行ってるね。<end>"
EN: "Come on, we'd better get back to the inn. Lulluantha's going on ahead, okay?<end>"
KO: "자, 빨리 여관으로 돌아가야지. 루루안타, 먼저 가 있을게.<end>"

## 1860056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！<line-break><value:$28>ったら<line-break>ダメじゃない！　　　　　　　<line-break>早く宿屋に行かないと、<line-break>フリントさんが心配するよぉ。<line-break>ほら、早くぅ。<end>"
EN: "Honestly! <value:$28>, you can't do that! We have to hurry to the inn, or Flint will worry. Come on, hurry!<end>"
KO: "정말! <value:$28>, 이러면 안 되잖아! 빨리 여관에 가야 해. 플린트 씨가 걱정할 거야. 자, 빨리.<end>"

## 1860057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたが無事で何よりだ。<line-break>…この町で、何をやっていたのか<line-break>聞きたいところだが…。<end>"
EN: "I'm just glad you're safe. Though I would like to know what you were doing in this town...<end>"
KO: "네가 무사해서 다행이야. …이 도시에서 뭘 하고 있었는지 묻고 싶긴 하지만….<end>"

## 1860058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえ、あなたであっても、<line-break>それは申し上げられません…。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Even to you, I cannot disclose that...<end>"
KO: "설령 당신이라 해도, 그건 말씀드릴 수 없습니다….<end>"

## 1860059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いいさ。察しはついてる。<line-break>だが、感心しねぇな。<end>"
EN: "Fine. I've got a fair idea anyway. Can't say I approve, though.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 짐작은 가. 하지만 감탄할 일은 아니군.<end>"

## 1860060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…言葉もございません。<line-break>この町の住民には<line-break>申しわけなく思っております…。　<end>"
EN: "I have no excuse. I am deeply sorry for what happened to the people of this town...<end>"
KO: "…드릴 말씀이 없습니다. 이 마을 주민들에게 죄송하게 생각하고 있습니다….<end>"

## 1860061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あんたを止めたところで<line-break>他の奴がやるだけだからな。<line-break>俺は根を断つように動いてみるさ。<end>"
EN: "Well, even if I stopped you, someone else would just do it. I'll see what I can do about cutting it off at the root.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 널 막아 봐야 다른 녀석이 할 뿐이니까. 난 뿌리를 끊는 쪽으로 움직여 보지.<end>"

## 1860062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、今度のお務めが終わったら、<line-break>お暇をいただこうかと<line-break>考えております。<end>"
EN: "I am considering asking to leave once my next duty is complete.<end>"
KO: "저는 이번 임무가 끝나면 휴가를 받을까 생각하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1860063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。<line-break>それがいいんだろうが…。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたは得難い存在だろうからな。<line-break>そう簡単に手放してくれるかどうか。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. That's probably for the best, but... someone like you is hard to replace. I doubt they'll let you go so easily.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그게 좋겠지만…. 너는 귀한 존재일 테니까 그렇게 쉽게 놓아줄지 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1860064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントさん、ただいまー。<end>"
EN: "Mr. Flint, we're back!<end>"
KO: "플린트 씨, 다녀왔어요.<end>"

## 1860065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아ー, 그 사람!<end>"

## 1860066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、フリントさん、　　　　　　　<line-break>聞いて、聞いて！<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタたち、変なおじさんたちに<line-break>襲われたんだけど、このお兄さんが<line-break>助けてくれたんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Mr. Flint, listen, listen! Some strange men attacked us, but this man saved us!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 플린트 씨, 들어 봐, 들어 봐! 릴비들이 이상한 아저씨들한테 습격당했는데 이 오빠가 구해 줬어.<end>"

## 1860068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Xenetes, I hear you saved both of them. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨, 두 사람을 도와주셨다면서요. 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1860072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、なに。<line-break>大したことはしてないさ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、俺はそろそろ行くとするか。<end>"
EN: "It was nothing. I didn't do much. Well, I'd better be going.<end>"
KO: "아니, 별거 아냐. 대단한 일도 아니었어. 자, 난 슬슬 가 볼까.<end>"

## 1860073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えぇ！？　もう行っちゃうのぉ？<end>"
EN: "What? You're leaving already?<end>"

## 1860074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml

## 1860076

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Now, it's time for us to leave as well. We're bound for the royal capital, Rostorl.<end>"

## 1860079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、なに、この短剣を<line-break>荷に積むのを忘れていたのでね。　<line-break>お前が持っていてくれないか？<line-break>ロストールのお客様に渡さねば<line-break>ならない大切なものなのだ。<line-break>肌身放さず持っていてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I forgot to pack this dagger with the rest of the luggage. Would you carry it for me? It's important, and I must deliver it to a customer in Rostorl. Keep it on you at all times.<end>"
KO: "아니, 이 단검을 짐에 싣는 걸 잊어서 말이야. 네가 가지고 있어 주지 않겠나? 로스톨의 손님에게 전해야 할 중요한 물건이야. 몸에서 떼지 말고 가지고 있어 줘.<end>"

## 1860082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの短剣を手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained Flint's Dagger.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 단검을 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1860083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Mr. Flint! Everything's ready! Hurry, hurry!<end>"
KO: "플린트 씨! 준비됐어. 빨리 빨리!<end>"

## 1860084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Well then, shall we go, <value:$28>? I'm counting on you to look after that dagger.<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 가자, <value:$28>. 단검 건은 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1860085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、この辺にしよう。　　　　<line-break>ここで少し休憩だ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Let's stop here. We'll rest for a while.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이쯤 하지. 여기서 잠깐 쉬자.<end>"

## 1860086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、ちょっと周りが気になってね。<line-break>ノーブルを出る前のように<line-break>何者かが襲ってきたりしないかと。<line-break>なに、心配はいらないさ。<line-break><value:$28>だって剣が使えるし、<line-break>いざというときは父さんが守るからな。<end>"
EN: "I was just keeping an eye on our surroundings. After what happened before we left Noble, I thought someone might attack us again. But don't worry. <value:$28> can use a sword, and if anything happens, Father will protect you.<end>"
KO: "아, 주변이 조금 신경 쓰여서 말이야. 노블을 떠나기 전처럼 누군가 습격해 오지 않을까 해서. 뭐, 걱정할 건 없어. <value:$28>도 검을 쓸 줄 알고, 여차하면 아빠가 지켜 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 1860087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、ルルアンタを守りなさい。<line-break>それと短剣をなくさないでおくれ。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You protect Lulluantha. And please don't lose that dagger.<end>"
KO: "넌 루루안타를 지켜 줘. 그리고 단검을 잃어버리지 말아 줘.<end>"

## 1860088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今度は団体のようだ。　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ、<line-break>下がっていなさい。<end>"
EN: "This time, there seems to be a whole group of them. <value:$28>, Lulluantha, stay back.<end>"
KO: "…이번엔 단체인 모양이군. <value:$28>, 루루안타, 뒤로 물러나 있어.<end>"

## 1860097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ。今の地震で気配がばれたか。　<line-break>さすがだな、フリント。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Damn. That earthquake gave us away. Sharp as ever, Flint.<end>"
KO: "칫. 방금 지진 때문에 기척을 들켰나. 역시 플린트로군.<end>"

## 1860098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁーっ！<line-break>あんたたちぃ、この間のぉ！<end>"
EN: "Ah! It's you! From the other day!<end>"
KO: "아앗! 너희들, 지난번 그 녀석들이잖아!<end>"

## 1860099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるせぇ、このガキ！<line-break>今日はこの前のようには　　　　　<line-break>いかないぜえ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up, brat! Things won't go the way they did last time!<end>"
KO: "시끄러워, 이 꼬맹이! 오늘은 저번처럼 되진 않을 거다!<end>"

## 1860100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どのようなご用件でしょう？　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What business do you have with us?<end>"
KO: "…무슨 용건이십니까?<end>"

## 1860101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブルの代官、ボルボラから　　　<line-break>手紙を預かってるはずだ。<line-break>それを渡してもらおう。<end>"
EN: "You should have a letter from Bolbora, the governor of Noble. Hand it over.<end>"
KO: "노블의 대관 볼보라에게서 편지를 맡았을 거다. 그걸 내놔.<end>"

## 1860102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのような物は存じ上げません。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I know of no such thing.<end>"
KO: "그런 물건은 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1860103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、はいそうですかとは<line-break>引き下がれんな。素直に出した方が　<line-break>身のためなんじゃないか？<end>"
EN: "You can't expect us to take your word for it and leave. It'd be in your best interest to hand it over quietly, wouldn't it?<end>"
KO: "그렇다고 '네, 알겠습니다' 하고 물러설 순 없지. 순순히 내놓는 게 신상에 좋지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1860104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとぉ、目の細いおじさぁん！<line-break>そんなものないって言ってるでしょぉ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, squinty-eyed mister! He just told you he doesn't have it!<end>"
KO: "저기요, 눈 가느다란 아저씨이! 그런 건 없다니까요오!<end>"

## 1860105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんかうるさいのがいるな。<line-break>だが、もう人質はいらないんでな。<line-break>静かにしてないと痛い目にあうぜ？　<end>"
EN: "We've got a noisy one here. But we don't need any more hostages. Keep quiet, or you're going to get hurt.<end>"
KO: "시끄러운 녀석이 하나 있군. 하지만 이제 인질은 필요 없어. 얌전히 있지 않으면 아픈 꼴을 보게 될 텐데?<end>"

## 1860106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前たち、<line-break>このガキどもを大人しくさせちまえ！<line-break>リルビーの方はお頭への手みやげだ。<end>"
EN: "You lot, shut these brats up! The Lilubee will make a fine gift for the boss.<end>"
KO: "너희들, 이 꼬맹이들을 얌전히 만들어 버려! 릴비는 두목에게 줄 선물이다.<end>"

## 1860107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供たちに手出しはさせん！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I won't let you lay a hand on the children!<end>"
KO: "아이들에게는 손대지 못하게 하겠다!<end>"

## 1860108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、よそ見してるとケガするぜ。<line-break>お前の相手はこの俺だ！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Careful. Take your eyes off me and you'll get hurt. I'm your opponent!<end>"
KO: "어이, 한눈팔면 다친다. 네 상대는 바로 나다!<end>"

## 1860109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>こちらが終わったらすぐに行く！　<line-break>それまで持ちこたえてくれ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'll come as soon as I'm finished here! Hold out until then!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이쪽이 끝나면 곧 갈게! 그때까지 버텨 줘!<end>"

## 1860110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっへっへ。今日は、この前の男が<line-break>いねえからなあ。たっぷりと<line-break>お返ししてやるぜえ！<end>"

KO: "헤헤헤. 오늘은 지난번 그 남자가 없군. 실컷 갚아 주마!<end>"

## 1860111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이, 이 녀석들, 애들 주제에 강하잖아. 이런 얘긴 못 들었다고, 젠장!<end>"

## 1860112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>フリントさんを助けなきゃ！<end>"

## 1860113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、この毒、相当に効きがいい。<line-break>…にしても、この程度とは<line-break>石火のフリントも大したことねぇ。<end>"

## 1860114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "응? 뭐야, 그 녀석들 당한 거냐. 정말 어쩔 수 없는 놈들이군.<end>"

## 1860116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860117

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "플린트 씨, 플린트 씨! 정신 차려요!<end>"

## 1860118

## 1860119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860121

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントさん！　しっかりして！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>。急いで<line-break>お医者様に看てもらわないと……。<end>"
