KO: "플린트 씨! 정신 차려요! <value:$28>. 빨리 의사에게 보여 드려야 해…….<end>"

## 1860123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ダメだ！　そんなことより……　<line-break><value:$28>……王都へ…。<line-break>…ロストールへ行くんだ…。<end>"

KO: "…안 돼! 그것보다… <value:$28>… 왕도로…. 로스톨로 가….<end>"

## 1860124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなぁ！<line-break>早く看てもらわないとぉ！<end>"

KO: "그, 그런! 빨리 진찰받아야 해요!<end>"

## 1860125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…い、今は一刻を争うのだ…。　　<line-break>私のことは…心配いらない…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、頼む…。<line-break>王都…ロス…ト…ルへ急いでくれ。<line-break>早く…。<end>"
EN: "Every moment counts now... Don't worry about me... <value:$28>, please... Hurry to the royal capital... to Ros... torl. Quickly...<end>"
KO: "…지, 지금은 한시가 급해… 나는 걱정하지 마… <value:$28>, 부탁한다… 왕도 로스…토…올로 서둘러 줘. 빨리…<end>"

## 1860126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1860127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…응. 알았어. 그럼 로스톨로 서둘러야 해.<end>"

## 1860128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "응? <value:$28> 아니야. 왜 그렇게 급해?<end>"

## 1860129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだって！？<line-break>で、フリントは今、どこに？<end>"

KO: "뭐라고!? 그래서 플린트는 지금 어디에 있지?<end>"

## 1860130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町の外までは来ているんだな？<line-break>わかった。お前はすぐに<line-break>宿屋の手配をしてこい。<line-break>フリントは、<line-break>俺が宿屋まで運んでやる。<end>"
EN: "He's made it as far as just outside town, right? All right. Go make arrangements at the inn right away. I'll carry Flint there myself.<end>"
KO: "마을 밖까지 와 있구나? 알았다. 넌 바로 여관을 준비해 와. 플린트는 내가 여관까지 옮기겠다.<end>"

## 1860132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>…頼みが…ある。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>例の短剣の中に…<line-break>エリス様への…密書が入っている。<line-break><end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a favor to ask. Inside that dagger... there's a Secret Letter for Lady Eris.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, …부탁이… 있다. 그 단검 안에… 에리스 님께 드릴… 밀서가 들어 있다.<end>"

## 1860133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…その密書を、<line-break>王妃…エリス様に…　　　　　　　<line-break>…届けてくれ。<line-break>フ、フリントの子と言えば…<line-break>エリス様に…お目通り<line-break>…かなう…はず。<end>"
EN: "Take the Secret Letter... to Queen Eris. Tell her you're Flint's child... and she should grant you an audience.<end>"
KO: "…그 밀서를 왕비… 에리스 님께… 전달해 줘. 플, 플린트의 아이라고 하면… 에리스 님을… 뵐 수… 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1860134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんでぇ…なんでフリントさんが<line-break>そんな手紙を持ってるのぉ…？<end>"
EN: "But why...? Why did Flint have a letter like that...?<end>"
KO: "…왜… 왜 플린트 씨가 그런 편지를 가지고 있는 거야…?<end>"

## 1860135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すまない…お前たちには<line-break>黙っていたが…私はエリス王妃に…<line-break>仕えていたのだ…。<line-break>こ、今回のお役目が果たせたら…<line-break>お暇をいただき…３人で一緒に<line-break>世界を回ろうと…考えていた…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... I kept it from you both, but I was in Queen Eris's service. Once this mission was over... I meant to leave her service, and then the three of us could travel the world together...<end>"
KO: "…미안하다… 너희에겐 말하지 않았지만… 나는 에리스 왕비를… 섬기고 있었다…. 이, 이번 임무를 끝내면… 휴가를 받아… 셋이 함께 세계를 돌아다닐… 생각이었어….<end>"

## 1860136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがこのような目に遭うとは…　<line-break>…これも、自業自得というもの<line-break>かもしれん…ごほっ。<end>"
EN: "And instead, it came to this... Perhaps I brought it on myself... Cough!<end>"
KO: "그런데 이런 꼴을 당하다니… 이것도 자업자득일지 모르겠군… 콜록.<end>"

## 1860137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリント、もういい。<line-break>しゃべるな。<end>"
EN: "That's enough, Flint. Don't talk.<end>"
KO: "플린트, 이제 됐어. 말하지 마.<end>"

## 1860138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>はやく、密書を…、　　　　　　　<line-break>エリス…王妃に…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... hurry. Take the Secret Letter... to Queen... Eris...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…, 서둘러 밀서를… 에리스… 왕비에게….<end>"

## 1860139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってやれ。親父さんは、<line-break>お前が行かないと納得しないだろう。<line-break>ここは俺が見ている。<line-break>ロストールの王宮は、<line-break>正面の通りを抜けたところにある<line-break>広場の先だ。<end>"
EN: "Go on. Your father won't be satisfied unless you go. I'll look after him. Rostorl Palace is beyond the square at the end of the street straight ahead.<end>"
KO: "가 봐. 아버지는 네가 가지 않으면 납득하지 않을 거다. 여긴 내가 보고 있을게. 로스톨 왕궁은 정면 거리를 지나면 나오는 광장 너머에 있어.<end>"

## 1860140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はっきり言って<line-break>おやじさんは危険な状態だ…。<line-break>　<line-break>この衰弱ぶり…俺が見たところ、<line-break>刃に毒が塗ってあったようだ。<line-break>どうやら、シロウラギリという猛毒だ。<end>"
EN: "I'll be blunt: your father's in grave danger. Judging by how weak he's become, it looks as though the blade was poisoned. Apparently it's Shirouragiri, a deadly poison.<end>"
KO: "…솔직히 말해서 아버지는 위험한 상태야…. 이 쇠약한 정도를 보니, 내가 보기엔 칼날에 독이 발려 있었던 것 같아. 아무래도 시로우라기리라는 맹독이야.<end>"

## 1860141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス王妃は、<line-break>雌狐と呼ばれるほどの謀略家。<line-break>毒にも詳しい。<line-break>シロウラギリの毒に<line-break>フリントがやられたと言って、<line-break>生命のかけらをもらってこい。<end>"
EN: "Queen Eris is such a schemer that they call her the Vixen. She knows her poisons, too. Tell her Flint was poisoned with Shirouragiri and get a Life Fragment from her.<end>"
KO: "에리스 왕비는 암여우라 불릴 정도의 모략가다. 독에도 밝지. 시로우라기리 독에 플린트가 당했다고 말하고 생명의 조각을 받아 와.<end>"

## 1860142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いで戻れ。<line-break>…一刻を争う状況だ。<end>"
EN: "Come straight back. Every moment counts.<end>"
KO: "서둘러 돌아가. …한시가 급한 상황이다.<end>"

## 1860143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いでね、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Please hurry, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "서둘러, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1860144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3エリス王妃に会って<line-break>生命のかけらをもらってこい。<line-break>王妃はロストール王宮だ。<end>王妃のいる王宮へは、<line-break>とにかく奥へ奥へと行けばいい。<line-break>頼んだぜ、<value:$28>。<end>急いで戻れ。<line-break>…思ったより、衰弱が激しい。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Go see Queen Eris and get a Life Fragment from her. She's at Rostorl Palace.<end>Just keep heading farther in and you'll reach the palace where the Queen is. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>Come straight back. He's weakening faster than I thought.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3에리스 왕비를 만나 생명의 조각을 받아 와. 왕비는 로스톨 왕궁에 있어.<end>왕비가 있는 왕궁에는 계속 안쪽으로 가면 돼. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>서둘러 돌아와. …생각보다 쇠약이 심해.<end>"

## 1860145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王宮を知ってるかーい？<line-break>青い紋章の入った柱の間を行けば、　<line-break>貴族街に出るのさー。<line-break>その貴族街のさらに奥に行けば<line-break>王宮に出るのさー。<line-break>遠いから、ボクは行かないけどねー。<end>"
EN: "Do you know the way to Rostorl Palace? Pass between the pillars with blue crests, and into the noble quarter you go. Keep going deeper, and there stands the palace. Far too much walking for me, of course.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕궁 알아~? 파란 문장이 들어간 기둥 사이를 지나면 귀족가가 나와~. 그 귀족가 더 안쪽으로 가면 왕궁이 나오지~. 멀어서 난 안 가지만~.<end>"

## 1860146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮か？<line-break>この道をずっとまっすぐ行けば<line-break>王宮の門に出るよ。<end>"
EN: "The palace? Just keep going straight along this road and you'll come to the palace gates.<end>"
KO: "왕궁? 이 길을 계속 곧장 가면 왕궁 문이 나와.<end>"

## 1860147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0（こっちだと街の外に出てしまう…。<line-break>　王宮は確か逆方向だったな。）<end>（こっちだと街の外に出ちゃう…。<line-break>　王宮は確か逆方向よね。）<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0(This way leads out of town... The palace was in the other direction, wasn't it?)<end>(I'll end up outside town if I go this way... The palace is in the other direction, right?)<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0(이쪽으로 가면 도시 밖으로 나가 버린다…. 왕궁은 분명 반대 방향이었지.)<end>(이쪽으로 가면 도시 밖으로 나가 버려…. 왕궁은 분명 반대 방향이었지.)<end>"

## 1860148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（父さんが心配だから、<line-break>　早く帰らなきゃ。）<end>"
EN: "(I'm worried about Dad. I have to hurry home.)<end>"
KO: "(아버지가 걱정되니까 빨리 돌아가야 해.)<end>"

## 1860149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、お前は！<end>"
EN: "Who are you?!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 넌!<end>"

## 1860150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、待て。<line-break>何か急ぎの用がありそうだ。　　　　<line-break>どうしたのだ？<end>"
EN: "Wait. It looks like you have urgent business. What is it?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 기다려라. 무언가 급한 용무가 있는 것 같군. 무슨 일이냐?<end>"

## 1860151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0フリントの息子、だと？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>むむ？　確かにその短剣の<line-break>柄にあるのは王家の紋章に違いない。<line-break>少し待て。<end>フリントの娘、だと？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>むむ？　確かにその短剣の<line-break>柄にあるのは王家の紋章に違いない。<line-break>少し待て。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Flint's son, you say? Hmm... Yes, that is unmistakably the royal crest on the dagger's hilt. Wait a moment.<end>Flint's daughter, you say? Hmm... Yes, that is unmistakably the royal crest on the dagger's hilt. Wait a moment.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0플린트의 아들이라고? 음? 확실히 그 단검 자루에 있는 건 왕가의 문장이 틀림없군. 잠깐 기다려라.<end>플린트의 딸이라고? 음? 확실히 그 단검 자루에 있는 건 왕가의 문장이 틀림없군. 잠깐 기다려라.<end>"

## 1860152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様がお会いになるそうだ。　　　<line-break>ついてこい。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The Queen will see you. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "왕비님께서 만나시겠답니다. 따라오시오.<end>"

## 1860153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…密書は確かに受け取った。<line-break>ご苦労だった。<line-break>これは報酬だ。<end>"
EN: "…I have received the Secret Letter. Well done. Here is your reward.<end>"
KO: "…밀서는 확실히 받았다. 수고했다. 이건 보수다.<end>"

## 1860154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0シロウラギリの毒、だと？　　　　<line-break>フリントがか？<line-break>しかし…<line-break>フリントの息子、<line-break><value:$28>といったな。<line-break>ずいぶんと毒に詳しいではないか。<end>シロウラギリの毒、だと？　　　　<line-break>フリントがか？<line-break>しかし…<line-break>フリントの娘、<line-break><value:$28>といったな。<line-break>ずいぶんと毒に詳しいではないか。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Shirouragiri poison, you say? Flint was poisoned? But... Flint's son―<value:$28>, was it? You seem remarkably well versed in poisons.<end>Shirouragiri poison, you say? Flint was poisoned? But... Flint's daughter―<value:$28>, was it? You seem remarkably well versed in poisons.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0시로우라기리의 독이라고? 플린트가? 하지만… 플린트의 아들, <value:$28>라고 했지. 독에 대해 꽤 잘 아는군.<end>시로우라기리의 독이라고? 플린트가? 하지만… 플린트의 딸, <value:$28>라고 했지. 독에 대해 꽤 잘 아는군.<end>"

## 1860155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、<line-break>ゼネテスという冒険者がな…。　　<line-break>なるほど。<line-break>よかろう。<line-break>生命のかけらを与える。<line-break>これでフリントも回復しよう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, an adventurer named Xenetes… I see. Very well. I shall grant you a Life Fragment. With this, Flint should recover.<end>"
KO: "호오, 제네테스라는 모험자가…. 그렇군. 좋다. 생명의 조각을 주마. 이걸로 플린트도 회복하겠지.<end>"

## 1860156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "下がるがよい。<line-break>時をおいて、また連絡する。<line-break>役目、ご苦労だった。<end>"
EN: "You may go. I will contact you again in due course. You have served well.<end>"
KO: "물러가도 좋다. 때가 되면 다시 연락하지. 수고했다.<end>"

## 1860157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>ぐすっ。フリントさんが大変なの…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... *sniff* Flint's in terrible trouble...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 훌쩍. 플린트 씨가 큰일이야….<end>"

## 1860159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…エリス様は？<end>"
EN: "Lady Eris...?<end>"
KO: "…에리스 님은?<end>"

## 1860160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今まで、お前に…隠し事をしていた…。<line-break>すまなかった…。<line-break>あ、あとは、あとは…。<line-break>…ゼ…ゼネテス…様…。<end>"
EN: "I've kept things from you all this time... I'm sorry. And the rest... the rest... L-Lord Xenetes...<end>"
KO: "지금까지 네게… 숨긴 게 있었다…. 미안했다…. 아, 그리고, 그리고…. …제… 제네테스… 님….<end>"

## 1860161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break>あとのことは引き受ける。<end>"
EN: "I understand. Leave the rest to me.<end>"
KO: "알았다. 나머지는 맡아 두지.<end>"

## 1860162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様に…ついていくのだ。　　　<line-break>この方なら…お前を…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Go with Lord Xenetes. With him... you can...<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님을… 따라가거라. 이분이라면… 너를….<end>"

## 1860163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっく、ひっく…<line-break>フリントさん、フリントさん！<line-break>　<line-break>目を開けてよぉ！<line-break>そんなのイヤだよぉ！<line-break>うわ～ん！！！<end>"
EN: "H-hic... Flint! Flint! Open your eyes! I don't want this! Waaaah!<end>"
KO: "흑, 흑… 플린트 씨, 플린트 씨! 눈을 떠 줘! 이건 싫어! 으아앙!!!<end>"

## 1860164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>もう…ずっとそうしてるよ？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... you've been like this for ages, you know?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 벌써… 계속 그러고 있잖아?<end>"

## 1860165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、お使いに行ってくるね？<line-break>おいしいものたくさん作るから、<line-break>今夜はちゃんと食べようね？<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha's going out on an errand, all right? I'll make lots of tasty things, so let's make sure we eat tonight, all right?<end>"
KO: "루루안타, 심부름 다녀올게. 맛있는 거 많이 만들 테니까 오늘 밤은 꼭 제대로 먹자, 응?<end>"

## 1860166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、じゃあ<line-break>ルルアンタ、行ってくるからね。<end>"
EN: "Then... then Lulluantha's going now, okay?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 그럼 루루안타 다녀올게!<end>"

## 1860167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー！<line-break>あの人、この前のぉ…！<end>"
EN: "Ah! It's that person from before...!<end>"
KO: "아! 그 사람, 지난번의…!<end>"

## 1860168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ！　フリントの野郎め！<line-break>ニセの書状をつかませてくれるとは<line-break>あじなまねをしやがって！<line-break>小遣いでも稼ごうと思ってたが、<line-break>こんなことがお頭にばれたら<line-break>一大事だぜ。<end>"
EN: "Damn that Flint! Giving me a forged letter was a clever little trick. I only meant to make some spending money, but if the boss finds out about this, I'm finished.<end>"
KO: "쳇! 플린트 녀석! 가짜 서장을 쥐여 주다니 제법 잔꾀를 부렸군! 용돈이나 벌어 보려 했는데 이런 게 두목한테 들키면 큰일이야.<end>"

## 1860169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のはフリントさんのぉ…<line-break>…ルルアンタ、許さないんだから！<end>"
EN: "That one was for Flint... Lulluantha will never forgive you!<end>"
KO: "방금 건 플린트 씨의… …루루안타, 용서 안 할 거야!<end>"

## 1860170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>今のはルルアンタだったような…。<line-break>どこかにお使いか？<end>"
EN: "Hm? Was that Lulluantha just now...? Is she off on an errand?<end>"
KO: "응? 방금 루루안타였던 것 같은데…. 어디 심부름이라도 가나?<end>"

## 1860171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだぁ。まだふさぎ込んでるのか？<end>"
EN: "What, are you still moping?<end>"
KO: "뭐야. 아직도 풀이 죽어 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1860172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんの気持ちはわかるが、<line-break>少しは元気を出さにゃいかんな。<line-break>ルルアンタも心配している。<line-break>あの子も、父親代わりの人を失って<line-break>つらいはずだ。それなのに、お前さんを<line-break>元気づけようと一生懸命なんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "I know how you feel, but you've got to pull yourself together a little. Lulluantha's worried about you. She's grieving too―she lost the man who was like a father to her. Even so, she's doing everything she can to cheer you up.<end>"
KO: "자네 마음은 알겠지만 조금은 기운을 내야 해. 루루안타도 걱정하고 있어. 그 아이도 아버지 같은 사람을 잃어서 힘들 텐데, 그래도 자네를 격려하려고 필사적이잖아.<end>"

## 1860173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからはお互いに助けあって<line-break>生きていかなきゃならない。いつまでも<line-break>お前さんがそんな風でどうするんだ。<end>"
EN: "From now on, the two of you will have to help each other survive. You can't go on like this forever.<end>"
KO: "이제부터는 서로 도우며 살아가야 해. 언제까지나 자네가 그런 식이면 어쩌겠나.<end>"

## 1860174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタと言えば、<line-break>さっき通りで見かけたな。<line-break>広場の方に走っていったが…。<line-break>お前さん、<line-break>何か用事を頼んだりしたか？<end>"
EN: "Speaking of Lulluantha, I saw her in the street a little while ago. She was running toward the square. Did you send her on an errand?<end>"
KO: "루루안타라면 아까 거리에서 봤어. 광장 쪽으로 달려가던데…. 자네, 무슨 심부름이라도 시켰나?<end>"

## 1860175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。やっぱり、お使いか。<line-break>ま、町の外に出たわけじゃない。<line-break>じきに戻るだろう。<line-break>それよりも、お前さんがようやく<line-break>やる気を出してきたところで、<line-break>ひとつ話がある。<end>"
EN: "So she is on an errand. Well, she hasn't left town. She'll be back soon enough. More importantly, now that you're finally showing some spirit again, there's something we need to discuss.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 역시 심부름이었나. 뭐, 도시 밖으로 나간 건 아니니 곧 돌아오겠지. 그보다 네가 드디어 의욕을 내기 시작했으니 할 이야기가 하나 있다.<end>"

## 1860176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父さんからよろしく言われているが、<line-break>これからずっと、お前さんに<line-break>くっついてるわけにはいかない。<line-break>お前さんには、ひとりでも<line-break>生きていける力を身につけて<line-break>もらわにゃならん。<end>"
EN: "Your father asked me to look after you, but I can't stay by your side forever. You need to learn how to survive on your own.<end>"
KO: "네 아버지가 잘 부탁한다고 했지만 앞으로 계속 네 곁에 붙어 있을 수는 없어. 너 혼자서도 살아갈 수 있는 힘을 익혀야 한다.<end>"

## 1860177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし。お前さんも<line-break>剣をいじったことくらいは<line-break>あるらしいからな。<line-break>俺と同じ、冒険者になってもらう。<line-break>俺にあとを頼むってことは、<line-break>親父さんもそれを望んでいたはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Good. It seems you've at least handled a sword before. You're going to become an adventurer, like me. If your father entrusted you to me, that must be what he wanted too.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 너도 검을 좀 만져 본 모양이니 나와 같은 모험자가 되어라. 나한테 뒤를 부탁했다는 건 아버지도 그걸 바랐다는 뜻일 거야.<end>"

## 1860178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。それじゃあ、約束だからな。<line-break>お前さんが一人前の冒険者になるまでは<line-break>一緒に行かせてもらうことにする。<line-break>となると、行かなけりゃならん<line-break>ところがあるな。ついてきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Then it's a promise. I'll travel with you until you're a full-fledged adventurer. That means there's somewhere we need to go. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "알았어. 그럼 약속한 거다. 네가 한 사람 몫 하는 모험가가 될 때까지 같이 가 주지. 그러면 가야 할 곳이 있군. 따라와.<end>"

## 1860179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、冒険者がまずやらなきゃ<line-break>ならんことは、冒険者ギルドへの<line-break>登録だ。決まりなんでな。<line-break>というわけで、この町のギルドに<line-break>行くとしよう。先に外で待っている。<line-break>用意ができたら、来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Now, the first thing an adventurer has to do is register with the Adventurers' Guild. Rules are rules. So, let's head to the Guild here in town. I'll go on ahead and wait outside. Come and find me when you're ready.<end>"
KO: "자, 모험가가 가장 먼저 해야 하는 일은 모험가 길드 등록이다. 규칙이라서 말이지. 그러니 이 마을 길드로 가자. 나는 먼저 밖에서 기다릴 테니 준비되면 와.<end>"

## 1860180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "元気になったようですね。<line-break>気をつけて行ってらしてください。<end>"
EN: "You seem much better now. Take care out there.<end>"
KO: "기운을 되찾으신 것 같네요. 조심해서 다녀오세요.<end>"

## 1860181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急いでいたのではないか？　　　　　<line-break>用がすんだのであれば、<line-break>早々に立ち去るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Weren't you in a hurry? If you've finished your business, be on your way.<end>"
KO: "서두르고 있던 것 아니냐? 볼일이 끝났다면 어서 떠나도록 해라.<end>"

## 1860182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec186-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You've done well. You may return to Flint.<end>"
KO: "수고했다. 플린트에게 돌아가도록 해라.<end>"

## 1870001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はノエルの使いで<line-break>お前に会いに来た。<line-break><line-break>シルヴァを襲ったソウルイーターが<line-break>竜王の島に現れたのだ。ノエルは先に<line-break>竜王の島へ向かった。<end>"
EN: "I've come to see you today on Noel's behalf. The Soul Eater that attacked Silva has appeared on Dragon King's Island. Noel has already gone on ahead.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 노엘의 심부름으로 너를 만나러 왔다. 실바를 습격한 소울 이터가 용왕의 섬에 나타났다. 노엘은 먼저 용왕의 섬으로 향했다.<end>"

## 1870002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、今のノエルの力では<line-break>ソウルイーターに勝つのは難しいと<line-break>止めたのだが、聞き入れられなかった。<line-break>逆に、お前に加勢を頼んでくるように<line-break>言われてな、こうして出向いた<line-break>というわけだ。<end>"
EN: "I tried to stop her. With Noel's current strength, defeating the Soul Eater would be difficult, but she refused to listen. Instead, she sent me to ask for your help. That is why I am here.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금 노엘의 힘으로는 소울 이터를 이기기 어렵다며 말렸지만 듣지 않더군. 오히려 네게 원군을 부탁해 오라고 해서 이렇게 찾아온 거다.<end>"

## 1870003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>ノエルの求めに応じて、今すぐに<line-break>竜王の島へ出向く気があるか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Will you answer Noel's request and leave for Dragon King's Island at once?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 노엘의 요청에 응해 지금 당장 용왕의 섬으로 갈 생각이 있나?<end>"

## 1870004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今すぐに行く<end>"
EN: "I'll go right away.<end>"
KO: "지금 당장 간다<end>"

## 1870005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は忙しい<end>"
EN: "I'm busy right now.<end>"
KO: "지금은 바빠<end>"

## 1870006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。それなら急いでもらおう。<line-break>今ならノエルに追いつけるかもしれぬ。<line-break>　<line-break>今のノエルは自分が見えていない。<line-break>危険を省みずに無茶するかも<line-break>しれんからな。<end>"
EN: "Good. Then make haste. You may still be able to catch up with Noel. She is not thinking clearly right now. She may do something reckless without regard for the danger.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 서둘러 줘. 지금이라면 노엘을 따라잡을지도 모른다. 지금 노엘은 자기 자신을 보지 못하고 있어. 위험도 생각하지 않고 무리할지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1870007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。仕方ないな。<line-break>ノエルは悲しむだろうが、<line-break>お前にも都合があるだろう。<line-break>せめて、ノエルの勝利を祈って<line-break>やってくれ。それではな。<end>"
EN: "I see. Very well. Noel will be saddened, but you have your own affairs to consider. At least pray for her victory. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 어쩔 수 없군. 노엘은 슬퍼하겠지만 너에게도 사정이 있겠지. 적어도 노엘의 승리를 빌어 줘. 그럼 잘 가라.<end>"

## 1870008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここが竜王様のおられる島だ。<line-break>ノエルはすでにソウルイーターと<line-break>戦っているかもしれぬな。<line-break>お前も急いでくれ。<end>"
EN: "This is the island where the Dragon King resides. Noel may already be fighting the Soul Eater. You'd better hurry as well.<end>"
KO: "여기가 용왕님이 계신 섬이다. 노엘은 이미 소울 이터와 싸우고 있을지도 모른다. 너도 서둘러라.<end>"

## 1870009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ…レイヴン…<line-break>カフィン…<line-break>こんなことに…なるなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Raven... Coffin... I never thought this would happen...<end>"
KO: "아아… 레이븐… 카핀… 이런 일이… 되다니….<end>"

## 1870010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<line-break><value:$28>…さん。<line-break>来てくれたんですね…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>... You came...<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28>… 씨. 와 주셨군요….<end>"

## 1870011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、無事か？<end>"
EN: "Noel, are you all right?<end>"
KO: "노엘, 무사한가?<end>"

## 1870012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナーシェス…。<line-break>…ごめんなさい、<line-break>あなたの言うとおりだった…。<line-break>レイヴンも…カフィンも<line-break>私を守って…。<line-break>…あぅっ！<end>"
EN: "Narscess... I'm sorry. You were right... Raven and Coffin, they both protected me... Ngh!<end>"
KO: "나셰스…. …미안해. 네 말이 맞았어…. 레이븐도… 카핀도 나를 지키다가…. …윽!<end>"

## 1870013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、無理をするな。<end>"
EN: "Noel, do not push yourself.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 무리하지 마.<end>"

## 1870014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>レイヴンとカフィンのふたりは<line-break>私がみる。<line-break>ソウルイーターは手負いだ。<line-break>今なら倒せるだろう。<line-break>任せるぞ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I will tend to Raven and Coffin. The Soul Eater is wounded. You should be able to defeat it now. I leave it to you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 레이븐과 카핀 두 사람은 내가 보겠다. 소울 이터는 부상당했어. 지금이라면 쓰러뜨릴 수 있겠지. 맡긴다.<end>"

## 1870015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 씨.<end>"

## 1870016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お願いします<line-break>みんなの命を…。<end>"
EN: "Please... save everyone's lives...<end>"
KO: "…부탁드립니다. 모두의 목숨을….<end>"

## 1870017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>さん。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒してくれて。<line-break>…でも、ふたりが、<line-break>カフィンも、レイヴンも<line-break>目をあけてくれないんです…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Thank you for defeating the Soul Eater. But neither of them―Coffin or Raven―will open their eyes...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 씨. 감사합니다. 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨려 줘서. …하지만 두 사람 모두, 카핀도 레이븐도 눈을 떠 주지 않아요….<end>"

## 1870018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっと、<line-break>ソウルイーターを倒したのに…。<line-break>こんなはずじゃ、なかったのに…。<line-break>わたし、何をやってるんだろう…<line-break>また、同じ過ちをくりかえして…<line-break>また、大切な人たちを失って…。<end>"
EN: "We finally defeated the Soul Eater... This wasn't how it was supposed to end. What am I even doing? Repeating the same mistake again... Losing the people I love all over again...<end>"
KO: "…겨우 소울 이터를 쓰러뜨렸는데…. 이럴 생각이 아니었는데…. 나, 대체 뭘 하는 거지… 또 같은 실수를 반복하고… 또 소중한 사람들을 잃고….<end>"

## 1870019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、このふたりを生き返らせる<line-break>手段はないでもない。<end>"
EN: "Noel, there may yet be a way to bring these two back to life.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 이 두 사람을 되살릴 방법이 아예 없는 건 아니다.<end>"

## 1870020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、なんて…？<end>"
EN: "What did you just say...?<end>"
KO: "방금, 뭐라고…?<end>"

## 1870021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このふたりを生き返らせる手段は<line-break>ないでもない、そう言ったのだ。<end>"
EN: "I said there may yet be a way to bring these two back to life.<end>"
KO: "이 두 사람을 되살릴 방법이 아예 없는 건 아니라고 말한 거다.<end>"

## 1870022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　え！？<line-break>…それは、どうすれば…？<end>"
EN: "What? There is?! What do I have to do...?<end>"
KO: "어? 어!? …그건 어떻게 하면 돼…?<end>"

## 1870023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは、竜王様のおられる地。<line-break>竜王様に願うのだ。ふたりの復活を。<end>"
EN: "This is the land where the Dragon King dwells. Pray to the Dragon King for their resurrection.<end>"
KO: "여기는 용왕님이 계신 땅. 용왕님께 빌어라. 두 사람의 부활을.<end>"

## 1870024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王様に…願う…？<end>"
EN: "Pray... to the Dragon King?<end>"
KO: "용왕님께… 소원을…?<end>"

## 1870025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オズワルドは以前より竜王様への<line-break>信仰があつかった。お前も幼少より、<line-break>竜王様に祈りを捧げてきたのだろう？<end>"
EN: "Oswald has long been a land of deep devotion to the Dragon King. You have offered prayers to the Dragon King since childhood, have you not?<end>"
KO: "오즈왈드는 예전부터 용왕님에 대한 신앙이 깊었다. 너도 어릴 때부터 용왕님께 기도를 올려왔겠지?<end>"

## 1870026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　あ、はい。<line-break>いつも、お祈りには<line-break>参加してましたけど…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Ah, yes. I always took part in the prayers, but...<end>"
KO: "네? 아, 네. 늘 기도에는 참가했지만요…<end>"

## 1870027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのお前の願いなら、<line-break>無限のソウルを持つお前の<line-break>願いであるのなら、<line-break>竜王様がお聞き届けに<line-break>なるやもしれぬ。<line-break>さぁ、願うがいい。<end>"
EN: "Then the Dragon King may heed your plea―yours, who possesses the Infinite Soul. Go. Make your wish.<end>"
KO: "그 소원이 무한의 소울을 지닌 네 소원이라면 용왕님께서 들어주실지도 모른다. 자, 소원을 빌어라.<end>"

## 1870028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王様、どうかわたし、<line-break>ノエル・シルヴァの<line-break>願いをお聞き届けください。　　　<line-break>竜王様に捧げられた町、<line-break>オズワルドの人々の命を救うために<line-break>戦った、勇気ある友人たち…<end>"
EN: "Dragon King, please hear the plea of Noel Silva. My brave friends fought to save the lives of the people of Oswald, a town devoted to you...<end>"
KO: "용왕님, 부디 저 노엘 실바의 소원을 들어 주세요. 용왕님께 바쳐진 도시 오즈월드 사람들의 생명을 구하기 위해 싸운 용감한 친구들….<end>"

## 1870029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン、レイヴン、両名の<line-break>ソウルの復活を！<end>"
EN: "Restore the Souls of Coffin and Raven!<end>"
KO: "카핀과 레이븐, 두 사람의 소울을 부활시켜 주세요!<end>"

## 1870030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、この声は…！<end>"
EN: "Th-this voice...!<end>"
KO: "이, 이 목소리는…!<end>"

## 1870031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…竜王様だ。<end>"
EN: "It is the Dragon King...<end>"
KO: "…용왕님이다.<end>"

## 1870032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　我を崇めし娘、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者、<line-break>　　　　　　　　ノエルよ。<line-break>　　　　　　汝の願い、しかと聞いた。<end>"
EN: "Noel, maiden who worships me, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I have heard your plea.<end>"
KO: "나를 숭배하는 딸, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자 노엘이여. 그대의 소원, 분명히 들었다.<end>"

## 1870033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　我が力をもってすれば、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　汝の願いを聞き届けるのは<line-break>　　　　　　　たやすいこと。<line-break>　　　　　　　　だが…<end>"
EN: "With my power, granting your wish would be a simple matter. But...<end>"
KO: "내 힘이라면 그대의 소원을 들어주는 것은 쉬운 일이다. 하지만….<end>"

## 1870034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　だが、そのときは　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝は我の授けし使命を<line-break>　　　　　　　果たさねばならぬ。<line-break>　　　　　　　それでも、よいか？<end>"
EN: "But if I do, you must fulfill the mission I bestow upon you. Do you accept, even so?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그때는 그대가 내가 내린 사명을 완수해야 한다. 그래도 좋으냐?<end>"

## 1870035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王様の…使命…？　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>はい、かまいません！<line-break>わたしにできることなら<line-break>何でもします！<end>"
EN: "A mission... from the Dragon King? Yes, I accept! If it's something I can do, I'll do anything!<end>"
KO: "용왕님의… 사명…? 네, 괜찮아요! 제가 할 수 있는 일이라면 뭐든 하겠습니다!<end>"

## 1870036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしの大切な人たちを<line-break>助けるためなら、<line-break>なんだって…！<end>"
EN: "If it will save the people I love, I'll do anything...!<end>"
KO: "내 소중한 사람들을 구할 수 있다면 뭐든지…!<end>"

## 1870037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　その言葉、　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　偽りはないか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Do you speak those words truly?<end>"
KO: "그 말에 거짓은 없느냐?<end>"

## 1870038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　よかろう。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　汝の願い、<line-break>　　　　　　しかと聞き届けた。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Very well. Your wish is granted.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그대의 소원, 분명히 들어주었다.<end>"

## 1870039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル！<end>"
EN: "Noel!<end>"
KO: "노엘!<end>"

## 1870040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノエル。<end>"
EN: "Noel...<end>"
KO: "…노엘.<end>"

## 1870041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…カフィン、<line-break>…レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Coffin... Raven!<end>"
KO: "…카핀, …레이븐!<end>"

## 1870042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、こんなことって…！<end>"
EN: "Oh... I can't believe this...!<end>"
KO: "아아, 이런 일이…!<end>"

## 1870043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配させてしまったな。<line-break>…すまない。<end>"
EN: "I made you worry... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "걱정 끼쳤군. …미안하다.<end>"

## 1870044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、あんたはまだ<line-break>ひとりでは放っておけないから<line-break>もう少し一緒にいてやらないとね。<end>"
EN: "Noel, we still can't leave you on your own. We'll have to stay with you a little longer.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 넌 아직 혼자 내버려 둘 수 없으니 조금 더 같이 있어 줘야겠네.<end>"

## 1870045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うん。<end>"
EN: "...Yeah.<end>"
KO: "…응.<end>"

## 1870046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、<line-break>ありがとう。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>よかったね。あんたが<line-break>がんばってるってこと、<line-break>ちゃんと見てくれてる方がいたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel, thank you. Isn't it wonderful? Someone has been watching over you and sees how hard you've been trying.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 고마워. 다행이네. 네가 열심히 하고 있다는 걸 제대로 지켜봐 준 분이 있었어.<end>"

## 1870047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うん。<end>"
EN: "...Yeah.<end>"
KO: "…응.<end>"

## 1870048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル。<line-break>そして、カフィン、レイヴン。<line-break>　<line-break>竜王様の与えられた奇跡の力を<line-break>いつまでも忘れないことだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel. Coffin, Raven. Never forget the miraculous power bestowed by the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "노엘. 그리고 카핀, 레이븐. 용왕님이 내려 주신 기적의 힘을 언제까지나 잊지 마라.<end>"

## 1870049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。決して忘れません。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>竜王様がわたしを必要とされるなら、<line-break>そのときは、必ず今日のお礼に！<end>"
EN: "I won't. I will never forget. If the Dragon King ever has need of me, I promise I'll repay today's kindness!<end>"
KO: "네. 절대 잊지 않겠습니다. 용왕님께서 저를 필요로 하신다면 그때는 반드시 오늘의 은혜를 갚겠습니다!<end>"

## 1870050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行くとするか。<end>"
EN: "Then let us be on our way.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가볼까.<end>"

## 1870051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、待ってください！<end>"
EN: "Wait, please!<end>"
KO: "아, 기다려 주세요!<end>"

## 1870052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>今日は本当に<line-break>ありがとうございました！　　　　<line-break>わたし、<value:$28>さんなら<line-break>きっと、助けに来てくれるって<line-break>信じてたんです！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, thank you so much for everything today! I knew <value:$28> would come to help us!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 오늘은 정말 감사했습니다! 저는 <value:$28> 님이라면 분명 구하러 와 주실 거라고 믿고 있었어요!<end>"

## 1870053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたし、よかったです。<line-break><value:$28>さんという人に<line-break>出会うことができて！<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad I got to meet someone like you, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "저, 정말 다행이에요. <value:$28> 님이라는 분을 만날 수 있어서요!<end>"

## 1870054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、帰りましょうか！<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we go home?<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아갈까요!<end>"

## 1870055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　ノエルよ…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　我との約束、<line-break>　　　　　　　忘れるでないぞ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Noel... Do not forget your promise to me.<end>"
KO: "노엘이여…. 나와의 약속을 잊지 말거라.<end>"

## 1870056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>わたしたち、<line-break>一度オズワルドに戻ってみます。<line-break>でも、冒険者はやめません。<line-break>ですから、オズワルドを出たら<line-break>またお会いしましょう！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, we're going back to Oswald for a while. But we won't stop being adventurers. So once we leave Oswald, let's meet again!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 저희는 한번 오즈월드로 돌아가 보려고 해요. 하지만 모험자를 그만두진 않아요. 오즈월드를 나오면 다시 만나요!<end>"

## 1870057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>あたしたちを助けに来てくれて、<line-break>本当にありがとう。<line-break>あたしは今まで、<line-break>竜王様のご加護ってものを<line-break>そんなには信じてなかったけど、<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, thank you so much for coming to save us. Until now, I never really believed in the Dragon King's protection, but...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 우리를 구하러 와 줘서 정말 고마워. 나는 지금까지 용왕님의 가호라는 걸 그렇게 믿진 않았지만,<end>"

## 1870058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたとノエルを会わせてくれた<line-break>ことには、感謝したいわ。<end>"
EN: "But I am grateful to the Dragon King for bringing you and Noel together.<end>"
KO: "당신과 노엘을 만나게 해 준 것만큼은 감사하고 싶어.<end>"

## 1870059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>失礼します！<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, we'll be going!<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28> 님, 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 1870061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec187-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度は俺たちが　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>君の助けになれるといいな。<line-break><end>"
EN: "This time, I hope we can help you.<end>"
KO: "다음에는 우리가 네 도움이 될 수 있으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1880000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍イベント開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "해방군 이벤트 시작<end>"

## 1880001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前が来るのを待っていたんだ。<line-break>クリュセイス様のお話を聞いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I've been waiting for you. Please hear what Lady Khryuseis has to say.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 네가 오길 기다리고 있었다. 크류세이스 님의 말씀을 들어 줘.<end>"

## 1880002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待っていました、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>あなたに頼みたいことがあります。<line-break>先日、リベルダムの廃墟から、<line-break>埋蔵金のありかを記した地図が<line-break>見つかったのです。<end>"
EN: "I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>. I have a favor to ask. A map showing the location of a hidden fortune was recently found among the ruins of Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다, <value:$28>. 부탁드릴 일이 있습니다. 얼마 전 리벨덤 폐허에서 매장금의 위치를 적은 지도가 발견됐습니다.<end>"

## 1880003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく、リベルダムの商人が<line-break>隠していたのでしょう。ですから<line-break>それなりの額が期待できます。<line-break>おわかりですね？　この埋蔵金を<line-break>解放軍の資金としたいのです。<end>"
EN: "It was probably hidden by one of Liberdam's merchants, so we can expect a considerable sum. You understand, I trust? I want to use this fortune to fund the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "아마 리벨덤의 상인이 숨겨 둔 것이겠죠. 그러니 상당한 금액을 기대할 수 있습니다. 아시겠습니까? 이 매장금을 해방군의 자금으로 쓰고 싶습니다.<end>"

## 1880005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1880006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1880007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、さっそく<line-break>調査に向かってください。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>地図によると、埋蔵金は<line-break>ロストールの東にある洞窟に<line-break>隠されているようです。<end>"
EN: "Then please begin your investigation at once. According to the map, the fortune appears to be hidden in a cave east of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "그럼 곧바로 조사하러 가 주세요. 지도에 따르면 매장금은 로스톨 동쪽 동굴에 숨겨져 있는 모양입니다.<end>"

## 1880008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それと、以前お父様に聞いた話では<line-break>財宝を隠すときに、門番として<line-break>モンスターを置く商人もいたそうです。<line-break>くれぐれも、用心してください。<end>"
EN: "One more thing. My father once told me that some merchants left monsters to guard their hidden treasure. Please be extremely careful.<end>"
KO: "그리고 예전에 아버지께 들은 이야기로는 보물을 숨길 때 문지기로 몬스터를 두는 상인도 있었다고 합니다. 부디 조심하세요.<end>"

## 1880009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "埋蔵金は<line-break>ロストールの東に行ったところにある<line-break>洞窟に隠されているようです。<line-break>よろしく、頼みますよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems the buried treasure is hidden in a cave east of Rostorl. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "매장금은 로스톨 동쪽에 있는 동굴에 숨겨져 있는 모양입니다. 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1880010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の活動には、どうしても<line-break>資金が必要だ。リベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金なら期待できる。<line-break>頼んだぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army cannot carry on without funds. The buried treasure of a Liberdam merchant looks promising. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "해방군 활동에는 아무래도 자금이 필요하다. 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이라면 기대할 만해. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1880011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…解放軍の活動よりも<line-break>優先させることが、<line-break>あなたにはあるというのですか？<line-break>仕方ありませんね。<line-break>他の仲間に当たってみることにします。<line-break>もう、行っていいですよ。<end>"
EN: "You have something more important than the work of the Liberation Army? Very well. I will ask one of our other comrades. You may go now.<end>"
KO: "…해방군 활동보다 우선시할 일이 당신에게 있다는 건가요? 어쩔 수 없군요. 다른 동료를 알아보겠습니다. 이제 가셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 1880012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はあなたに用はありませんよ。<line-break>どこにでも行ってください。<end>"
EN: "I have no need of you at present. Go wherever you please.<end>"
KO: "지금은 당신에게 볼일이 없습니다. 어디든 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前なら、埋蔵金の調査に適任だと<line-break>思ったんだがな。<line-break>残念だよ。<end>"
EN: "I thought you'd be just the person to investigate the buried treasure. Too bad.<end>"
KO: "네가 매장금 조사에 적임자라고 생각했는데. 아쉽군.<end>"

## 1880014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスターに守らせているということは<line-break>リベルダム商人の埋蔵金はこの奥だね。<line-break>さぁ、見てみよう。<end>"
EN: "The fact that monsters were set to guard it means the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure must be farther in. Come on, let's take a look.<end>"
KO: "몬스터에게 지키게 했다는 건 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이 이 안쪽에 있다는 뜻이겠군. 자, 확인해 보자.<end>"

## 1880015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "埋蔵金を発見した。<end>"
EN: "Buried treasure found.<end>"
KO: "매장금을 발견했다.<end>"

## 1880016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お金は貯め込まれているだけでは<line-break>無意味だからね。お金にとっても<line-break>見つけてもらってよかったのかもね。<line-break>さぁ、<value:$28>。<line-break>戻って、埋蔵金を発見したことを<line-break>報告しないと。<end>"
EN: "Money is worthless if all you do is hoard it. Maybe even the money itself is glad we found it. Come on, <value:$28>. We need to go back and report that we found the hidden treasure.<end>"
KO: "돈은 쌓아 두기만 하면 의미가 없으니까. 돈 입장에서도 발견된 게 다행일지도 모르지. 자, <value:$28>. 돌아가서 매장금을 발견했다고 보고해야지.<end>"

## 1880017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスターが守っているってことは<line-break>埋蔵金はこの奥みたいだね。<line-break>　<line-break>どれくらいあるんだろう。<line-break>早く見てみようよ。<end>"
EN: "If monsters are guarding it, the hidden treasure must be farther in. I wonder how much there is. Come on, let's hurry and see!<end>"
KO: "몬스터가 지키고 있다는 건 매장금이 이 안쪽에 있다는 뜻 같네. 얼마나 있을까. 빨리 가 보자.<end>"

## 1880018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわぁ！　さすが、リベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金だね。ボクが想像してたのよりも<line-break>ずっとすごいや。<line-break>これで解放軍のお金の問題も解決だね。<line-break>さぁ、戻ろう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Wow! You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. It's far more impressive than I imagined! This should solve the Liberation Army's money troubles. Come on, let's head back, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "우와! 역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이네. 내가 상상한 것보다 훨씬 대단해. 이걸로 해방군의 자금 문제도 해결이군. 자, 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>15でも、もったいない話だよね。<line-break>これだけお金があるなら生活が苦しい<line-break>人たちに分けてあげればいいのに…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>19でも、これだけお金があるんだったら<line-break>お金がなくて困ってる人に<line-break>分けてあげればいいのに…。 <end>でも、これだけお金があるのに<line-break>貧しい人たちの生活をよくするようには<line-break>使ってあげないんだよね…。 <end>"

KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>15그래도 아까운 일이네. 이만큼 돈이 있으면 생활이 어려운 사람들에게 나눠 주면 좋을 텐데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그래도 이렇게 돈이 많으면 돈이 없어 곤란한 사람들에게 나눠 주면 좋을 텐데….<end>그래도 이렇게 돈이 많은데 가난한 사람들의 생활을 낫게 하는 데는 쓰지 않는구나….<end>"
