<end><if><value:$01><equal>12さすがはリベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金だな。こんなところに<line-break>これだけ蓄えていたとは。<line-break>これで、解放軍の財政難も<line-break>それなりに解消するだろう。<line-break>さっそく戻って報告しようぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13大したものだな。<line-break>…戻るぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10うひゃあ。こりゃすげえや。<line-break>これだけあればバッチリだな。<line-break>戻って報告しようぜ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14さすがはリベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金じゃわい。<line-break>さぁ、戻って報告じゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17うっげー！　目が痛くなるわ、下劣な<line-break>人間の欲望の固まりを見せつけられて！<line-break>もう、一秒たりともいたくなくてよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…埋蔵金は発見した。<line-break>戻って報告だ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23さすがはリベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金とでもいうべきか…<line-break>よくこれだけ蓄えたものだ。<line-break>さぁ、戻ろう<end><if><value:$01><equal>27これはすごい…。<line-break>これで、解放軍の財政難も<line-break>十分に解消されるはずだ。<line-break>さっそく戻って報告しよう。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26さすがはリベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金じゃな。<line-break>さぁ、戻って報告じゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24さすがはリベルダムの商人の<line-break>埋蔵金ですね。<line-break>さっそく戻って報告しましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25すごい…<line-break>これだけあれば十分よね<line-break>はやく戻ろうよ<end><if><value:$01><equal>21さすがに大した量だな。<line-break>これだけあれば解放軍の財政難も<line-break>解消するだろう。<line-break>さぁ、戻って報告するぞ、<line-break><value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>192戻って報告しましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193す、すごすぎます。<line-break>まさかこんなにもあるなんて…<line-break>早速戻って報告しましょう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194おびただしい量の虚栄と貪欲…。<line-break>くだらないね。<line-break>さあ、早く戻るとしよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191さすがリベルダム商人の<line-break>埋蔵金なだけはあるわね。<line-break>目が痛くなりそうね。<line-break>戻って報告ね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190これは驚いた。<line-break>これだけあれば解放軍の財政難も<line-break>解消するな。<line-break>戻って報告だ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2This is... incredible. It certainly lives up to being the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. Let's go back and report this at once. And don't pocket any of it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Well... this is quite a haul. You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. With this much, the Liberation Army's budget should recover considerably. Let us return and report our discovery at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Hee-hee. With this much, no one would notice if I skimmed off ten or twenty percent. What should I do...? I think I'll take some! Eek... I was only joking! <value:$28>, you're looking awfully serious...<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Whoa! This is incredible. This much ought to do the trick. Let's go back and report it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7The Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure is every bit as impressive as one would expect. Let's go back and report this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. This should be more than enough to resolve the Liberation Army's financial troubles. Come, <value:$28>. Let us return and make our report.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. To think so much was amassed in a place like this. It should ease the Liberation Army's financial troubles considerably. Let's head back and report this at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Impressive. ...We're going back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Whoa! This is incredible. This much ought to do the trick. Let's go back and report it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. Come, let us return and report it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Ugh! Being confronted with this mass of vile human greed is making my eyes hurt! I don't want to stay here another second!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20The hidden treasure has been found. Back to report it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23One might expect as much from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure... Quite a hoard. Come, let us return.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27This is incredible... This should be more than enough to resolve the Liberation Army's financial troubles. Let's return and report it at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. Come, let us return and report it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24You'd expect no less from the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. Let's return and report it at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Amazing... This should be enough. Let's hurry back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21That is an impressive amount. This should resolve the Liberation Army's financial troubles. Come, <value:$28>. We're going back to report this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Let's go back and report this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Th-this is incredible. I never imagined there would be so much... Let's go back and report this at once.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Such a vast accumulation of vanity and greed... How absurd. Come, let us hurry back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191It certainly lives up to being the Liberdam merchants' hidden treasure. The sight of it is enough to make my eyes hurt. Let's go back and report this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Now this is a surprise. This much should resolve the Liberation Army's financial troubles. Back to report it.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2이건… 대단하네. 역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금답다. 바로 돌아가 보고하자. …슬쩍하면 안 돼.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이건 대단하군. 역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이야. 이 정도면 해방군 재정도 많이 나아지겠지. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4후후. 이 정도면 1할이나 2할 슬쩍해도 모르겠지. 어쩔까… 챙겨야지! 꺅… 농담이에요. <value:$28> 눈이 진심이야….<end><if><value:$01><equal>5우와, 대단한데. 이 정도면 충분해. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이라 대단하네. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이네. 이 정도면 해방군의 재정난도 해결될 거야. 자, <value:$28>. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이군. 이런 곳에 이렇게 많이 모아뒀다니. 해방군 재정난도 어느 정도 해결되겠어. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13대단하군. …돌아간다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10우와, 대단한데. 이 정도면 충분해. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이다. 자, 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17우웩! 천박한 인간의 욕망 덩어리를 보니 눈이 아파! 단 1초도 있고 싶지 않아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…매장금은 찾았다. 돌아가 보고한다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이라 해야 하나… 이렇게 모으다니 대단하군. 자, 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이건 대단해…. 이걸로 해방군의 재정난도 해결될 거야. 바로 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이군. 자, 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금이네요. 바로 돌아가 보고하죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25대단해… 이 정도면 충분하겠지. 빨리 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21정말 많은 양이군. 이 정도면 해방군 재정난도 해결되겠지. 자, 돌아가 보고한다, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192돌아가 보고하죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193너, 너무 많아요. 설마 이 정도일 줄은…. 바로 돌아가 보고해요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194엄청난 양의 허영과 탐욕…. 시시하군. 자, 빨리 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191역시 리벨덤 상인의 매장금답네. 눈이 아플 정도야. 돌아가 보고하자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190놀랍군. 이 정도면 해방군의 재정난도 해결되겠어. 돌아가 보고한다.<end>"

## 1880025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2こら、<value:$28>。<line-break>何をしてるのよ。<line-break>さっさと戻るわよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3何をしておるんじゃ。<line-break>さっさと戻るぞ、<value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>ったら<line-break>やっぱりねらってたのね。<line-break>私にも３割くらいくれないかな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5何してんだよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まさか、ネコババする気じゃ<line-break>ないだろうな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7何をしているの。<line-break>さっさと行くわよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>。<line-break>…まさか、少しくらいとっても<line-break>平気なんて思ってないわよね？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>12おいおい、<value:$28>。<line-break>少しくらいなら、くすねても大丈夫<line-break>なんて思ってないだろうな？<line-break>滅多なことするもんじゃないぜ。<line-break>世の中には信用ってもんがある。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…何をしている。<line-break>早く行くぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10何してんだよ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>まさか、ネコババしようってんじゃ<line-break>ないだろうな？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14何をしとるんじゃ。<line-break>よもや、多少なりともくすねようと<line-break>思ってはおるまいな？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15何してるの、<value:$28>？<line-break>ま・さ・か、ネコババしようなんて<line-break>思ってないよね？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17アタクシの崇高な時間はそんなはしたな<line-break>額で買えるようなものではなくてよ。<line-break>さっさと帰るのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ちょっとぉ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ま・さ・か、少しくらいなら取っても<line-break>ばれないなんて思ってるのぉ？<line-break>そんなことしてると、<line-break>ルルアンタ、怒っちゃうよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…何をしている。<line-break>早く戻るぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21何をしている。<line-break>下らぬことをするな。 <end>…ひょっとして、<line-break>少しくらい取ったってばれない<line-break>なんて、考えてないよね？<line-break>そういうことしてると<line-break>信用なくすもんね。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Hey, <value:$28>. What are you doing? Come on, we're going back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3What are you doing? Come, <value:$28>. We're going back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>, so you were after it after all. Think you could give me about thirty percent?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5What are you doing, <value:$28>? You're not thinking of pocketing some, are you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7What are you doing? Come on, we're leaving.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>... You aren't thinking a little would be fine, are you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Come on, <value:$28>. You aren't thinking it'd be all right to skim off a little, are you? Don't do anything rash. Trust matters in this world.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13...What are you doing? Let's go.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10What are you doing, sis? You're not planning to pocket any of it, are you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What are you doing? Surely you are not thinking of pocketing even a little?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15What are you doing, <value:$28>? You couldn't possibly be thinking of pocketing some, could you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17My precious time cannot be bought for such a paltry sum. Hurry up, we're leaving!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey, <value:$28>. You aren't thinking no one would notice if you took a little, are you? Lulluantha will get angry if you do that.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...What are you doing? We're going back.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21What are you doing? Do not waste time on such foolishness.<end>...You aren't thinking no one would notice if you took a little, are you? Doing that will cost you people's trust.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2야, <value:$28>. 뭐 하는 거야. 빨리 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3뭐 하는 게냐. 얼른 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4<value:$28>, 역시 노리고 있었구나. 나한테도 3할 정도 주면 안 될까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>. 설마 슬쩍 챙기려는 건 아니겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7뭐 하는 거야. 얼른 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>. …설마 조금쯤 가져가도 괜찮다고 생각하는 건 아니지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12야야, <value:$28>. 조금쯤 슬쩍해도 괜찮다고 생각하는 건 아니지? 함부로 그러는 거 아니야. 세상엔 신용이란 게 있어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…뭐 하는 거냐. 빨리 가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10뭐 하는 거야, 누나. 설마 슬쩍 챙기려는 건 아니겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14뭐 하는 게냐. 설마 조금이라도 슬쩍할 생각은 아니겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>? 서·설·마, 슬쩍 챙기려는 건 아니지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17이 몸의 고귀한 시간은 그런 푼돈으로 살 수 있는 게 아니라고요. 빨리 돌아가는 거예요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19잠깐, <value:$28>. 서·설·마, 조금쯤 가져가도 안 들킬 거라고 생각하는 거야? 그러다 루루안타 화낼 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…뭐 하는 거냐. 빨리 돌아가자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21뭐 하는 거냐. 쓸데없는 짓 하지 마라.<end>…혹시 조금쯤 가져가도 안 들킬 거라고 생각하는 건 아니지? 그런 짓 하면 신용 잃는다고.<end>"

## 1880026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>何をしてるの？<line-break>早く行こうよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what are you doing? Come on, let's go.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 뭐 하고 있어? 빨리 가자.<end>"

## 1880027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、<value:$28>、<line-break>なにしてんの？　早く行こうよ。　<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、ネコババしようってんじゃ<line-break>ないよね？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, what are you doing? Come on, let's go. You're not planning to pocket anything, are you?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, <value:$28>, 뭐 하는 거야? 빨리 가자. 설마 슬쩍 챙기려는 건 아니지?<end>"

## 1880028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "埋蔵金が見つかったのですね？<line-break>それで、首尾はどうでした？<end>"
EN: "The buried treasure has been found, then? How did it go?<end>"
KO: "매장금을 찾으셨군요? 그래서 결과는 어땠습니까?<end>"

## 1880029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと、それほどの大金が！<line-break>これで解放軍も息を吹き返す。<end>"
EN: "That much? What a fortune! This will put the Liberation Army back on its feet.<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 그렇게 큰돈이! 이걸로 해방군도 다시 숨을 돌릴 수 있겠군.<end>"

## 1880030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それではさっそく<line-break>取りにゆかねばなりませんね。<end>"
EN: "Then it must be retrieved at once.<end>"
KO: "그럼 바로 가지러 가야겠군요.<end>"

## 1880031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、すぐに埋蔵金の回収作業に<line-break>移るように手配してください。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Please arrange for the recovery operation to begin immediately.<end>"
KO: "네, 바로 매장금 회수 작업에 들어가도록 준비해 주세요.<end>"

## 1880032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。それではさっそく！<end>"
EN: "Understood. Right away!<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 그럼 바로!<end>"

## 1880033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>その…ご苦労でしたね。<line-break>これで解放軍も、もち直します。<line-break>ありがとう…。<line-break>私を助けてくれて…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Um... you did well. This will put the Liberation Army back on its feet. Thank you... for helping me...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그… 수고했어요. 이걸로 해방군도 다시 일어설 겁니다. 고마워요…. 저를 구해 줘서….<end>"

## 1880034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、言っておきますけど、<line-break>今のはあくまで、解放軍を率いる身<line-break>として言ったのですよ。<line-break>そこのところを間違えないでね。<line-break>…もう、下がっていいですよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, but just so we're clear, I only said that in my capacity as leader of the Liberation Army. Don't get the wrong idea. You may go now.<end>"
KO: "아, 말해두겠지만 방금 건 어디까지나 해방군을 이끄는 입장에서 한 말이에요. 그 점 착각하지 마세요. …이제 물러가도 됩니다.<end>"

## 1880035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが見つけてきてくれた埋蔵金は<line-break>わたくしが責任を持って解放軍の<line-break>活動に役立てます。<line-break>任務、ご苦労でした。<line-break>下がっていいですよ。<end>"
EN: "I will personally see to it that the treasure you found is put to good use by the Liberation Army. Good work on the mission. You may go.<end>"
KO: "당신이 찾아온 매장금은 제가 책임지고 해방군 활동에 쓰겠습니다. 임무 수고하셨습니다. 물러가셔도 좋습니다.<end>"

## 1880036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたが来るのを待っていました。<line-break>話があります。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. I've been waiting for you. We need to talk.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 당신이 오길 기다리고 있었습니다. 할 이야기가 있습니다.<end>"

## 1880037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルのカルラ軍が<line-break>ロストールに攻め込む動きがあるのは　<line-break>知っていますね？<line-break>ロストールも今の戦力では苦しいはず。<line-break>そこで、我々解放軍がカルラ軍の後方を<line-break>かく乱し、援護しようと思います。<end>"
EN: "You know that Karula's Army in Dyneskal is preparing to invade Rostorl, don't you? Rostorl will have a hard time holding out with its current forces. So the Liberation Army intends to support Rostorl by disrupting Karula's rear.<end>"
KO: "딘갈의 카를라군이 로스톨로 쳐들어올 움직임이 있다는 건 알고 계시죠? 로스톨도 지금 전력으로는 버거울 겁니다. 그래서 우리 해방군이 카를라군 후방을 교란해 지원하려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1880038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、この作戦を実行に移すために<line-break>ロストールのエリス王妃に資金援助を<line-break>要請するのがあなたの役目です。<line-break>行ってくれますね？<end>"
EN: "To put this operation into action, your task is to ask Queen Eris of Rostorl for financial support. You'll go, won't you?<end>"
KO: "그래서 이 작전을 실행하기 위해 로스톨의 에리스 왕비에게 자금 지원을 요청하는 것이 당신의 역할입니다. 가 주시겠죠?<end>"

## 1880039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "資金援助の要請に行く<end>"
EN: "Go ask for financial aid<end>"
KO: "자금 지원 요청하러 간다<end>"

## 1880040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かない<end>"
EN: "Refuse to go<end>"
KO: "안 간다<end>"

## 1880041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王家まで滅んでしまっては、<line-break>解放軍は後ろ盾を失ってしまいます。<line-break>頼みましたよ。<end>"
EN: "If the Rostorl Royal House falls as well, the Liberation Army will lose its patron. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕가마저 멸망하면 해방군은 후원자를 잃고 맙니다. 부탁드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1880042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王家の存亡は、<line-break>我々解放軍にとっても死活問題です。<line-break>くれぐれも失敗しないように。<end>"
EN: "The survival of the Rostorl Royal House is a matter of life and death for us in the Liberation Army as well. Make certain you do not fail.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕가의 존망은 우리 해방군에게도 사활이 걸린 문제입니다. 절대 실패하지 않도록 하십시오.<end>"

## 1880043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>仕方ありません、他の人に<line-break>行ってもらうことにします。　<line-break>あなたへの用はそれだけです。<line-break>もう、下がってください。<end>"
EN: "I see. Very well, I'll send someone else. That was all I needed from you. You are dismissed.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 어쩔 수 없군요, 다른 사람에게 가 달라고 하겠습니다. 당신에게 볼일은 그게 전부입니다. 이제 물러가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたへの用はもうありません。<line-break>下がってください。<end>"
EN: "I have no further need of you. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "당신께 더 볼일은 없습니다. 물러가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>今戻ったか。クリュセイス様が<line-break>お待ちかねだ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. You're back. Lady Khryuseis has been waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 이제 돌아왔나. 크류세이스 님이 기다리고 계신다.<end>"

## 1880046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス王妃への援助の要請は<line-break>うまくいったようですね。<line-break>王家より活動資金が届いています。　<line-break>実は、あなたがロストールへ向かって<line-break>数日後に、王家から使者が来ました。<line-break>後方かく乱の件は無用だと。<end>"
EN: "It seems your appeal to Queen Eris was successful. Funding has arrived from the royal house. In fact, a royal messenger came a few days after you left for Rostorl. We were told there was no longer any need to disrupt the enemy's rear.<end>"
KO: "에리스 왕비께 지원을 요청한 건 잘된 모양입니다. 왕가에서 활동 자금이 왔어요. 실은 당신이 로스톨로 떠난 며칠 뒤 왕가의 사자가 와서 후방 교란 건은 필요 없다고 했습니다.<end>"

## 1880047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことかと尋ねたのですが、<line-break>作戦上の秘密だからと、<line-break>教えてくれませんでした。<line-break>ただ、活動資金は送ると。<line-break>…いったい、何があったのです？<end>"
EN: "I asked why, but the messenger refused to say, claiming it was a military secret. I was told only that we would receive the funding. What in the world happened?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일인지 물어봤지만, 작전상의 비밀이라며알려 주지 않았습니다. 다만 활동 자금은 보내겠다고.…대체 무슨 일이 있었던 겁니까?<end>"

## 1880048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍に参戦したと言う<end>"
EN: "Say that I joined Rostorl's army<end>"
KO: "로스톨군에 참전했다고 말한다<end>"

## 1880049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "参戦したことは伏せておく<end>"
EN: "Keep quiet about having joined the fighting<end>"
KO: "참전한 사실은 숨겨 둔다<end>"

## 1880050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やはりそうだったのですか。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>実は、すでにロストール軍の中に、<line-break>あなたに似た将軍がいたと報告を<line-break>受けていました。それで、もしやと…。<end>"
EN: "So it was true. I had already received reports of a general in Rostorl's army who resembled you. I wondered if perhaps...<end>"
KO: "…역시 그랬군요. 사실 이미 로스톨군 안에 당신과 닮은 장군이 있다는 보고를 받았습니다. 그래서 혹시나 하고….<end>"

## 1880051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…解放軍のために命を懸けて<line-break>くださったのですね。<line-break>解放軍を率いる者として礼を言います。<line-break>本当にありがとう。<line-break>…それと、ごめんなさい。<line-break>危険な目にあわせてしまって…。<end>"
EN: "You risked your life for the Liberation Army. As its leader, you have my thanks. Thank you, truly. And... I'm sorry. I put you in such danger.<end>"
KO: "…해방군을 위해 목숨을 거셨군요. 해방군을 이끄는 사람으로서 감사드립니다. 정말 고마워요. …그리고 미안해요. 위험한 일을 겪게 해서….<end>"

## 1880052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の任務では<line-break>本当にご苦労さまでした。<line-break>ゆっくり休んでください。　　<line-break>もう下がっていいですよ。<line-break>…それと、<line-break>みなさんも下がってください。<end>"
EN: "You've worked very hard on this mission. Please get some rest. You may go now. And the rest of you may leave as well.<end>"
KO: "이번 임무 정말 수고 많으셨습니다. 푹 쉬세요. 이제 물러가셔도 됩니다. …그리고 여러분도 물러가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍を率いる者として…だなんて…<line-break>わたくし、どうして、<line-break>素直に言えないの？<line-break>本当にわたくし、<line-break>かわいげのない女だわ…。<line-break>でも…。<end>"
EN: "“As leader of the Liberation Army”... Why can't I just say what I mean? I'm really not a very likable woman, am I? But...<end>"
KO: "해방군을 이끄는 자로서…라니…. 나는 왜 솔직하게 말하지 못하는 거지? 정말 나는 귀염성 없는 여자야…. 하지만….<end>"

## 1880054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、ロストール軍の中に、<line-break>あなたに似た将軍がいたと報告を<line-break>受けています。<line-break>資金を援助するかわりに<line-break>ロストール軍に参加するように<line-break>言われたのではないのですか？<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I've received reports of a general in Rostorl's army who resembled you. Were you told to join their army in exchange for the funding?<end>"
KO: "사실 로스톨군 안에 당신과 닮은 장군이 있었다는 보고를 받았습니다. 자금을 지원하는 대신 로스톨군에 참가하라는 말을 들은 것 아닙니까?<end>"

## 1880055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、言ってくれないのです。<line-break>本当のことを言ってください。<line-break>これは命令ですよ。<end>"
EN: "Why won't you tell me? Tell me the truth. That's an order.<end>"
KO: "왜 말씀해 주지 않는 겁니까. 사실대로 말해 주세요. 명령입니다.<end>"

## 1880056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やはり、そうだったのですか。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、なぜ隠していたのです？<line-break>あなたはわたくしに報告する義務が<line-break>あるはずですよ。<end>"
EN: "So it was true. But why did you hide it from me? You are obliged to report such things to me.<end>"
KO: "…역시 그랬군요. 하지만 왜 숨기고 있었죠? 당신에겐 제게 보고할 의무가 있었을 텐데요.<end>"

## 1880057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わざわざ報告する必要はない<end>"
EN: "There was no need to report it<end>"
KO: "일일이 보고할 필요는 없어<end>"

## 1880058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余計な心配をさせたくない<end>"
EN: "I didn't want to cause needless worry<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 걱정을 끼치고 싶지 않아<end>"

## 1880059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはないわ。<line-break>わたくしはあなたが属する組織の<line-break>指導者なのですよ。<line-break>部下が何をしているのか<line-break>把握していないなんて、<line-break>指導者として失格じゃない。<end>"
EN: "Of course you needed to tell me. I am the leader of the organization you belong to. If I don't even know what my own subordinates are doing, then I'm unfit to lead.<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아요. 저는 당신이 속한 조직의 지도자랍니다. 부하가 무엇을 하는지 파악하지 못한다면 지도자로서 실격이잖아요.<end>"

## 1880060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが命をかけて、援助の要請を<line-break>果たしてくれたことには、<line-break>本当に感謝しています。<line-break>でも、今後はこのような単独行動は<line-break>慎んでいただきたいわ。<line-break>よろしくて？<end>"
EN: "I am truly grateful that you risked your life to secure the aid we needed. But from now on, I must ask you not to act alone like this. Is that understood?<end>"
KO: "목숨을 걸고 지원 요청을 완수해 준 것은 정말 고맙게 생각합니다. 하지만 앞으로는 이런 단독 행동은 삼가 주세요. 알겠죠?<end>"

## 1880061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今のは、言い過ぎでした。<line-break>解放軍のために命を懸けてくれたのに<line-break>あんなことを言って。<line-break>今回の任務は本当にご苦労でした。<line-break>ゆっくり休んでください。<line-break>…みなさんも下がってください。<end>"
EN: "I went too far just now. You risked your life for the Liberation Army, and I said such things to you. You've worked very hard on this mission. Please get some rest. The rest of you may leave as well.<end>"
KO: "…방금 말은 지나쳤습니다. 해방군을 위해 목숨까지 걸어 주셨는데 그런 말을 하다니. 이번 임무는 정말 수고 많으셨습니다. 푹 쉬어 주세요. …여러분도 물러나 주세요.<end>"

## 1880062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…心配させたくなかったですって？　　<line-break>冗談ではないわ！<line-break>あなたは組織のメンバーなのよ！<line-break>…わたくしの組織のメンバーが<line-break>戦場に出ているというのに、わたくしは<line-break>知らないなんて…バカみたいじゃない。<end>"
EN: "You didn't want me to worry? Don't be ridiculous! You are a member of this organization! One of my own people was out on a battlefield, and I knew nothing about it. I look like a complete fool.<end>"
KO: "…걱정시키고 싶지 않았다고요? 농담하지 마세요! 당신은 조직의 일원이에요! …제 조직의 일원이 전장에 나가 있는데 제가 모르고 있다니… 바보 같잖아요.<end>"

## 1880063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい。言い過ぎました。<line-break>でも、今後はわたくしへの報告なしに<line-break>危険なことはしないように。<line-break>今日はゆっくり休んでください。<line-break>ご苦労様でした。<line-break>…みなさんも下がってください。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I went too far. But from now on, you are not to do anything dangerous without reporting to me first. Get some rest today. You've earned it. The rest of you may leave as well.<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다. 말이 지나쳤어요. 하지만 앞으로는 제게 보고도 없이 위험한 일은 하지 마세요. 오늘은 푹 쉬세요. 수고하셨습니다. …여러분도 물러가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたくし、<line-break>どうして素直に言えないの？　<line-break>…ありがとうって。<line-break>わたくしのために戦ってくれて<line-break>いるのに…、わたくしのために<line-break>気を使ってくれているのに…。<end>"
EN: "Why can't I simply say what I mean? Why can't I just say thank you? You're fighting for me, and trying so hard not to worry me...<end>"
KO: "…저, 왜 솔직하게 말하지 못하는 걸까요? …고맙다고. 저를 위해 싸워 주고 있는데…, 저를 위해 신경 써 주고 있는데….<end>"

## 1880065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなことでは、わたくし<line-break>どんどん<value:$28>に<line-break>嫌われてしまう…。<end>"
EN: "If I keep behaving like this, <value:$28> will like me less and less...<end>"
KO: "이러다간 저, 점점 <value:$28>에게 미움받고 말겠어요….<end>"

## 1880066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を考えているの、クリュセイス？<line-break><value:$28>が戦っているのは<line-break>解放軍のためなのよ。<line-break>そうに決まっている。<line-break>そうに…。<end>"
EN: "What are you thinking, Khryuseis? <value:$28> is fighting for the Liberation Army. Of course that's the reason. It must be...<end>"
KO: "무슨 생각이야, 크류세이스? <value:$28>이 싸우는 건 해방군을 위해서야. 분명 그래. 분명….<end>"

## 1880067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2どうしたのです、<value:$28>？<line-break>あなたには埋蔵金を調査する任務が<line-break>あるはずですよ。<line-break>こんなところで、<line-break>ぐずぐずしていられる時間は<line-break>ないのではないかしら？<end><value:$28>、<line-break>埋蔵金の調査を急いでください。<line-break>　<line-break>わたくしたち解放軍の活動には、<line-break>どうしても資金が必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2What are you doing here, <value:$28>? You have a cache of treasure to investigate. Surely you have no time to dawdle here?<end><value:$28>, please hurry with the investigation of the treasure cache. The Liberation Army desperately needs funds to continue its work.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2왜 그러시죠, <value:$28>? 당신에게는 매장금을 조사할 임무가 있잖아요. 이런 곳에서 꾸물거릴 시간은 없는 것 아닌가요?<end><value:$28>, 매장금 조사를 서둘러 주세요. 저희 해방군의 활동에는 아무래도 자금이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1880068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3え？　解放軍はどうしてそんなに　　<line-break>資金が必要なのか、ですって？<line-break>　<line-break>組織の運営にはそれなりの資金が<line-break>いるのです。わたくしの運営の仕方に<line-break>問題があると言いたいのですか？<end>資金もそうですけど、人員の不足も<line-break>解放軍の深刻な悩みです。リベルダム　<line-break>陥落の際、大勢の仲間を失いました。<line-break>ですから、今後もあなたを頼ってしまう<line-break>かもしれませんね。<line-break>…解放軍の指導者として、ですが。<end>あなたの見つけてきてくれた埋蔵金の<line-break>おかげで、解放軍も少しは<line-break>持ち直しそうです。<line-break>ですが、資金があるにこしたことは<line-break>ありません。今後も解放軍の仲間には<line-break>節制してもらわないと。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3What? Why does the Liberation Army need so much money? Any organization requires a certain amount of funding to operate. Are you suggesting there is something wrong with the way I manage it?<end>Funds are not our only concern. The Liberation Army is also desperately short of personnel. We lost many of our comrades when Liberdam fell. I may have to call upon you again in the future―as leader of the Liberation Army, of course.<end>Thanks to the treasure you found, the Liberation Army should be able to regain some of its footing. Still, one can never have too much funding. Our comrades will have to remain frugal.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3네? 해방군은 왜 그렇게 많은 자금이 필요하냐고요? 조직을 운영하려면 그만한 자금이 필요합니다. 제 운영 방식에 문제가 있다고 말씀하고 싶은 건가요?<end>자금도 그렇지만 인원 부족도 해방군의 심각한 고민입니다. 리벨덤 함락 때 많은 동료를 잃었습니다. 그러니 앞으로도 당신에게 의지하게 될지도 모르겠네요. …해방군의 지도자로서 말이에요.<end>당신이 찾아 준 매장금 덕분에 해방군도 조금은 회복할 것 같습니다. 하지만 자금은 많을수록 좋지요. 앞으로도 해방군 동료들에게 절약해 달라고 해야겠어요.<end>"

## 1880069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>早く、エリス王妃の元へ　　　<line-break>向かってください。<line-break>この作戦は時期を逃しては…。<line-break>とにかく、急いでください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please go to Queen Eris at once. If we miss our chance, this operation will... Never mind. Just hurry.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 서둘러 에리스 왕비에게 가 주세요. 이 작전은 때를 놓치면…. 아무튼 서둘러 주세요.<end>"

## 1880070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>エリス王妃への援助要請の件は<line-break>どうなったのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what became of our request for aid from Queen Eris?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 에리스 왕비께 원조를 요청한 건은 어떻게 됐습니까?<end>"

## 1880071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。王妃は<line-break><value:$28>にロストール軍への<line-break>参加を求めてきたのですか…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So the queen asked <value:$28> to join Rostorl's army...<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 왕비가 <value:$28>에게 로스톨군 참가를 요청한 것이군요….<end>"

## 1880072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>どうしてロストール軍への参加を　　<line-break>拒否したんですか？<line-break>ロストール軍に参加して<line-break>それなりの功績を挙げられれば<line-break>援助を承知してくれたんでしょう？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, why did you refuse to join Rostorl's army? If you had joined and distinguished yourself, the queen would have agreed to help us, wouldn't she?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 왜 로스톨군 참가를 거절하셨어요? 로스톨군에 참가해서 어느 정도 공적을 세우면 지원을 승인해 줬을 거잖아요?<end>"

## 1880073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやめなさい。わたくしが与えた<line-break>任務は、わたくしの提案を王妃に<line-break>伝えることです。<line-break>王妃がそれを聞き入れなかった<line-break>というのでは、仕方ありません。<end>"
EN: "That is enough. The task I gave was simply to convey my proposal to the queen. If she would not accept it, then there was nothing more to be done.<end>"
KO: "그만두세요. 제가 드린 임무는 제 제안을 왕비에게 전하는 것입니다. 왕비께서 받아들이지 않으셨다면 어쩔 수 없지요.<end>"

## 1880074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうでした。申しわけありません。<line-break>クリュセイス様、<line-break>それと、<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're right. Please forgive me, Lady Khryuseis―and you as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 그랬죠. 죄송합니다. 크류세이스 님, 그리고 <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1880075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>ご苦労様でした。王家にはあらためて<line-break>別の打診をしてみます。<line-break>今日は下がっていいですよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for your efforts, <value:$28>. I will approach the royal house again with a different proposal. You may leave for the day.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 수고하셨습니다. 왕가에는 다시 다른 제안을 해 보겠습니다. 오늘은 돌아가셔도 됩니다.<end>"

## 1880076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3今は特にあなたに頼まなければ　　<line-break>ならない任務はありません。<line-break>　<line-break>わたくしには、あなたと悠長に<line-break>お話をしている時間はないのです。<line-break>下がっていていただけるかしら？<end>何をしているのか、ですって？<line-break>ディンガル軍の動きを探らせています。<line-break>その報告を待っているのです。<line-break>ロストールをリベルダムの二の舞に<line-break>させるわけにはいきません。あなたも<line-break>軍の動向には注意してください。<end>今はうかつには動けません。<line-break>解放軍は、壊滅を免れたとはいえ、<line-break>リベルダムで多くの仲間を失いました。<line-break>これまで以上に慎重に時期を選び、<line-break>ディンガルに気取られないように<line-break>動かなくては。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3I have no particular missions for you at present. Nor do I have time for a leisurely conversation with you. Would you kindly leave?<end>You ask what I'm doing? I'm having the Dyneskal Army's movements investigated, and I'm waiting for the report. We cannot let Rostorl suffer the same fate as Liberdam. You should keep a close eye on the army's movements as well.<end>We cannot afford to act carelessly now. Though the Liberation Army escaped destruction, we lost many comrades at Liberdam. We must choose our moment more carefully than ever and act without being detected by Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3지금은 특별히 당신에게 부탁할 임무가 없습니다. 저에게는 당신과 한가롭게 이야기할 시간도 없어요. 물러나 주시겠어요?<end>무엇을 하고 있느냐고요? 딩갈군의 움직임을 조사하게 했습니다. 그 보고를 기다리고 있어요. 로스톨을 리벨덤의 전철을 밟게 할 수는 없습니다. 당신도 군의 동향을 주의해 주세요.<end>지금은 섣불리 움직일 수 없습니다. 해방군은 괴멸을 면했다 해도 리벨덤에서 많은 동료를 잃었습니다. 전보다 더 신중하게 시기를 고르고, 딩갈에 눈치채이지 않도록 움직여야 합니다.<end>"

## 1880077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>その、怪我はありませんか？　　　　<line-break><value:$28>は無理をするから。<line-break>…変なことを言ってごめんなさい。<line-break>今のわたくしには、<value:$28><line-break>しかいないから…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. You aren't hurt, are you? You always push yourself too hard, <value:$28>. Forgive me. I shouldn't say such things. It's just that now, <value:$28> is all I have...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 그, 다친 곳은 없나요? <value:$28>는 무리를 하니까요. …이상한 말 해서 미안해요. 지금의 저에겐 <value:$28>밖에 없어서….<end>"

## 1880078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、わたくしには<line-break>これ以上大切な人を失うことに<line-break>耐えられそうもありませんもの。<line-break>あなたのことを頼ってしまった<line-break>今のわたくしには…。<end>"
EN: "I don't think I could bear to lose anyone else dear to me. Not now that I've come to rely on you...<end>"
KO: "저는 이제 더 이상 소중한 사람을 잃는 걸 견딜 수 없을 것 같아요. 당신께 의지해 버린 지금의 저는….<end>"

## 1880079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>わたくし、この前、リベルダムの　　　<line-break>難民のもとへ行ってきました。<line-break>実際に彼らの暮らしにふれる必要がある<line-break>と思ったのです。これまでは、資金と<line-break>物資の工面をするだけでしたから。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I recently went to visit the refugees from Liberdam. I felt I needed to see how they actually lived. Until then, all I had done was arrange funds and supplies.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 저는 지난번에 리벨덤 난민들을 찾아갔습니다. 실제로 그들의 생활을 접할 필요가 있다고 생각했어요. 지금까지는 자금과 물자를 마련하는 것뿐이었으니까요.<end>"

## 1880080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、初めて知りました。　　　　　<line-break>人間の生きる力の強さを。<line-break>その姿の美しさを。<line-break>いえ、今まで見ようとしなかっただけ<line-break>かもしれませんね。わたくし、非市民と<line-break>聞くだけで、ゆがんで見てましたから。<end>"
EN: "It was there that I first understood the strength of the human will to live, and the beauty of it. No... perhaps I had simply refused to see it before. The mere word “noncitizen” was enough to distort my view of them.<end>"
KO: "그때 처음 알았습니다. 인간이 살아가는 힘이 얼마나 강한지, 그 모습이 얼마나 아름다운지를요. 아니, 지금까지 제가 보려 하지 않았던 것뿐일지도 모르겠네요. 저는 비시민이라고만 들으면 삐딱하게 봤으니까요.<end>"

## 1880081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外見は、はっきり言って昔のスラムと<line-break>同じかそれ以上に不衛生だけど、<line-break>その中で生きていく力を失わない人々。<line-break>逆に教えられました。生きていくことの<line-break>大切さを…こんな風に考えられるのも、<line-break>あなたのおかげですね。<end>"
EN: "To be frank, the place is as unsanitary as the old Slums were, if not worse. Yet the people there have not lost their will to survive. They were the ones who taught me how precious it is simply to live. And I can think this way now because of you.<end>"
KO: "겉보기엔 솔직히 예전 슬럼과 같거나 더 비위생적이지만, 그 안에서도 살아갈 힘을 잃지 않는 사람들이 있어요. 오히려 제가 배웠습니다. 살아간다는 것의 소중함을…. 이렇게 생각할 수 있게 된 것도 당신 덕분이군요.<end>"

## 1880082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしの心の窓を開けてくれたのは<line-break>あなたの優しさですもの。<end>"
EN: "It was your kindness that opened the window of my heart.<end>"
KO: "제 마음의 창을 열어 준 건 당신의 다정함이니까요.<end>"

## 1880083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1880084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくし、<value:$28>には<line-break>ずっとそばにいてほしいのです。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…でも、それはわたくしのわがまま。<line-break>そんなもので、あなたを<line-break>縛っておくことなどできません。<end>"
EN: "I want you to stay by my side forever, <value:$28>. But that is only my selfish wish. I cannot use it to bind you to me.<end>"
KO: "저는 <value:$28>가 계속 곁에 있어 줬으면 해요. …하지만 그건 제 욕심. 그런 걸로 당신을 묶어 둘 수는 없어요.<end>"

## 1880085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、これだけは覚えていてください。<line-break>ここには、あなたを待っている<line-break>わたくしがいることを…。<line-break>どこに行こうとも、必ず<line-break>わたくしのもとへ帰ってきてください。<end>"
EN: "But please remember this much: I will be here, waiting for you. Wherever you go, please always come back to me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이것만은 기억해 주세요. 여기에는 당신을 기다리는 제가 있다는 것을…. 어디를 가더라도 반드시 제게 돌아와 주세요.<end>"

## 1880086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あのとき、<line-break>あなたは言ってくれましたね。<line-break>わたくしのこと、放っておけないと。<line-break>わたくし、今でも覚えています。<line-break>あなたがそんな風に言ってくれて<line-break>本当にうれしかったですから。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you told me then that you couldn't simply abandon me. I still remember. Hearing you say that made me truly happy.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그때 당신이 말해 주셨지요. 저를 그냥 두고 볼 수 없다고. 저는 지금도 기억하고 있습니다. 당신이 그렇게 말해 주셔서 정말 기뻤으니까요.<end>"

## 1880087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0わたくしは卑怯でした。<line-break>あなたにぬれぎぬを着せたのに、<line-break>…あなたを愛してしまった。<line-break>…ずっと素直になれなかった。<line-break>あなたに許してもらう勇気が、<line-break>わたくしにはなかったのです。<end>わたくしは卑怯でした。<line-break>あなたにぬれぎぬを着せたのに、<line-break>…あなたを頼ってしまった。<line-break>…ずっと素直になれなかった。<line-break>あなたに許してもらう勇気が、<line-break>わたくしにはなかったのです。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0I was a coward. Even after I falsely accused you... I fell in love with you. I could never bring myself to be honest. I lacked the courage to ask your forgiveness.<end>I was a coward. Even after I falsely accused you... I came to rely on you. I could never bring myself to be honest. I lacked the courage to ask your forgiveness.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0저는 비겁했습니다. 당신에게 누명을 씌워 놓고도… 당신을 사랑하게 됐습니다. …계속 솔직해지지 못했습니다. 당신에게 용서받을 용기가 제게는 없었습니다.<end>저는 비겁했습니다. 당신에게 누명을 씌워 놓고도… 당신에게 의지해 버렸습니다. …계속 솔직해지지 못했습니다. 당신에게 용서받을 용기가 제게는 없었습니다.<end>"

## 1880088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、わたくしのこんな気持ちを、<line-break>あなたは一言で解消してくれました。<line-break>放っておけないから、と。<end>"
EN: "But with just a few words, you put all those feelings to rest. You said you couldn't simply abandon me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이런 제 마음을 당신은 한마디로 풀어 주셨습니다. 그냥 두고 볼 수 없으니까, 라고.<end>"

## 1880089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、<value:$28>も<line-break>忘れないでください。あなたのことを<line-break>放っておけないわたくしがいることを。<end>"
EN: "So please remember this too, <value:$28>: I cannot simply abandon you either.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>도 잊지 말아 주세요. 당신을 그냥 내버려 둘 수 없는 제가 있다는 걸.<end>"

## 1880090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>難民の数は膨れ上がる一方です。　<line-break>このままでは、また資金が…。<line-break><value:$28>には、<line-break>１００００ギアほど提供してくれる<line-break>ようなあてはありませんか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, the number of refugees continues to swell. At this rate, we will run short of funds again. Do you know of anyone who might provide around 10,000 Gea, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 난민은 계속 늘어나고 있습니다. 이대로면 또 자금이…. <value:$28>에게 10000기어 정도 제공해 줄 만한 사람이 없습니까?<end>"

## 1880091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "資金を援助する<end>"
EN: "Provide the funds<end>"
KO: "자금을 지원한다<end>"

## 1880092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "期待されても困る<end>"
EN: "Don't expect that from me<end>"
KO: "기대받아도 곤란해<end>"

## 1880093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "資金を援助してくれるのですか？<line-break>あなたが！？<end>"
EN: "You will provide the funds? You?!<end>"
KO: "자금을 지원해 주시는 겁니까? 당신이!?<end>"

## 1880094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…感謝の言葉もありません。<line-break>この資金を必要としている人たちに<line-break>かわってお礼を言わせてください。<line-break>本当にありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I cannot thank you enough. Please allow me to offer my thanks on behalf of all those who need this money. Thank you, truly.<end>"
KO: "…감사의 말씀도 다 못 드리겠습니다. 이 자금을 필요로 하는 사람들을 대신해 감사드립니다. 정말 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1880095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１００００ギアを資金提供した。<end>"
EN: "Provided 10,000 Gea in funding.<end>"
KO: "10000기어를 자금으로 제공했다.<end>"

## 1880096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、わたくし、さっそく<line-break>難民を支援できるように手配を　　<line-break>整えてきます。<line-break><value:$28>、今のわたくしは<line-break>あなただけが頼りです。<line-break>それでは、行ってきます。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall go and make the arrangements to aid the refugees at once. <value:$28>, you are the only person I can rely on now. I must be going.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저는 바로 난민 지원 준비를 하러 가겠습니다. <value:$28>, 지금의 저는 당신만 믿고 있습니다. 그럼 다녀올게요.<end>"

## 1880097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですよね。<line-break>１００００ギアなんて、そんなに簡単に<line-break>手に入れられるわけがありませんね。<end>"
EN: "Of course. There is no easy way to obtain 10,000 Gea.<end>"
KO: "…그렇죠. 10000기어라니 그렇게 간단히 손에 넣을 수 있을 리 없겠죠.<end>"

## 1880098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理なこと言ってしまいましたね。<line-break>…忘れてください。<end>"
EN: "I asked too much of you. Please forget I said anything.<end>"
KO: "무리한 말을 해 버렸네요. …잊어 주세요.<end>"

## 1880100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>恥を忍んで言います。実はまた<line-break>資金が足りなくなりそうなのです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I am ashamed to admit this, but it seems we are about to run short of funds again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…, 부끄럽지만 말씀드리겠습니다. 사실 또 자금이 부족해질 것 같습니다.<end>"

## 1880101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今の解放軍は小さな組織です。<line-break>解放軍の運営だけなら、これまでの　<line-break>資金で問題はありませんでした。<line-break>ですが、問題はロセン、リベルダムの<line-break>難民なのです。彼らの生活を支援する<line-break>ための資金がないのです。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army is a small organization now. The funds we already had would have been enough to maintain the army itself. The problem is the refugees from Rocen and Liberdam. We have no money with which to support them.<end>"
KO: "…지금의 해방군은 작은 조직입니다. 해방군 운영만이라면 지금까지의 자금으로 문제없었습니다. 하지만 문제는 로센과 리벨덤의 난민입니다. 그들의 생활을 지원할 자금이 없습니다.<end>"

## 1880102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ軍はロセンとリベルダムを<line-break>攻略する際、無抵抗の市民は決して<line-break>殺さないように厳命しています。<line-break>そのおかげで多くの命が無意味に<line-break>奪われることなくすみました。<line-break>ですが、そのかわりに…。<end>"
EN: "When taking Rocen and Liberdam, Karula's Army issued strict orders that civilians who did not resist were never to be killed. Thanks to that, many lives were spared a senseless end. But in exchange...<end>"
KO: "카를라군은 로센과 리벨덤을 공략할 때 저항하지 않는 시민은 절대 죽이지 말라고 엄명했습니다. 덕분에 많은 목숨이 무의미하게 희생되지 않았습니다. 하지만 그 대신에….<end>"

## 1880103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "両方の町の郊外には、<line-break>難民があふれているのです。<line-break>彼らを見捨てることはできません。<end>"
EN: "The outskirts of both towns are now overflowing with refugees. I cannot abandon them.<end>"
KO: "두 마을의 교외에는 난민이 넘쳐납니다. 그들을 버릴 수는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1880104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の中には、ディンガル軍への<line-break>抵抗運動よりも、難民の救済に資金が<line-break>流れるのが不満な仲間もいます。<line-break>ですが、わたくしには、ディンガルとの<line-break>戦いのために、難民を放っておくなど<line-break>どうしてもできないのです。<end>"
EN: "Some in the Liberation Army resent seeing funds spent on refugee relief rather than resistance against the Dyneskal Army. But I cannot abandon the refugees merely for the sake of fighting Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "해방군 안에는 딩갈군에 대한 저항보다 난민 구호에 자금이 쓰이는 걸 불만스러워하는 동료도 있습니다. 하지만 저는 딩갈과 싸운다는 이유로 난민을 내버려 둘 수는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1880106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せめて、今あと<line-break>１００００ギアあれば…。<end>"
EN: "If only we had another 10,000 Gea now...<end>"
KO: "적어도 지금 10000기어만 더 있으면….<end>"

## 1880107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "資金を援助する<end>"
EN: "Provide the funds<end>"
KO: "자금을 지원한다<end>"

## 1880108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな大金は渡せない<end>"
EN: "I can't give you that much<end>"
KO: "그런 거액은 줄 수 없다<end>"

## 1880109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　今なんて…？<end>"
EN: "What? What did you just say?<end>"
KO: "어? 방금 뭐라고…?<end>"

## 1880110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あなたが、<line-break>資金を出してくれると言うのですか？<line-break>１００００ギアも！？<end>"
EN: "Y-you mean you will provide the funds? All 10,000 Gea?!<end>"
KO: "다, 당신이 자금을 내 주시겠다고요? 10000기어나!?<end>"

## 1880111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてそれほどの大金を<line-break>そんなに簡単に出してくれるのです？<line-break>解放軍のため？　難民のため？<line-break>それとも、わたくしの…。<end>"
EN: "How can you part with such a great sum so readily? Is it for the Liberation Army? For the refugees? Or for me...<end>"
KO: "어째서 그토록 큰돈을 그렇게 쉽게 내주시는 겁니까? 해방군을 위해서? 난민을 위해서? 아니면, 저를….<end>"

## 1880112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくし、何を言っているのかしら。<line-break><value:$28>は、解放軍の一員<line-break>ですもの、解放軍のためよね。<line-break>でも、あなたはわたくしのやり方に<line-break>賛同してくれるのですね。<line-break>…正直に言って、うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "What am I saying? You are a member of the Liberation Army, <value:$28>, so of course it is for the army. But this means you approve of what I am doing. To be honest, that makes me very happy.<end>"
KO: "제가 무슨 말을 하는 걸까요. <value:$28>는 해방군의 일원이니 해방군을 위해서겠지요. 그래도 당신은 제 방식에 찬성해 주시는군요. …솔직히 기쁩니다.<end>"

## 1880113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの資金提供の申し出、<line-break>ありがたく受けいれさせてもらいます。<line-break>本当にありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I gratefully accept your offer of financial aid. Thank you, truly.<end>"
KO: "당신의 자금 지원 제안, 감사히 받겠습니다. 정말 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1880114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１００００ギアを資金提供した。<end>"
EN: "Provided 10,000 Gea in funding.<end>"
KO: "10000기어를 자금으로 제공했다.<end>"

## 1880115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで、難民の暮らしをいくらか<line-break>楽にしてあげられます。わたくし<line-break>さっそく手はずを整えなくては。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>今日のこと、わたくし<line-break>決して忘れませんから。<end>"
EN: "This will make life a little easier for the refugees. I must begin making arrangements at once. <value:$28>, I will never forget what you have done today.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 난민의 생활을 조금은 편하게 해 줄 수 있겠습니다. 저도 바로 준비를 해야겠군요. <value:$28>, 오늘 일은 절대 잊지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 1880116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余計なことを言ってしまいましたね。<line-break>こんなことを言っていても資金が<line-break>降ってくるわけじゃありませんのに…。<line-break><value:$28>、もう解放軍に<line-break>いても、ディンガルと戦うことは<line-break>できないかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "I should not have said any of that. Talking about it will not make money fall from the sky. <value:$28>, even if you remain with the Liberation Army, you may no longer be able to fight Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 말을 했군요. 이런 말을 해 봐야 자금이 하늘에서 떨어지는 것도 아닌데…. <value:$28>, 이제 해방군에 있어도 딘갈과 싸울 수 없을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1880117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、あなたがディンガルとの戦いを<line-break>望むなら、解放軍を離れた方が<line-break>いいかもしれません。<line-break>わたくしには、そう望む人を<line-break>止める権利はありません。<line-break>…もう、行ってください。<end>"
EN: "If fighting Dyneskal is what you want, perhaps you should leave the Liberation Army. I have no right to stop anyone who wishes to do so. Please, go now.<end>"
KO: "만약 당신이 딘갈과의 싸움을 원한다면 해방군을 떠나는 편이 좋을지도 모릅니다. 저는 그렇게 바라는 사람을 막을 권리가 없습니다. …이제 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1880118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2わたくしが間違っているのかしら？<line-break>あの難民のすべてを救おうなんて<line-break>思い上がりなのかしら？<end>ディンガルをはやく倒すことが<line-break>できれば、これ以上の難民を<line-break>出さずにすむかもしれない。<line-break>でも、今困っている人たちを<line-break>見過ごすことはできません。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Am I wrong? Is it arrogance to think I can save every one of those refugees?<end>If we could defeat Dyneskal quickly, perhaps we could prevent more people from becoming refugees. But I cannot turn away from those who are suffering now.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2제가 틀린 걸까요? 저 난민을 전부 구하겠다는 건 오만한 생각일까요?<end>딘갈을 빨리 무너뜨릴 수 있다면 더 이상의 난민이 생기는 걸 막을 수 있을지도 몰라요. 하지만 지금 곤란한 사람들을 외면할 수는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1880119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>今日はどうしたのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 오늘은 무슨 일이죠?<end>"

## 1880120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難民支援のための<line-break>資金不足のことですか？<line-break>今のところ、どうにか大丈夫です。　<line-break>…でも、<value:$28>はどうして<line-break>そこまで、資金を援助してくれようと<line-break>するのですか？<end>"
EN: "Is this about the shortage of funds for refugee aid? We are managing somehow for now. But why are you willing to provide so much financial help, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "난민 지원을 위한 자금 부족 이야기입니까? 지금은 어떻게든 괜찮습니다. …그런데 <value:$28>은 왜 그렇게까지 자금을 지원하려 하는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1880121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他の解放軍の面々は、<line-break>ディンガルへの憎しみのあまり<line-break>戦うことばかり主張しているのに…。　<line-break>前々から不思議に思っていました。<line-break>あなたはどうして、そこまで<line-break>わたくしの活動を助けてくれるのです？<end>"
EN: "The other members of the Liberation Army are so consumed by their hatred of Dyneskal that all they speak of is fighting. I have wondered about this for some time. Why do you go so far to help me with my work?<end>"
KO: "다른 해방군 사람들은 딘갈을 증오한 나머지 싸우자는 말만 하는데…. 전부터 궁금했습니다. 당신은 왜 이렇게까지 제 활동을 도와주는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1880122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難民が哀れだからですか？<line-break>それとも…<end>"
EN: "Is it because you pity the refugees? Or...<end>"
KO: "난민들이 불쌍해서입니까? 아니면….<end>"

## 1880123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難民を救うのも解放軍の義務だから<end>"
EN: "Because saving refugees is also the Liberation Army's duty<end>"
KO: "난민을 구하는 것도 해방군의 의무니까<end>"

## 1880124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイスを放っておけないから<end>"
EN: "Because I can't just leave Khryuseis alone<end>"
KO: "크류세이스를 그냥 둘 수 없으니까<end>"

## 1880125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…ですよね。<line-break>わたくしの考えは間違っては<line-break>いないのですよね。<line-break>あなたがそう言ってくれて<line-break>わたくしも、少しは気が楽に<line-break>なりました。<end>"
EN: "Yes... of course. Then I am not wrong in what I am doing. Hearing you say so has eased my mind a little.<end>"
KO: "그래… 그렇죠. 제 생각은 틀리지 않은 거죠. 당신이 그렇게 말해 주니 저도 조금은 마음이 편해졌어요.<end>"

## 1880126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、わたくしは…。<end>"
EN: "But I...<end>"
KO: "하지만 저는….<end>"

## 1880127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたくし、これから行かなければ<line-break>いけないところがあったのを<line-break>思い出しました。<line-break>失礼させていただくわ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I just remembered that I have somewhere I must be. Please excuse me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…저, 이제 가 봐야 할 곳이 있었던 걸 떠올렸습니다. 먼저 실례하겠습니다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1880128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（わたくし、何を期待しているの？<line-break>　<value:$28>がわたくしを<line-break>　許してくれるはずないのに…。）<end>"
EN: "(What was I hoping for? <value:$28> could never forgive me...)<end>"
KO: "(나는 뭘 기대하는 거지? <value:$28>이 나를 용서할 리 없는데….)<end>"

## 1880129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　…そんな、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "What? No... surely not...<end>"
KO: "어? …그럴 리가….<end>"

## 1880130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたくし、あなたには　　　　　　<line-break>一生許してもらえないと<line-break>思っていました。<line-break>ぬれぎぬを着せて…あんなにひどい<line-break>仕打ちをしたわたくしには、あなたに<line-break>優しくしてもらう資格はないと…。<end>"
EN: "I thought you would never forgive me. After falsely accusing you and treating you so cruelly, I believed I had no right to receive any kindness from you...<end>"
KO: "…저는 당신에게 평생 용서받지 못할 거라고 생각했어요. 누명을 씌우고… 그렇게 심한 일을 한 제게 당신의 친절을 받을 자격은 없다고….<end>"

## 1880131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、わたくし、あなたを<line-break>解放軍の一員としてしか見ないように<line-break>決めていたのです。<line-break>わたくしを助けてくれるあなたを<line-break>ついつい頼ってしまいそうに<line-break>なるから…。<end>"
EN: "That is why I decided I would see you only as a member of the Liberation Army. With you helping me, I knew I might find myself relying on you...<end>"
KO: "그래서 저는 당신을 해방군의 일원으로만 보기로 마음먹었어요. 저를 도와주는 당신에게 자꾸 기대고 싶어질 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1880132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと、決して<line-break>許されないことなのに…。<line-break>虫がよすぎることなのに…。　　　　　<line-break>でも、お父様を失い、<line-break>おじさまに捨てられたわたくしを<line-break>支えてくれる人は…もういないから…。<end>"
EN: "I could never allow myself to do that. It would be far too selfish. But after losing Father and being cast aside by Uncle, there is no one left to support me...<end>"
KO: "그런 건 절대로 용납될 수 없는데…. 너무 이기적인 일인데…. 하지만 아버님을 잃고 숙부님에게 버림받은 저를 지탱해 줄 사람은… 이제 아무도 없어서….<end>"

## 1880133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>…。<line-break>ごめんなさい、<value:$28>！<line-break>わたくしの愚かさを許してください！<line-break>そして、許されるなら、<line-break>…わたくし、あなたのことを<line-break>好きになってもかまいませんか？<end><value:$28>…。<line-break>ごめんなさい、<value:$28>！<line-break>わたくしの愚かさを許してください！<line-break>そして、許されるなら、<line-break>…わたくし、あなたのことを<line-break>これからも頼ってかまいませんか？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>... I'm sorry, <value:$28>! Please forgive my foolishness! And if I may... may I allow myself to love you?<end><value:$28>... I'm sorry, <value:$28>! Please forgive my foolishness! And if I may... may I continue to rely on you?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>…. 미안해요, <value:$28>! 제 어리석음을 용서해 주세요! 그리고 용서받을 수 있다면… 제가 당신을 좋아해도 괜찮을까요?<end><value:$28>…. 미안해요, <value:$28>! 제 어리석음을 용서해 주세요! 그리고 용서받을 수 있다면… 앞으로도 당신에게 의지해도 괜찮을까요?<end>"

## 1880134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>…。<line-break>これからも、わたくしのそばに<line-break>いてください。<line-break>わたくしを、支えて…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>... Please stay by my side from now on. Please... support me.<end>"
KO: "고마워요, <value:$28>…. 앞으로도 제 곁에 있어 주세요. 저를 지탱해 주세요….<end>"

## 1880135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3クリュセイス様なら<line-break>今、はずしてらっしゃる。　　　　　　<line-break>本当に、忙しい人だ。<line-break>何もかも自分ひとりで背負い込んで…。<line-break>どうやら、ほとんど、<line-break>眠ってらっしゃらないようだ。<end>クリュセイス様は<line-break>お仕事がお忙しくてな。<line-break>今日も飛び回っていらっしゃる。<line-break>そのうち、本当に忙殺されて<line-break>死んでしまうのではないかと<line-break>思うほどだ。<end>クリュセイス様は、激務で心身ともに　　　<line-break>すり減らしておられる。まだ若いのだから、<line-break>恋のひとつでもしていたかろうに。<line-break>我らにつらくあたるときもあるが仕方ない。<line-break>この解放軍を、リベルダム陥落後、<line-break>ひとりで支えてきたようなものだからな。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Khryuseis is away at the moment. She really never stops. She takes everything on herself... It seems she barely sleeps.<end>Khryuseis has been busy with her duties. She's rushing from place to place again today. At this rate, I fear she may literally work herself to death.<end>The relentless work is wearing Khryuseis down, in both body and mind. She's still young; surely she'd rather have time for a little romance. She can be hard on us sometimes, but who can blame her? Since the fall of Liberdam, she's practically held the Liberation Army together single-handedly.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3크류세이스 님이라면 지금 자리를 비우셨네. 정말 바쁜 분이야. 무엇이든 혼자 떠안고 계셔서… 거의 잠도 못 주무시는 모양이더군.<end>크류세이스 님은 일이 무척 바쁘셔서 오늘도 이곳저곳 뛰어다니고 계시네. 그러다 정말 과로로 쓰러져 돌아가시는 건 아닐까 싶을 정도야.<end>크류세이스 님은 격무로 몸과 마음이 모두 닳아 가고 계시네. 아직 젊으니 연애도 하고 싶으실 텐데. 우리에게 모질게 대하실 때도 있지만 어쩔 수 없지. 리벨덤 함락 뒤 이 해방군을 사실상 혼자 떠받쳐 오셨으니까.<end>"

## 1880136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>あなたの存在は解放軍に<line-break>欠くべからざるものです。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, the Liberation Army couldn't do without you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 당신의 존재는 해방군에 없어서는 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 1880137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このままディンガル帝国を　　　　　　<line-break>放っておくわけにはいかない！<line-break>そうでしょう、<value:$28>さん？<end>"
EN: "We can't just stand by and let the Dyneskal Empire carry on like this! You agree, don't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "이대로 딩갈 제국을 내버려 둘 수는 없어요! 그렇죠, <value:$28>님?<end>"

## 1880138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで？<end>"
EN: "Why?<end>"
KO: "왜?<end>"

## 1880139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！<end>"
EN: "That's right!<end>"
KO: "맞아!<end>"

## 1880140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…、なんでって…。<line-break>それは…その…。<line-break>とにかくディンガルは悪者なんですよ！<end>"
EN: "Huh? Why...? Well... um... Anyway, Dyneskal is the villain here!<end>"
KO: "네… 왜냐고요…. 그건… 저…. 어쨌든 딩갈은 나쁜 놈들이에요!<end>"

## 1880141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうです！<line-break>だから、打倒ディンガルのためには<line-break>命も惜しんではいけないんです！<end>"
EN: "Exactly! And if we're to defeat Dyneskal, we mustn't hesitate to lay down our lives!<end>"
KO: "그래요! 그러니까 딘갈을 쓰러뜨리기 위해선 목숨도 아까워해선 안 되는 겁니다!<end>"

## 1880142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2ディンガルの奴らはずる賢い。<line-break>奴らはロセンでは、王政の時より少しだけ<line-break>住民よりの政治をしているんだ。<line-break>だから、ロセンの住民の中には<line-break>むしろディンガルの統治を歓迎<line-break>する動きもある。困ったものだ。<end>リベルダムでのディンガルの統治の<line-break>話を聞いたか？　奴らは、市民非市民の　<line-break>階級制度をなくしてしまったんだ。<line-break>おかげで非市民だった連中は、差別を<line-break>受けなくなったって喜んでいるらしい。<line-break>困ったものだ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2The Dyneskal are a crafty lot. In Rocen, they're governing just a little more in the residents' interests than the monarchy did. So some of the people there are actually starting to welcome Dyneskal rule. That's a problem.<end>Have you heard how the Dyneskal are governing Liberdam? They've abolished the whole citizen and noncitizen class system. Apparently, the former noncitizens are delighted that they're no longer being discriminated against. That's a problem.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2딩갈 놈들은 교활해. 로센에서는 왕정 때보다 아주 조금 주민 편에 선 정치를 하고 있거든. 그래서 로센 주민 중에는 오히려 딩갈의 통치를 반기는 움직임도 있어. 곤란한 일이지.<end>리벨덤에서 딩갈이 어떻게 통치하는지 들었나? 놈들은 시민과 비시민의 계급 제도를 없애 버렸어. 덕분에 비시민이던 자들은 차별받지 않게 됐다며 좋아한다더군. 곤란한 일이지.<end>"

## 1880143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムが陥落し、<line-break>解放軍は財政上の大きな基盤を失った。<line-break>蓄えがなくなるのは時間の問題だ。<line-break>このままでは、ディンガルを倒す前に<line-break>我々が自滅してしまう。<line-break>なんとかしなければ…。<end>"
EN: "With Liberdam fallen, the Liberation Army has lost a major source of financial support. It is only a matter of time before our reserves run dry. At this rate, we will destroy ourselves before we can defeat Dyneskal. We have to do something...<end>"
KO: "리벨덤이 함락되면서 해방군은 재정의 큰 기반을 잃었다. 비축분이 바닥나는 건 시간문제다. 이대로라면 딩갈을 쓰러뜨리기도 전에 우리가 먼저 자멸한다. 어떻게든 해야 해….<end>"

## 1880144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2知っているか？　クリュセイス様は<line-break>解放軍の資金の大部分を、リベルダムや<line-break>ロセンの難民の救済に回すらしい。<line-break>ただでさえ、資金難だというのに<line-break>これでは、いくら資金があっても<line-break>足らないぞ。困ったものだ。<end>クリュセイス様は難民の救済にもっと<line-break>資金を回すように主張しているそうだ。<line-break>以前は非市民と毛嫌いしていたのにな。<line-break>今は一刻も早くディンガルを倒して<line-break>難民をこれ以上増やさないことの方が<line-break>重要なんだが。困ったものだ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Have you heard? Lady Khryuseis apparently intends to spend most of the Liberation Army's funds on relief for the refugees from Liberdam and Rocen. We are already short of money. At this rate, no amount will ever be enough. What a mess.<end>They say Lady Khryuseis is insisting that even more money be devoted to refugee relief. And she used to despise them for being non-citizens. But what matters more right now is defeating Dyneskal as soon as possible, so we don't end up with even more refugees. What a mess.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2알고 있나? 크류세이스 님은 해방군 자금의 대부분을 리벨덤과 로센 난민 구제에 쓸 생각이라더군. 안 그래도 자금난인데 이래서는 돈이 아무리 있어도 부족해. 곤란한 일이지.<end>크류세이스 님은 난민 구제에 더 많은 자금을 쓰자고 주장한다더군. 전에는 비시민이라며 싫어했으면서 말이야. 지금은 하루빨리 딩갈을 쓰러뜨려 난민이 더 늘지 않게 하는 쪽이 중요한데. 곤란한 일이지.<end>"

## 1880145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ軍は町を攻略するときに<line-break>無抵抗の住民には手を出さないように　<line-break>命じているそうです。<line-break>どうやら、皇帝ネメアのお達し<line-break>らしいんですがね。まぁ、<line-break>ありがたいことではあるんですが、　　<end>"
EN: "I hear Karula's Army has orders not to harm civilians who offer no resistance when they take a town. Apparently, the order came from Emperor Nemea himself. Well, I suppose we should be grateful, but...<end>"
KO: "카를라군은 마을을 공략할 때 저항하지 않는 주민에게 손대지 말라고 명령한다더군요. 아무래도 황제 네메아의 명령인 모양입니다. 뭐, 고마운 일이긴 합니다만….<end>"

## 1880146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンやリベルダムの郊外に難民が<line-break>あふれているのは、カルラが住民を　　<line-break>殺さなかったからなんだそうです。<line-break>我々解放軍の立場上、彼らを見捨てて<line-break>おけない。それを見すかしたカルラの<line-break>作戦だと言っている人もいましたよ。<end>"
EN: "They say the outskirts of Rocen and Liberdam are overflowing with refugees because Karula spared the people there. Given our position as the Liberation Army, we cannot simply abandon them. Some say Karula foresaw that and made it part of her strategy.<end>"
KO: "로센과 리벨덤 교외에 난민이 넘치는 건 카를라가 주민들을 죽이지 않았기 때문이라고 합니다. 우리 해방군의 입장상 그들을 내버려 둘 수 없죠. 그걸 간파한 카를라의 작전이라고 말하는 사람도 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1880147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>これからも、わたくしのことを<line-break>支えていてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Please continue to support me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 앞으로도 저를 계속 지탱해 주세요.<end>"

## 1880148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はうかつには動けません。<line-break>解放軍は、壊滅を免れたとはいえ、<line-break>リベルダムで多くの仲間を失いました。<line-break>これまで以上に慎重に時期を選び、<line-break>ディンガルに気取られないように<line-break>動かなくては。<end>"
EN: "We cannot afford to act rashly now. The Liberation Army may have escaped destruction, but we lost many comrades in Liberdam. We must choose our moment more carefully than ever and move without alerting Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "지금은 섣불리 움직일 수 없습니다. 해방군은 괴멸은 피했지만 리벨덤에서 많은 동료를 잃었습니다. 전보다 더 신중하게 때를 고르고, 딘갈에게 들키지 않게 움직여야 합니다.<end>"

## 1880149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec188-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（回収は解放軍に任せよう。）<end>"
EN: "(Best leave the recovery to the Liberation Army.)<end>"
KO: "(회수는 해방군에 맡기자.)<end>"

## 1890001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、久しぶりの新顔だな…。<end>"
EN: "Heh. A new face, after all this time.<end>"
KO: "후후, 오랜만의 신참이군….<end>"

## 1890002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恐れることはない。<line-break>真に汝が恐れるべきは<line-break>他にある。<line-break>むしろ余は汝が力を得る<line-break>助けとなろう。<line-break>…また訪れるがよい。<end>"
EN: "Do not be afraid. What you truly ought to fear lies elsewhere. If anything, I shall help you gain power. Come and visit me again.<end>"
KO: "두려워할 것 없다. 진정 네가 두려워해야 할 것은 따로 있다. 오히려 나는 네가 힘을 얻도록 도와주겠다. …다시 찾아오라.<end>"

## 1890003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。余だ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. It is I.<end>"
KO: "그렇다. 짐이다.<end>"

## 1890004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、さすがにこのおぞましい姿には　<line-break>驚きを隠せぬか…。<line-break>　<line-break>余はシャローム。<line-break>人類の革新を夢見たために<line-break>竜王にうとまれ、この有様だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Even you cannot hide your shock at this hideous form, it seems. I am Shalom. I dreamed of Humanity's Evolution, and for that the Dragon King scorned me and reduced me to this.<end>"
KO: "후후, 역시 이 흉측한 모습엔 놀라움을 감출 수 없나…. 나는 샤롬. 인류의 혁신을 꿈꿨다는 이유로 용왕의 미움을 사 이 꼴이 되었지.<end>"

## 1890005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見知り置くがよい、<line-break><value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Mark me well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "잘 기억해 두어라, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 1890007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また訪れるがよい。<end>"
EN: "You may visit again.<end>"
KO: "다시 찾아오도록 해.<end>"

## 1890008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、無限のソウルの持ち主か…。<line-break>汝は余にとって<line-break>どのような者になるのか…。<line-break>なんにせよ、汝の力…、<line-break>人類の革新のため、<line-break>利用させてもらおう…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. So you are the bearer of the Infinite Soul. I wonder what you will become to me... Whatever the answer, I shall put your power to use for Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "후후, 무한의 소울의 소유자인가…. 그대는 짐에게 어떤 존재가 될 것인가…. 어쨌든 그 힘은 인류 혁신을 위해 이용하겠다….<end>"

## 1890009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類は永き眠りの途上にある。<line-break>魔力を失い、<line-break>腐りゆく肉体に魂をゆだねている。<line-break>だが、ひとたび覚醒すれば、<line-break>強大な魔力と<line-break>不滅の肉体が手に入るのだ。<end>"
EN: "Humanity is in the midst of a long sleep. Its magic lost, it has entrusted its soul to decaying flesh. But once it awakens, it will gain immense magical power and an immortal body.<end>"
KO: "인류는 기나긴 잠에 빠져 있다. 마력을 잃고 썩어 가는 육체에 영혼을 맡긴 채. 하지만 한번 각성하면 강대한 마력과 불멸의 육체를 손에 넣을 수 있다.<end>"

## 1890010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて余は<line-break>それを証明してみせた。<end>"
EN: "I once proved it.<end>"
KO: "과거 짐은 그것을 증명해 보였다.<end>"

## 1890011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…、<line-break>信用できぬようだな。<line-break>仕方あるまい、この姿では。　<line-break>だが、この姿こそ<line-break>覚醒を経験した証とも言えよう。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... You do not believe me. Hardly surprising, given my present form. Yet this very form may itself be called proof that I have undergone the awakening.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 믿기 어려운 모양이군. 이 모습이니 어쩔 수 없지. 하지만 이 모습이야말로 각성을 경험한 증거라 할 수 있다.<end>"

## 1890012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "憎き支配者…竜王。<line-break>奴は、革新を果たした余を恐れた。<line-break>人類の力を恐れたのだ。<line-break>それゆえ、余を<line-break>こそくな術で縛りおった。<end>"
EN: "That accursed ruler... the Dragon King. He feared me once I became the embodiment of Humanity's Evolution. He feared the power of humanity. And so he bound me with his underhanded arts.<end>"
KO: "증오스러운 지배자… 용왕. 놈은 혁신을 이룬 나를 두려워했다. 인류의 힘을 두려워한 것이다. 그래서 나를 비겁한 술법으로 묶어 두었다.<end>"

## 1890013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余の肉体は滅びを知らぬ。<line-break>いつの日か必ず完全なる革新を果たし、<line-break>竜王を封じてくれよう。<end>"
EN: "My body cannot perish. One day, I shall bring Humanity's Evolution to completion and seal away the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "짐의 육체는 멸망을 모른다. 언젠가 반드시 완전한 혁신을 이루어 용왕을 봉인해 주겠다.<end>"

## 1890014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このザマからは想像もつかぬだろうが、<line-break>かつて余は国王として<line-break>君臨したこともあった。<end>"
EN: "You would never guess it from the sorry state I am in now, but I once reigned as a king.<end>"
KO: "지금 이 꼴로는 상상도 못 하겠지만 한때 짐은 국왕으로 군림한 적도 있었다.<end>"

## 1890015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１２００年も前になる。<line-break>この世では小国同士の小競り合いが<line-break>繰り返されていた。<line-break>当時、すでに魔法は失われていたゆえ<line-break>戦はすべて肉弾戦であった。<end>"
EN: "That was 1,200 years ago. Small states were forever skirmishing with one another. Magic had already vanished from the world, so every battle was fought hand-to-hand.<end>"
KO: "1200년도 더 전의 일이다. 이 세상에서는 소국들 사이의 분쟁이 계속되고 있었다. 당시 이미 마법은 사라져 있었기에 전쟁은 모두 육탄전이었다.<end>"

## 1890016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ神聖王国アルレシアのみが<line-break>神より魔法を授かっていたのだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>アルレシアは魔法を武器に<line-break>小国を平定し、戦乱を終結させた。<end>"
EN: "Only the Holy Kingdom of Alresia had been granted magic by God. With magic as its weapon, Alresia subdued the small states and brought the strife to an end.<end>"
KO: "오직 신성왕국 알레시아만이 신에게서 마법을 받았다. 알레시아는 마법을 무기로 소국들을 평정하고 전란을 끝냈다.<end>"

## 1890017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このときアルレシアを統治していたのが<line-break>７人の魔道士、アルレシア七王。<line-break>余もそのひとりであった。<end>"
EN: "Alresia was ruled in those days by seven sorcerers known as the Seven Kings of Alresia. I was one of them.<end>"
KO: "당시 알레시아를 통치하던 이들이 일곱 명의 마도사, 알레시아 칠왕이었다. 짐도 그중 하나였지.<end>"

## 1890018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、大国の存在を嫌い、<line-break>再び世を乱そうとした者がある。<line-break>…竜王だ。<line-break>竜王は、七王のうちのふたり、<line-break>…余とディーヴァをそそのかし、<line-break>アルレシアに対抗させた。<end>"
EN: "But there was one who resented the existence of a great power and sought to plunge the world into turmoil once more: the Dragon King. He incited two of the Seven Kings, Diva and me, to oppose Alresia.<end>"
KO: "하지만 대국의 존재를 싫어해 다시 세상을 어지럽히려 한 자가 있었다. …용왕이다. 용왕은 일곱 왕 중 둘, …짐과 디바를 부추겨 알레시아에 맞서게 했다.<end>"

## 1890019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々が築いた魔道王国ラドラスと<line-break>アルレシアの間で<line-break>再び戦が始まったのだ。<line-break>すべては奴の目論見どおり…。<line-break>神をかたり、世をかき乱す<line-break>あの者こそ人類の敵なのだ。<end>"
EN: "War began anew between Alresia and the Magic Kingdom Ladras, which we had founded. Everything unfolded exactly as he intended. The one who poses as God and sows chaos throughout the world is humanity's enemy.<end>"
KO: "우리가 세운 마도왕국 라드라스와 알레시아 사이에서 다시 전쟁이 시작됐다. 모든 것은 놈의 계획대로…. 신을 사칭해 세상을 어지럽히는 그자야말로 인류의 적이다.<end>"

## 1890020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまで、<line-break>可能性を秘めた者の多くが<line-break>竜王の手で葬られてきた。<end>"
EN: "Many who possessed such potential have already been slain at the Dragon King's hands.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 가능성을 품은 자들 중 많은 이가 용왕의 손에 묻혀 왔다.<end>"

## 1890021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>汝も注意することだ。<line-break>　<line-break>目立った真似をすると<line-break>竜王に消される…。<end>"
EN: "Take heed as well, <value:$28>. Draw too much attention, and the Dragon King will have you erased.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그대도 조심하라. 눈에 띄는 짓을 하면 용왕에게 지워질 것이다….<end>"

## 1890022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類が覚醒を果たすまで、<line-break>竜王に屈する日々が続くのだ。<end>"
EN: "Until humanity achieves its awakening, it will remain in submission to the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "인류가 각성할 때까지 용왕에게 굴복하는 나날이 계속될 것이다.<end>"

## 1890023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ、<line-break>世界を見て、いかに感じた？　<line-break>　<line-break>世界は悲しみに満ちている…。<line-break>もう少し力があればと思うことが<line-break>多くはないか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you have seen the world. What did you make of it? It is filled with sorrow. Have you not often wished you had just a little more power?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여, 세계를 보고 무엇을 느꼈나? 세계는 슬픔으로 가득하다…. 조금만 더 힘이 있었다면 하고 생각한 적이 많지 않나?<end>"

## 1890024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新たな力を得たくはないか？<end>"
EN: "Would you not like to gain a new power?<end>"
KO: "새로운 힘을 얻고 싶지 않나?<end>"

## 1890025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そう警戒するな。<line-break>余は、汝に期待している。　　　<line-break>悪いようにはせんよ。<line-break>この塔の最上階。<line-break>その奧には、マノンの腕輪という<line-break>強大な力を秘めた魔道器がある。<end>"
EN: "Heh. There is no need to be so wary. I have high hopes for you, and I mean you no harm. Deep within the highest floor of this tower lies a magical artifact of immense power known as Manon's Bracelet.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇게 경계하지 마라. 짐은 그대에게 기대하고 있다. 나쁘게 하진 않겠다. 이 탑의 최상층, 그 안쪽에는 마논의 팔찌라는 강대한 힘을 품은 마도기가 있다.<end>"

## 1890026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マノンは、かつて<line-break>この塔にいた女魔道士だ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>そのマノンの力を封じた腕輪に<line-break>打ち勝つことができれば、<line-break>汝は新たな力を得よう。<end>"
EN: "Manon was a female mage who once lived in this tower. If you can overcome the bracelet containing Manon's sealed power, you shall gain new power.<end>"
KO: "마논은 예전에 이 탑에 있던 여마도사다. 그 마논의 힘을 봉인한 팔찌를 이겨낼 수 있다면 그대는 새로운 힘을 얻을 것이다.<end>"

## 1890027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はまだ、マノンの腕輪は<line-break>深い眠りについているようだが、<line-break>私が起こしておこう。<line-break>訪れてみるがいい。<end>"
EN: "For now, Manon's Bracelet still seems to be deep asleep, but I shall awaken it. Go and pay it a visit.<end>"
KO: "지금은 아직 마논의 팔찌가 깊이 잠들어 있는 것 같지만, 내가 깨워 두지. 한번 찾아가 보도록 해.<end>"

## 1890028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。<line-break>最も深く暗き魔法を欲しくはないか？<line-break>それは、新月の塔で手に入ろう。<line-break>天に近き場所に封印された<line-break>禁断の闇の魔法デモリッシュ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Do you not desire the deepest, darkest magic? You will find it in New Moon Tower: Demolish, the forbidden dark magic sealed away close to the heavens.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여. 가장 깊고 어두운 마법을 원하지 않느냐? 그것은 신월의 탑에서 얻을 수 있다. 하늘과 가까운 곳에 봉인된 금단의 어둠 마법 데몰리시.<end>"

## 1890029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネガヴァニティアのソウルを<line-break>宿す者が訪れれば、<line-break>守護者が迎え撃つという。<line-break>守護者を倒し、祭壇にたどり着ければ、<line-break>汝は禁忌の力を手にしよう。<line-break>フフ、挑戦してみるがよいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The Guardian is said to confront anyone who comes bearing the Soul of Negavanitia. Defeat the Guardian and reach the altar, and forbidden power shall be yours. Heh heh. Go on, then. See if you can.<end>"
KO: "네가바니티아의 소울을 품은 자가 찾아오면 수호자가 맞서 싸운다고 한다. 수호자를 쓰러뜨리고 제단에 도달하면 그대는 금기의 힘을 얻을 것이다. 후후, 도전해 보아라.<end>"

## 1890030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王が動いたようだな。<line-break>ラドラスの浮上は、<line-break>さすがに捨て置けなかったようだ…。<line-break>ラドラスの力は<line-break>世界の均衡を容易に崩すからな。<end>"
EN: "It seems the Dragon King has made a move. Apparently, Ladras's rise was too much to ignore... Ladras's power can easily upset the Balance of the World.<end>"
KO: "용왕이 움직인 모양이군. 라드라스의 부상은 역시 그냥 둘 수 없었던 거겠지…. 라드라스의 힘은 세계의 균형을 쉽게 무너뜨리니까.<end>"

## 1890031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王とはそういう存在なのだ。<line-break>神の代行者として、<line-break>世界を存続させ続けるためにのみ動く。<line-break>疑うならば、この大陸にいる<line-break>すべての竜を殺して見よ。<line-break>そして、竜王の島に向かえ。<end>"
EN: "That is the nature of the Dragon King. As the divine agent, the Dragon King acts for one purpose alone: to keep the world in existence. If you doubt it, try killing every dragon on this continent. Then make your way to Dragon King's Island.<end>"
KO: "용왕이란 그런 존재다. 신의 대행자로서 세계를 계속 존속시키기 위해서만 움직이지. 의심스럽다면 이 대륙의 모든 용을 죽여 보아라. 그리고 용왕의 섬으로 향해라.<end>"

## 1890032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王はお前を見逃さんだろうよ。<line-break>すべての竜を殺したとなれば、<line-break>間違いなく世界を脅かす力の持ち主だ。<line-break>必ず始末しようとする。<line-break>危険すぎるからな。フフフ。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King would never let you go. If you had killed every dragon, there would be no question that you possessed the power to threaten the world. The Dragon King would be sure to try to dispose of you. You would be too dangerous. Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "용왕은 너를 놓아주지 않을 거다. 모든 용을 죽였다면 틀림없이 세계를 위협할 힘의 소유자다. 반드시 처리하려 하겠지. 너무 위험하니까. 후후후.<end>"

## 1890033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、いらっしゃい。<line-break>待っていたよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah, welcome. I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 기다리고 있었어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは。そう邪険にしないでよ。<line-break>ネメアを助けに来ただけなんだから。<line-break><value:$28>だって、そうでしょ？<line-break>でも、もういいや。<line-break>ネメア、いらなくなっちゃった。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha. Don't be so cold. I only came to save Nemea. That's why you're here too, isn't it, <value:$28>? But never mind. I don't need Nemea anymore.<end>"
KO: "아하하. 그렇게 매몰차게 굴지 마. 네메아를 구하러 왔을 뿐이니까. <value:$28>도 그렇지? 하지만 이제 됐어. 네메아, 필요 없어졌거든.<end>"

## 1890036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>君がいるからね。<end>"
EN: "Because I have you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 네가 있으니까.<end>"

## 1890038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、儀式を始めようか。<end>"
EN: "Now, shall we begin the ritual?<end>"
KO: "자, 의식을 시작할까.<end>"

## 1890039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグはネメアに降臨する。<line-break>僕もそう思っていたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>でもね、ウルグは<line-break>いつだって捜してるんだ。<line-break>もっとも適した入れ物をね。<end>"
EN: "Ulugh would descend into Nemea. That's what I thought too. But you see, Ulugh is always searching for the most suitable vessel.<end>"
KO: "울그는 네메아에게 강림한다. 나도 그렇게 생각했어. 하지만 울그는 언제나 찾고 있어. 가장 알맞은 그릇을.<end>"

## 1890040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>君は強くなりすぎたんだ。<line-break>ウルグはネメアより君がいいってさ。<line-break>君に降臨した方が<line-break>より強大な力を出せるんだよ。<line-break>破壊神は君の肉体をお望みだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you've become too strong. Ulugh wants you instead of Nemea. Descending into you will bring out far greater power. The God of Destruction desires your body.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 넌 너무 강해졌어. 울그는 네메아보다 네가 좋대. 네게 강림하는 편이 더 강대한 힘을 낼 수 있다는 거지. 파괴신은 네 육체를 원하셔.<end>"
