JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2な、なんですって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3なんじゃと！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4なんかすごいことになってきたわ。<line-break>…ばっくれた方がいいかも。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5な、なんだって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6そ、そんな…。<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>7なんですって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>9破壊神に見そめられるなんて<line-break><value:$28>もやるじゃん。<line-break>…でも、黙ってはいられないね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12なんだって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13なんだと！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10ウルグが姉ちゃんに？<line-break>まじかよ！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14なんじゃと！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15な、なんだって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、な、な、なんですってー！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19えぇ！？　そ、そんなぁ…。<line-break>やだよぉ、<value:$28>！<line-break>破壊神なんかになっちゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20何！<line-break>ウルグが<line-break><value:$28>にだと…！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>21なんてことだ！<line-break><value:$28>！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23ウルグだと！？<line-break>なんということだ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>27なんだって！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんじゃと！<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>24そ、そんな…。<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>様に！？<if><value:$01><equal>25ウルグが…？！<line-break>そんな…<end><value:$28>が、破壊神に！？<line-break>す、すごい詩ができそうだな…。<line-break>って、そんな場合じゃないよ。 <end>"

## 1890042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　なんだ…？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What's happening...?<end>"
KO: "어라? 뭐지…?<end>"

## 1890043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器に異物が混入…だと？<line-break>意志あるもの…、ソウル…？<end>"
EN: "A foreign presence within the divine artifact...? Something with a will of its own... a Soul...?<end>"
KO: "신기에 이물질이 섞였다고…? 의지를 지닌 것… 소울…?<end>"

## 1890044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして、シャリ君。<line-break>私はオルファウスといいます。<line-break>よろしくお見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you, Shalli. My name is Orphaus. Do remember me.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다, 샤리 군. 저는 오르파우스라고 합니다. 잘 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 1890045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、こうなることを見越し、<line-break>魂吸いの指輪にわざとソウルを<line-break>吸わせて潜んでいたのか…。<line-break>破壊神の復活を<line-break>直前でくい止めるために…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You foresaw this, had the Soul-Draining Ring absorb your Soul, and hid inside it deliberately... all to stop the God of Destruction's resurrection at the final moment...<end>"
KO: "큭, 이렇게 될 걸 내다보고 영혼흡수의 반지에 일부러 소울을 빨아들이게 한 뒤 숨어 있었나…. 파괴신의 부활을 직전에 막기 위해….<end>"

## 1890046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とっさの思いつきだったんですけど、<line-break>ネメアはできのいい子でしてね。<line-break>無言で理解してくれましたよ。<line-break>ウルグの復活は、失敗ですね。<end>"
EN: "It was only a spur-of-the-moment idea, but Nemea is a clever boy. He understood without a word. Ulugh's resurrection has failed.<end>"
KO: "순간 떠오른 생각이었지만 네메아는 영리한 아이여서 말없이 이해해 줬습니다. 울그의 부활은 실패네요.<end>"

## 1890047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもないさ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ウルグの力の一部は、<line-break>すでに<value:$28>の精神に<line-break>入り込んでるんだよ？<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't say that. Some of Ulugh's power has already seeped into <value:$28>'s mind, you know.<end>"
KO: "꼭 그렇지도 않아. 울그의 힘 일부는 이미 <value:$28>의 정신 속에 들어가 있거든?<end>"

## 1890048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうするつもり？<end>"
EN: "What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할 생각이야?<end>"

## 1890049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>を<line-break>甘く見ないことですね。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>闇の神器の力さえ取り除けば、<line-break><value:$28>は<line-break>破壊神にも負けはしないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "You underestimate <value:$28>. Once the power of the dark divine artifacts is removed, even the God of Destruction will not defeat <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>을 얕보지 마세요. 어둠의 신기의 힘만 제거하면 <value:$28>은 파괴신에게도 지지 않을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1890050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは、おもしろいね。<line-break>確かにそうかもしれないよ。<line-break>ま、今回は一本とられたってとこかな。<line-break>でもね、これが切り札じゃないんだよ。<line-break>フフフ、ここから戻れたら、<line-break>エンシャントにおいでよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha, how interesting. You may be right. I suppose you've bested me this time. But this wasn't my trump card. Heh heh... If you make it back from here, come to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 재미있네. 확실히 그럴지도 몰라. 뭐, 이번엔 한 방 먹었다고 해 두지. 하지만 이게 비장의 수는 아니야. 후후후, 여기서 돌아올 수 있으면 엔샨트로 와.<end>"

## 1890051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってるよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll be waiting, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있을게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　ワレを…うけいれよ…。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　お前の…ニクタイには…、<line-break>　　　　邪神の魂こそ…、ふさわしい…。<end>"
EN: "Accept me... The soul of an evil god... is the one most fitting for your flesh...<end>"
KO: "나를… 받아들여라…. 네… 육체에는… 사신의 영혼이야말로… 어울린다….<end>"

## 1890053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　相容れぬの…ならば…、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　ヨワき存在が…、<line-break>　　　　　　消え去る…、のみ…。<end>"
EN: "If we cannot coexist... then the weak being... will simply vanish...<end>"
KO: "서로 양립할 수… 없다면…. 약한 존재가… 사라질… 뿐….<end>"

## 1890054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よもや人の子に…、屈するとは…。　　　<line-break>　　　　ワが魂の一部…、ムネンだが、<line-break>　　　　　　汝のモノとなろう…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　フフ…、これも…よかろう…。<line-break>　　　　ワレのかけらが…残るのだ…。<line-break>　　　　わざわいの種は…、まかれた…。<end>"
EN: "To think I would yield... to a child of man... Though it galls me, a part of my soul... shall become yours... Heh... so be it... A fragment of me... will remain... The seed of calamity... has been sown...<end>"
KO: "설마 인간의 아이에게… 굴복할 줄이야…. 내 혼의 일부… 원통하지만 그대의 것이 되리라…. 후후… 이것도 좋겠지…. 나의 조각이… 남는 것이다…. 재앙의 씨앗은… 뿌려졌다….<end>"

## 1890055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よもや人の子に…、屈するとは…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　だが…、いずれ…、<line-break>　　　　　滅びの時は…訪れよう…。<line-break>　　　ワレが…、テを…下すまでも…なく。<line-break>　　　　だが…、ネガわくば…いつか…、<line-break>　　　　我がテにて…汝らに…、滅びヲ！<end>"
EN: "To think I would yield... to a child of man... But one day... the hour of destruction will come... without my lifting a hand... And yet... I pray that someday... I may bring ruin upon you all by my own hand!<end>"
KO: "설마 인간의 아이에게… 굴복하다니…. 하지만… 언젠가… 멸망의 때는 찾아오리라…. 내가… 손을… 쓰지 않더라도…. 하지만… 바라건대… 언젠가… 내 손으로… 너희에게… 멸망을!<end>"

Hurry back...<end><if><value:$01><equal>26I hope everything is all right...<end><if><value:$01><equal>24<value:$28>... Please, be safe...<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Please... Hurry back...<end>Come back, <value:$28>. Then let me write a poem about <value:$28>, who defeated Ulugh...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>…. 부탁이야, 무사해 줘!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이봐, 정말 괜찮은 거겠지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4괜찮을까? 전혀 움직이지 않는데요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>, 괜찮은 거야? 이대로인 건 아니겠지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>…. 괜찮지? 반드시 돌아올 거지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7<value:$28>…. 무사한 거겠지…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>9안 돌아오네. 정말 괜찮을까, <value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>12이겨라, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>…. 괜찮은 건가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 혹시…. 아니, 설마. 아니, 그래도. 아아! 빨리 돌아와!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14으음. 돌아오질 않는군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15괜찮지, <value:$28>…. 너라면 분명….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17언제까지 뻗어 있을 거야. 빨리 이런 곳에서 나가고 싶은데. 정말! 왜 안 돌아오는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>…. 부탁이야, 돌아와아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…승부가 난 모양이군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>21설마 이대로인 건 아니겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23<value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>27<value:$28>…. 빨리 돌아와…<end><if><value:$01><equal>26괜찮을까….<end><if><value:$01><equal>24<value:$28> 님… 부디 무사하시길….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25…부탁이야, 빨리 돌아와….<end>돌아와 줘, <value:$28>. 그리고 나에게 울그를 이긴 <value:$28>의 시를 만들게 해 줘….<end>"

## 1890058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくがんばりましたね。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に<line-break>降臨するとは計算外でしたが、<line-break>復活は阻止されました。<end>"
EN: "You did very well. I had not accounted for Ulugh manifesting within <value:$28>, but the resurrection was prevented.<end>"
KO: "정말 수고하셨습니다. 울그가 <value:$28>에게 강림한 것은 계산 밖이었지만 부활은 저지되었습니다.<end>"

## 1890059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグさえ退けてしまえば、<line-break>ネメアは自力で<line-break>戻ってこられるはずです。<line-break>我々はいったん戻って、<line-break>備えなければなりません。<line-break>最後の戦いにね。<end>"
EN: "Once Ulugh has been driven out, Nemea should be able to return on his own. For now, we must go back and prepare for the final battle.<end>"
KO: "울그만 물리치면 네메아는 자기 힘으로 돌아올 수 있을 겁니다. 우리는 일단 돌아가 준비해야 합니다. 마지막 싸움을 위해서요.<end>"

## 1890060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、猫屋敷に戻りますよ。<end>"
EN: "Come. We're returning to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "자, 고양이 저택으로 돌아가죠.<end>"

## 1890061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、兄さんはまだ…。<end>"
EN: "Then my brother is still...<end>"
KO: "그럼 형은 아직….<end>"

## 1890062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "安心なさい。<line-break>あの子は必ず帰ってきます。<line-break>私は、あの子を信じています。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. Your brother will come back. I have faith in him.<end>"
KO: "안심하세요. 그 아이는 반드시 돌아옵니다. 저는 그 아이를 믿고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1890063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん…。<end>"
EN: "Brother...<end>"
KO: "형….<end>"

## 1890064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかったわ。<line-break>私も、兄さんを信じて、<line-break>待っているわ。<line-break>それが、私の戦いね。<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll put my faith in my brother too, and wait for him. That's my battle.<end>"
KO: "…알겠어. 나도 오빠를 믿고 기다릴게. 그게 내 싸움이야.<end>"

## 1890065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなりましたね、あの子も。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia has grown stronger too.<end>"
KO: "…그 아이도 강해졌군요.<end>"

## 1890066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、私たちは、私たちの戦いを<line-break>続けねばなりません。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、あなたにも、<line-break>真の敵が見えてきたはずです。<end>"
EN: "Now, we must continue our own battle. <value:$28>, by now you too must have seen who the true enemy is.<end>"
KO: "자, 우리는 우리의 싸움을 계속해야 합니다. <value:$28>, 당신도 진정한 적이 보이기 시작했을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1890067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグの復活は阻止したものの、<line-break>戦いが終わったわけではないようです。<line-break>　<line-break>どうやら決戦の場は<line-break>エンシャントのようです。<line-break>心して向かってください。<end>"
EN: "Though we prevented Ulugh's revival, it seems the battle is not yet over. It appears the decisive battle will be at Ancient. Prepare yourself before you go.<end>"
KO: "울그의 부활은 막았지만 싸움이 끝난 것은 아닌 듯합니다. 아무래도 결전의 장소는 엔샨트인 듯합니다. 각오하고 가십시오.<end>"

## 1890068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が起こるかわかりませんが、<line-break>強きソウルと共にあれば<line-break>必ず道は開けますからね。<line-break>あなたには、<line-break>無限の可能性があるんですから。<end>"
EN: "We cannot know what will happen, but with a powerful Soul beside you, a path will surely open. Your possibilities are limitless.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이 일어날지는 모르지만 강한 소울과 함께라면 반드시 길이 열릴 겁니다. 당신에겐 무한한 가능성이 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1890069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと。<line-break>私もお手伝いしますよ。<line-break>必要なら言ってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, one more thing. I can help as well. Just ask if you need me.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고요. 저도 도와드릴게요. 필요하면 말씀해 주세요.<end>"

## 1890070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、いらっしゃい。<line-break>待っていたよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah, welcome. I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 기다리고 있었어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2また、あなたなの！<line-break>シャリ！！<line-break>ネメア様に何を！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3また、お主か！<line-break>シャリ！！<line-break>ネメア様に何をしおった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4また、シャリが何かしたみたい。<line-break>ネメア様の様子が変！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5てめえ、ネメアに何しやがった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6ネメアに何をしたの！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>7貴様！　ネメア様に何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>9ちょっと、あんた。<line-break>ネメア様に何してくれたわけ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>12ロストールだけじゃなく、　<line-break>こんなところでも<line-break>悪さしてやがったか。<line-break>ネメアにいったい何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13貴様！<line-break>ネメアに何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10ネメアの様子がおかしいぞ。<line-break>てめぇ、何しやがった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14お主！<line-break>ネメアに何をしおった！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアさんの様子が変だよ！<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17また、やっかいごとなのぉー！<line-break>ちょっと、シャリ！<line-break>今度はネメアに何したのよー！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ネメアさんの様子が変だよぉ！<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20貴様…、<line-break>ネメアに何をした？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>21貴様…！<line-break>ネメアに何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23貴様…！<line-break>ネメア様に何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27様子がおかしい…<line-break>シャリ！何をした！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26ネメア殿に何をしたんじゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24ネメア様に何をしたのですか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>25様子がおかしい…<line-break>あなた、何をしたの？！<end><value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアの様子が変だよ？<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。 <end>"

What the hell did you do to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6What did you do to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7You! What have you done to Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hey, you. What have you done to Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12So you weren't content to cause trouble in Rostorl―you've been at it here too. What the hell did you do to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You! What have you done to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10There's something wrong with Nemea. What the hell did you do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14You! What have you done to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, there's something wrong with Nemea! Shalli did something to him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, not more trouble! Shalli! What did you do to Nemea this time?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19There's something wrong with Nemea! Shalli did something to him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20You... What did you do to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21You...! What have you done to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23You...! What have you done to Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Something's wrong... Shalli! What did you do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What have you done to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24What have you done to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Something's wrong... What did you do?!<end><value:$28>, something's wrong with Nemea. Shalli did something to him.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2또 너야! 샤리!! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3또 네놈이냐! 샤리!! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했느냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4또 샤리가 뭔가 한 모양이야. 네메아 님 상태가 이상해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5네놈, 네메아한테 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7네놈! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9잠깐, 너. 네메아 님한테 무슨 짓을 한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12로스톨뿐 아니라 이런 데서도 못된 짓을 했냐. 네메아한테 대체 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13네놈! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10네메아 상태가 이상해. 네놈, 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, 네메아 씨 상태가 이상해! 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17또 귀찮은 일이야아! 잠깐 샤리! 이번엔 네메아한테 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19네메아 씨 상태가 이상해애! 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20네놈… 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21네놈…! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23네놈…! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27상태가 이상해… 샤리! 무슨 짓을 한 거냐!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26네메아 공에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 겁니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25상태가 이상해… 당신, 무슨 짓을 한 거야?!<end><value:$28>, 네메아 상태가 이상해? 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end>"

## 1890072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誤解しないでほしいな。<line-break>僕は、ネメアを救ってあげたんだ。<line-break>次元のはざまからね。<end>"
EN: "Don't misunderstand. I saved Nemea―from the Interdimensional Rift.<end>"
KO: "오해하지 않았으면 해. 나는 네메아를 구해 준 거야. 차원의 틈새에서 말이지.<end>"

## 1890073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく<value:$28>と僕で<line-break>闇の神器を１２個集めたのに、<line-break>あんなことで死なれちゃ困るからね。<line-break>フフフ。さあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>残りの神器を僕にくれないかな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "After <value:$28> and I went to all that trouble collecting twelve Artifacts of Darkness, I couldn't very well let Nemea die like that. Heh heh. Now then, <value:$28>. Would you give me the remaining artifacts?<end>"
KO: "모처럼 <value:$28>와 내가 어둠의 신기를 12개나 모았는데 그런 일로 죽으면 곤란하거든. 후후후. 자, <value:$28>. 남은 신기를 나한테 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1890074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　なんだ…？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What's happening...?<end>"
KO: "어라? 뭐지…?<end>"

## 1890075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器に異物が混入…だと？<line-break>意志あるもの…、ソウル…？<end>"
EN: "A foreign presence within the divine artifact...? Something with a will of its own... a Soul...?<end>"
KO: "신기에 이물질이 섞였다고…? 의지를 지닌 것… 소울…?<end>"

## 1890076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして、シャリ君。<line-break>私はオルファウスといいます。<line-break>よろしくお見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you, Shalli. My name is Orphaus. Do remember me.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다, 샤리 군. 저는 오르파우스라고 합니다. 잘 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 1890077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、こうなることを見越し、<line-break>魂吸いの指輪にわざとソウルを<line-break>吸わせて潜んでいたのか…。<line-break>破壊神の復活を<line-break>直前でくい止めるために…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You foresaw this, had the Soul-Draining Ring absorb your Soul, and hid inside it deliberately... all to stop the God of Destruction's resurrection at the final moment...<end>"
KO: "큭, 이렇게 될 걸 내다보고 영혼흡수의 반지에 일부러 소울을 빨아들이게 한 뒤 숨어 있었나…. 파괴신의 부활을 직전에 막기 위해….<end>"

## 1890078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とっさの思いつきだったんですけど、<line-break>ネメアはできのいい子でしてね。<line-break>無言で理解してくれましたよ。<line-break>ウルグの復活は、失敗ですね。<end>"
EN: "It was only a spur-of-the-moment idea, but Nemea is a clever boy. He understood without a word. Ulugh's resurrection has failed.<end>"
KO: "순간 떠오른 생각이었지만 네메아는 영리한 아이여서 말없이 이해해 줬습니다. 울그의 부활은 실패네요.<end>"

## 1890079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもないさ。<line-break>ウルグの力の一部は、<line-break>すでにネメアの精神に入り込んでる。<line-break>どうするつもり？<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't say that. Some of Ulugh's power has already seeped into Nemea's mind. What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "그렇지만도 않아. 울그의 힘 일부는 이미 네메아의 정신에 들어가 있어. 어떻게 할 생각이지?<end>"

## 1890080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決まっています。<line-break>ネメアの精神世界に入り込んで<line-break>追い払います。<end>"
EN: "The answer is obvious. The plan is to enter Nemea's mind and drive Ulugh out.<end>"
KO: "정해져 있습니다. 네메아의 정신세계에 들어가 쫓아내겠습니다.<end>"

## 1890081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは、おもしろいね。<line-break>さすがに賢者様はよく考えてるや。<line-break>ま、今回は一本とられたってとこかな。<line-break>でもね、これが切り札じゃないんだよ。<line-break>フフフ、ここから戻れたら、<line-break>エンシャントにおいでよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha, how interesting. The great sage has certainly thought this through. I suppose you've bested me this time. But this wasn't my trump card. Heh heh... If you make it back from here, come to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 재미있네. 역시 현자님은 잘 생각해. 뭐, 이번엔 한 방 먹었다고 해 두지. 하지만 이게 비장의 수는 아니야. 후후, 여기서 돌아갈 수 있으면 엔샨트로 와.<end>"

## 1890082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってるよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll be waiting, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있을게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ぐずぐずしていられません。<line-break>今から、ネメアの精神世界に行きます。<line-break>　<line-break>おそらく、破壊神に半ば乗っ取られた<line-break>ネメアと戦うことになるでしょう。<line-break>心してください。<end>"
EN: "There is no time to waste. It is time to enter Nemea's mind. Nemea will most likely be half possessed by the God of Destruction and will have to be fought. Be prepared.<end>"
KO: "자, 꾸물거릴 수 없습니다. 지금부터 네메아의 정신세계로 갑니다. 아마 파괴신에게 반쯤 잠식된 네메아와 싸우게 될 겁니다. 각오하세요.<end>"

## 1890084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行きますよ。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 갑니다.<end>"

## 1890085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　ダレ…だ…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　ワレの…復活を…、<line-break>　　　　　　邪魔する…モノは…。<end>"
EN: "Who... is it... obstructing my resurrection...?<end>"
KO: "누구…냐…. 나의… 부활을… 방해하는… 자는….<end>"

## 1890086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、かなり不安定な状態ですね。<line-break>これならば、ネメア本来の力も<line-break>ウルグとしての力も出せないはずです。<line-break>勝てるはずです。<line-break>今ならまだ間に合います。<end>"
EN: "As I suspected, his condition is highly unstable. He should be unable to wield either Nemea's own power or Ulugh's. We can defeat him. There is still time.<end>"
KO: "역시 상당히 불안정한 상태군요. 이렇다면 네메아 본래의 힘도 울그의 힘도 낼 수 없을 겁니다. 이길 수 있어요. 지금이라면 아직 늦지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 1890087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がネメアの精神を守っておきます。<line-break>本気で戦って打ち負かしてください。<line-break>でないと、みんな死にますよ！<end>"
EN: "I will protect Nemea's mind. Fight him with all your strength and defeat him. Otherwise, everyone will die!<end>"
KO: "제가 네메아의 정신을 지키겠습니다. 전력을 다해 싸워 쓰러뜨려 주세요. 그러지 않으면 모두 죽습니다!<end>"

## 1890088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　シすべき…モノたち…か…。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　キエうせるが…いい…。<line-break>　　　　オロかなる存在に…、滅びを！<end>"
EN: "So... you are the ones who must die... Begone... Doom upon you, foolish creatures!<end>"
KO: "죽어야 할… 자들인가…. 사라져라…. 어리석은 존재들에게… 멸망을!<end>"

## 1890089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　いずれ…滅びの時は…訪れよう…。　　　<line-break>　　　ワレが…、テを…下すまでも…なく。<line-break>　　　　だが…、ネガわくば…いつか…、<line-break>　　　　我がテにて…汝らに…、滅びヲ！<end>"
EN: "One day... the hour of destruction will come... without my lifting a hand... And yet... I pray that someday... I may bring ruin upon you all by my own hand!<end>"
KO: "언젠가… 멸망의 때는… 찾아올 것이다…. 내가… 손을… 쓰지 않아도…. 하지만… 바라건대… 언젠가… 내 손으로… 너희에게… 멸망을!<end>"

## 1890091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうでしょうね？<line-break>あとはネメア次第です。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>この子の精神が<line-break>ウルグに破壊されていなければ、<line-break>戻ってくるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Who can say? The rest is up to Nemea. If Ulugh hasn't destroyed his mind, he'll return.<end>"
KO: "글쎄요? 이제 네메아에게 달렸습니다. 이 아이의 정신이 울그에게 파괴되지 않았다면 돌아오겠지요.<end>"

## 1890092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともかく、<line-break>今は猫屋敷に戻りましょう。<end>"
EN: "In any case, let us return to Cat Manor for now.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 지금은 고양이 저택으로 돌아가죠.<end>"

## 1890093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ありがとう。<line-break>なんてお礼を言ったらいいのか…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I don't know how to thank you...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워. 뭐라고 감사해야 할지….<end>"

## 1890094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "安心するのは、まだ早いですよ。<line-break>ネメアはまだ目覚めていませんから。<line-break>　<line-break>この子が破壊神の精神に<line-break>負けていたら、このままですから。<end>"
EN: "It is still too soon to relax. Nemea has not awakened yet. If he has succumbed to the spirit of the God of Destruction, he will remain as he is.<end>"
KO: "안심하긴 아직 이릅니다. 네메아는 아직 깨어나지 않았으니까요. 이 아이가 파괴신의 정신에 졌다면 이대로입니다.<end>"

## 1890095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな…！<line-break>私に…、私にできることは？<end>"
EN: "Th-that can't be...! Is there... is there anything I can do?<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가…! 내가… 내가 할 수 있는 일은?<end>"

## 1890096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはネメアの戦いです。<line-break>手出しはできません。<end>"
EN: "This is Nemea's battle. We cannot intervene.<end>"
KO: "이건 네메아의 싸움입니다. 손댈 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1890097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん…。<end>"
EN: "Brother...<end>"
KO: "형….<end>"

## 1890098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかったわ。<line-break>私は、兄さんを信じて<line-break>待っているわ。<line-break>それが、私の戦いね。<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll put my faith in my brother and wait for him. That's my battle.<end>"
KO: "…알겠어. 난 오빠를 믿고 기다릴게. 그게 내 싸움이야.<end>"

## 1890099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなりましたね、あの子も。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia has grown stronger too.<end>"
KO: "…그 아이도 강해졌군요.<end>"

## 1890100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、私たちは、私たちの戦いを<line-break>続けねばなりません。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、あなたにも、<line-break>真の敵が見えてきたはずです。<end>"
EN: "Now, we must continue our own battle. <value:$28>, by now you too must have seen who the true enemy is.<end>"
KO: "자, 우리는 우리의 싸움을 계속해야 합니다. <value:$28>, 당신도 진정한 적이 보이기 시작했을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1890101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグの復活は阻止したものの、<line-break>戦いは終わったわけではないようです。<line-break>　<line-break>どうやら決戦の場は<line-break>エンシャントのようです。<line-break>心して向かってください。<end>"
EN: "Though we prevented Ulugh's revival, it seems the battle is not yet over. It appears the decisive battle will be at Ancient. Prepare yourself before you go.<end>"
KO: "울그의 부활은 막았지만 싸움이 끝난 것은 아닌 듯합니다. 아무래도 결전의 장소는 엔샨트인 듯합니다. 각오하고 가십시오.<end>"

## 1890102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が起こるかわかりませんが、<line-break>強きソウルと共にあれば<line-break>必ず道は開けますからね。<line-break>あなたには<line-break>無限の可能性があるのだから。<end>"
EN: "We cannot know what will happen, but with a powerful Soul beside you, a path will surely open. Your possibilities are limitless.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이 일어날진 모르지만 강한 소울과 함께라면 반드시 길은 열립니다. 당신에게는 무한한 가능성이 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1890103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、それと。<line-break>私もお手伝いしますよ。<line-break>必要なら言ってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, one more thing. I can help as well. Just ask if you need me.<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고요. 저도 도와드릴게요. 필요하면 말씀해 주세요.<end>"

## 1890104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントから撤退する。<line-break>すぐに兵を集結させ、<line-break>退却の準備に入れ。<end>"
EN: "We are withdrawing from Ancient. Rally the troops at once and prepare to retreat.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트에서 철수한다. 즉시 병력을 집결시키고 퇴각 준비에 들어가라.<end>"

## 1890105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1890106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンのカルラ軍へ伝令を走らせろ。<line-break>エンシャントは陥落した。<line-break>帝都からの撤退を開始するとな。<end>"
EN: "Send a messenger to Karula's Army in Rocen. Tell them Ancient has fallen and we are beginning our withdrawal from the imperial capital.<end>"
KO: "로센의 카를라군에 전령을 보내라. 엔샨트는 함락됐다. 제도에서 철수를 시작한다고 전해.<end>"

## 1890107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1890108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァ様！<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva!<end>"
KO: "벨제바 님!<end>"

## 1890109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "市内の状況はどうなっている。<end>"
EN: "What is the situation in the city?<end>"
KO: "시내 상황은 어떻게 되어 있지.<end>"

## 1890110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "完全に制圧されました！<line-break>中心地から西側にかけては、　　　　<line-break>壊滅状態です。<line-break>魔物が至る所を徘徊し、<line-break>城へたどり着くことすらできません。<end>"
EN: "It has been completely overrun! Everything from the city center westward has been devastated. Monsters are roaming everywhere, and we cannot even reach the castle.<end>"
KO: "완전히 제압당했습니다! 중심지에서 서쪽에 걸쳐 괴멸 상태입니다. 마물이 곳곳을 배회해 성에 도착하는 것조차 불가능합니다.<end>"

## 1890111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　あのシャリという奴、　　<line-break>いったい何者なのだ！<line-break>　<line-break>あれだけの数の魔物を<line-break>一度に召喚するとは！<line-break>しかも、邪竜まで襲来するとは！！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! Just who is this Shalli? To summon so many monsters at once, and then have an Evil Dragon attack as well!<end>"
KO: "큭! 그 샤리라는 녀석, 대체 정체가 뭐야! 저 많은 마물을 한꺼번에 소환하다니! 게다가 사룡까지 습격해 오다니!!<end>"

## 1890112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "認めたくありませんが、<line-break>我々では…、我々では<line-break>まったく相手になりません…！<end>"
EN: "I hate to admit it, but we... we are utterly outmatched!<end>"
KO: "인정하고 싶지 않지만 저희로는… 저희로는 전혀 상대가 되지 않습니다…!<end>"

## 1890113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの時と同じだ…。<line-break>必要なのは軍隊ではなく、　　　　　<line-break>勇者だということか。<line-break>魔王バロルを倒せたのは、<line-break>ネメア様とレーグ、オルファウス、<line-break>ケリュネイアという勇者たちだった。<end>"
EN: "It is just like before... So what we need is not an army, but a hero. The heroes who defeated Balor, the Demon King, were Lord Nemea, Reig, Orphaus, and Ceryneia.<end>"
KO: "그때와 똑같군…. 필요한 건 군대가 아니라 용사라는 건가. 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 것도 네메아 님과 레그, 오르파우스, 케류네이아라는 용사들이었어.<end>"

## 1890114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ネメア様のいない今、<line-break>いったい誰が<line-break>その役割を成しえるのか？<end>"
EN: "But with Lord Nemea gone, who can possibly fulfill that role?<end>"
KO: "하지만 네메아 님이 없는 지금, 대체 누가 그 역할을 해낼 수 있겠는가?<end>"

## 1890115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가!<end>"

## 1890116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いただろう？<line-break>あのエンシャントが、<line-break>瞬時にしてこの様だ。　<line-break>中はもっとひどい有様だ。<line-break>すでに死の都だよ。<end>"
EN: "Hard to believe, isn't it? Ancient was reduced to this in an instant. It is even worse inside. The city is already dead.<end>"
KO: "놀랐지? 그 엔샨트가 순식간에 이 꼴이 됐어. 안은 더 심해. 이미 죽음의 도시야.<end>"

## 1890117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院の長エルファスと　　　　　　　<line-break>シャリという魔道士、<line-break>そして妖術士ゾフォル。<line-break>この３人を相手に、ディンガル軍は<line-break>エンシャントを守りきれなかったのだ。<end>"
EN: "Elfas, head of the Inscription Order; the mage Shalli; and the sorcerer Zofor. Against those three, the Dyneskal Army could not hold Ancient.<end>"
KO: "시문원의 장 엘파스와 샤리라는 마도사, 그리고 요술사 조포르. 이 세 명을 상대로 딘갈군은 엔샨트를 지켜내지 못했다.<end>"

## 1890118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと…？<line-break>まさか、行くつもりか？<line-break>奴らと戦うつもりなのか！？<end>"
EN: "What...? You're not actually going, are you? You mean to fight them?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고…? 설마 갈 생각인가? 녀석들과 싸울 생각이냐!?<end>"

## 1890119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無謀だな、君は。<line-break>ネメア様から話を聞いてはいたが…。<line-break>　<line-break>そうか…。<line-break>なるほど、お前が…。<end>"
EN: "Reckless, aren't you? Lord Nemea told me about you, but... I see. So you're...<end>"
KO: "무모하군, 너는. 네메아 님께 이야기는 들었지만…. 그런가…. 과연, 네가….<end>"

## 1890120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どのみち軍隊では勝ち目がない。<line-break>我々は魔物をエンシャントから<line-break>出さないように防衛に徹する。<line-break>未来は、君に託そう。<end>"
EN: "An army has no hope of winning in any case. We will hold the line and keep the monsters from leaving Ancient. The future is in your hands.<end>"
KO: "어차피 군대로는 승산이 없어. 우리는 마물이 엔샨트에서 나오지 못하도록 방어에 전념한다. 미래는 너에게 맡기지.<end>"

## 1890121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傷付いたら戻るがいい。<line-break>回復してやろう。<end>"
EN: "Come back if you are wounded. I will heal you.<end>"
KO: "다치면 돌아오거라. 회복시켜 주지.<end>"

## 1890122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お気をつけください。<line-break>中は、もはや魔物の巣窟と<line-break>化しています。<end>"
EN: "Please be careful. Inside, it has become a den of monsters.<end>"
KO: "조심하십시오. 안은 이미 마물의 소굴이 되어 있습니다.<end>"

## 1890123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ様の姿が見えないのです。<line-break>あの方が、やられるとも<line-break>思えないのですが…。<end>"
EN: "There has been no sign of Lady Zagiv. I cannot imagine that she was defeated, but...<end>"
KO: "자기브 님이 보이지 않습니다. 그분이 당했을 거라고는 생각하기 어렵습니다만….<end>"

## 1890124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（もう、あと戻りはできない！）<end>"
EN: "(There's no turning back now!)<end>"
KO: "(이제 돌아갈 수 없어!)<end>"

## 1890125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様に代わる勇者か…。<line-break>人の革新は、引き継がれていく<line-break>ものなのかもしれぬな…。<end>"
EN: "A hero to take Lord Nemea's place... Perhaps Humanity's Evolution can be passed on.<end>"
KO: "네메아 님을 대신할 용사인가…. 인간의 혁신은 이어져 가는 것일지도 모르겠군….<end>"

## 1890126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい、<value:$28>。<line-break>ついに来たね。フフフ。　　　　<line-break>待ってたんだ。<line-break>これを見せてあげたくてね。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. You finally made it. Heh heh heh. I've been waiting. I wanted you to see this.<end>"
KO: "어서 와, <value:$28>. 드디어 왔구나. 후후후. 기다리고 있었어. 이걸 보여 주고 싶어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1890127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いてる？<line-break>倒したはずなのにね？<line-break>ははっ、僕が甦らせたのさ。<line-break>君がイシュバアルを<line-break>殺しちゃったからね。<line-break>こいつがその代わりなの。<end>"
EN: "Surprised? You thought you killed it, didn't you? Ha! I brought it back to life. Since you went and killed Ishbaal, this one will take its place.<end>"
KO: "놀랐어? 분명 쓰러뜨렸는데 말이지? 하하, 내가 되살렸어. 네가 이슈바알을 죽여 버렸으니까. 이 녀석이 그 대신이야.<end>"

## 1890128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントを完全に破壊するには<line-break>邪竜の力が必要だったからね。<end>"
EN: "I needed the power of an Evil Dragon to destroy Ancient completely.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트를 완전히 파괴하려면 사룡의 힘이 필요했으니까.<end>"

## 1890129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜エルアザルだよ。<line-break>死竜の洞窟で眠っていたのを　<line-break>僕が起こしてあげたんだ。<line-break>ここにエサがあるよーってね。<line-break>あはははは。<end>"
EN: "This is Elazar, an Evil Dragon. It was sleeping in Dead Dragon Cave, so I woke it up. I told it there was food waiting here. Ahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "사룡 엘아자르야. 죽은 용의 동굴에서 자고 있던 걸 내가 깨워 줬지. 여기 먹이가 있다고 말이야. 아하하하.<end>"

## 1890130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、こいつを倒さないと<line-break>この先には進めないよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now, if you want to go any farther, you'll have to defeat this thing, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨리지 않으면 앞으로 갈 수 없어, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1890131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　誰か来た？<end>"
EN: "Huh? Did someone just arrive?<end>"
KO: "어라? 누가 왔나?<end>"

## 1890132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その戦い、手を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "I will aid you in this battle.<end>"
KO: "그 싸움, 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 1890133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　我はイシュバアル…、すなわち破滅…。　　<line-break>　　　　　　破壊神により創られ…、<line-break>　　　この世に破壊をもたらす者なり…。<end>"
EN: "I am Ishbaal... destruction itself... Created by the God of Destruction... I am the one who brings destruction upon this world...<end>"
KO: "나는 이슈바알… 즉 파멸…. 파괴신에 의해 만들어져… 이 세상에 파괴를 가져오는 자….<end>"

## 1890134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　うぬか…、我が破壊を望まず、　　　　<line-break>　　　　立ちはだからんとする者は。<end>"
EN: "So it is you... the one who does not desire the destruction I bring and would stand in my way.<end>"
KO: "네놈인가… 나의 파괴를 바라지 않고 가로막으려는 자는.<end>"

## 1890135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はこの恐怖の存在を<line-break>目の前にしても、<line-break>まだ立ち向かうの？<end>"
EN: "Even with this terrifying being before you, you still mean to fight?<end>"
KO: "이 공포의 존재를 눈앞에 두고도 아직 맞설 생각이야?<end>"

## 1890136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、さすがだよ。<line-break>それでこそ、だよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I expected no less. That's more like it.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 역시 대단해. 그래야지.<end>"

## 1890137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、こいつを倒さないと<line-break>この先には進めないよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>! You'll have to defeat this thing before you can go any farther!<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨리지 않으면 앞으로 갈 수 없어, <value:$28>!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　誰か来た？<end>"
EN: "Huh? Did someone just arrive?<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2ネメア様！<line-break>生きていらっしゃったんですね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3おお！　ネメア様！<line-break>ご無事で何より！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4ネメアだわ。<line-break>このしぶとさ、見習わなくちゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5おおっ！　ネメアだ！<line-break>くー、おいしいところで<line-break>帰ってきたなぁ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6さすが！<line-break>やっぱり戻ってきたよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7ネメア様！<line-break>ご無事でしたか！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>8おかえりなさい、ネメア。<line-break>よくがんばりましたね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9さっすが、うちらの大将よねー。<line-break>やっぱ、無事だったんだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12まったく、さすがだな。<line-break>生きて帰ってくるとは。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールはこんな奴を<line-break>相手にしてたのか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13無事だったか。<line-break>ネメア、さすがに大したものだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10うお！　ネメアだ！<line-break>姉ちゃん、やっぱネメアはすげぇよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14さすが、ネメア。<line-break>やはり戻ってきたか。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15よかった無事で…。<line-break>おかえりなさい、ネメアさん。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17あら、ちゃんと帰ってきたのね。<line-break>下等生物にしては、上できだわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>19わーい、戻ってきたー。<line-break>ご理解ください、<value:$28>様。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20遅かったな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21帰ってきたというのか…<line-break>さすが…というべきかな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23ネメア様！<line-break>ご無事で何よりです<end><if><value:$01><equal>27生きて帰ってくるとは…<line-break>さすがだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26さすがネメア殿じゃな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24生きていらっしゃったのですね<line-break>ネメア様<end><if><value:$01><equal>25生きて帰っていらっしゃるなんて<line-break>さすがネメア様です<end>さすがネメアだ。<line-break>まだまだネメアの詩は<line-break>続きそうだなぁ。 <end>"

## 1890141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまない。<line-break>手間取った。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me. I was delayed.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 시간이 좀 걸렸다.<end>"

## 1890142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったね。<line-break>帰ってきたんだ。<line-break>闇の世界から。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'll be. He came back―from the world of darkness.<end>"
KO: "곤란하군. 돌아왔어. 어둠의 세계에서.<end>"

## 1890143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の世界で私は知った。<line-break>光も闇も、何もかもを無に帰する虚無。<line-break>その存在を。<line-break>虚無の子シャリよ。<line-break>お前は何を望んでいる？<line-break>すべてを無に帰することか？<end>"
EN: "In the world of darkness, I learned that the Void exists―the Void that returns light, darkness, and all things to nothingness. Shalli, Child of the Void, what do you desire? To return everything to nothingness?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 세계에서 나는 알았다. 빛도 어둠도, 모든 것을 무로 되돌리는 허무. 그 존재를. 허무의 아이 샤리여. 너는 무엇을 바라는가? 모든 것을 무로 되돌리는 것인가?<end>"

## 1890144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、ネメア。<line-break>僕は虚無から生まれたけど、<line-break>僕に命を吹き込んだのは別のものさ。<end>"
EN: "No, Nemea. I was born from the Void, but something else breathed life into me.<end>"
KO: "아니야, 네메아. 난 허무에서 태어났지만 내게 생명을 불어넣은 건 다른 것이었어.<end>"

## 1890145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、その存在自体が<line-break>謎めいていた。あるいは、<line-break>敵ではないのかもしれぬ。<line-break>だが、お前の思いどおりにはさせん。<line-break>この大地は、ここに生まれ、<line-break>ここで育ち、死にゆく、我らのものだ。<end>"
EN: "Your very existence was shrouded in mystery. Perhaps you are not our enemy after all. But I will not let you have your way. This land belongs to us―to those born here, who grow here and die here.<end>"
KO: "너는 존재 자체가 수수께끼였다. 어쩌면 적이 아닐지도 모르지. 하지만 네 뜻대로 하게 둘 수는 없다. 이 대지는 여기서 태어나 자라고 죽어 가는 우리들의 것이다.<end>"

## 1890146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、ネメア。<line-break>もう思いどおりになってるんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>僕はあと少しで、<line-break>この時代での役割を終える。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, Nemea. Things are already going exactly as I intend. Soon, my part in this age will be over.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 네메아. 이미 내 뜻대로 되고 있어. 나는 이제 조금만 있으면 이 시대에서의 역할을 끝낸다.<end>"

## 1890147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<line-break>またあとで会おうね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Bye for now. I'll see you again later, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. 나중에 또 만나자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、もはや時間はない。<line-break>今、このエンシャントに<line-break>強い闇の気配が集結している。<line-break>それを絶たねばなるまい。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, there is no time left. A powerful darkness is gathering here in Ancient. We must put an end to it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이제 시간이 없다. 지금 이 엔샨트에 강한 어둠의 기운이 모이고 있다. 그것을 끊어야 한다.<end>"

## 1890149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行こう。<line-break>すべてを終わらせに。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go and put an end to everything.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자. 모든 걸 끝내러.<end>"

## 1890150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士のひとり、<line-break>縛られしものサムスンだな。<end>"
EN: "That is Samson, the Bound One―one of the Knights of the Round Table serving Ulugh, God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 하나, 속박된 자 삼손이군.<end>"

## 1890151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやらこいつを甦らせた奴が、<line-break>この先にいるようだ。<line-break>　<line-break>まずはこいつを片付ける。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It seems whoever revived this creature is farther ahead. First, we deal with this one. Come, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 이 녀석을 되살린 자가 이 앞에 있는 모양이다. 우선 이 녀석부터 처리한다. 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1890152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またも円卓騎士のひとりか。<line-break>嘲笑うものザハクと言ったか？　<line-break>　<line-break>よほどの妖術士でなければ<line-break>このような大物は召喚できまい。<line-break>この先にいるのは、ゾフォルか？<end>"
EN: "Another Knight of the Round Table. Zahak, the Mocking One, was it? Only a sorcerer of tremendous power could summon a creature of this magnitude. Is Zofor waiting ahead?<end>"
KO: "또 원탁기사 중 하나인가. 비웃는 자 자하크라고 했나? 상당한 요술사가 아니라면 이런 거물을 소환하지 못할 거다. 이 앞에 있는 건 조포르인가?<end>"

## 1890153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1890154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec189-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ケリュネイア。<line-break>どこまでついてくるつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia... How far do you intend to follow me?<end>"
KO: "…케류네이아. 어디까지 따라올 생각이지?<end>"
